\displaystyle\overset{(\mathrm{iv})}{=}\gamma\left(V^{\star}(s_{t+1})-V^{\pi_{t+1}}(s_{t+1})\right)+Q_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})-Q^{\star}(s_{t},a_{t})+\zeta_{t}

\displaystyle\overset{(\mathrm{v})}{\leq}\frac{\gamma(3-\gamma)}{2}\left(V^{\star}(s_{t+1})-V^{\pi_{t+1}}(s_{t+1})\right)+Q_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})-Q^{\star}(s_{t},a_{t})+\zeta_{t}.

(56)

In the series of equalities above, (i) is because
\pi_{t}(s_{t})=a_{t}
in Algorithm
1
. (ii) is by the Bellman equation (
1
) and Bellman optimality equation (
2.1
). (iii) is because by the definition in (
13
),
P_{t}(V^{\star}-V^{\pi_{t}})=V^{\star}(s_{t+1})-V^{\pi_{t}}(s_{t+1})
. In (iv), we group a few component terms into a newly-defined term

\zeta_{t}:=V_{t-1}(s_{t})-Q_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})+\gamma\left(P_{s_{t},a_{t}}-P_{t}\right)\left(V^{\star}-V^{\pi_{t}}\right)+\gamma\left(V^{\pi_{t+1}}(s_{t+1})-V^{\pi_{t}}(s_{t+1})\right).

(57)

Lastly, (v) is due to
\gamma\leq\frac{\gamma(3-\gamma)}{2}
.

The regret can be further manipulated with an upper bound on
Q_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})-Q^{\star}(s_{t},a_{t})
via the application of Lemma
4
. Such manipulation leads to a recursion on
V_{t-1}(s_{t})-V^{\pi_{t}}(s_{t})
. Rearranging (
56
), we obtain a recursion as follows:

\displaystyle\quad\left(1-\frac{\gamma(3-\gamma)}{2}\right)\sum_{t=1}^{T}\big(V_{t-1}(s_{t})-V^{\pi_{t}}(s_{t})\big)

\displaystyle=\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(V_{t-1}(s_{t})-V^{\pi_{t}}(s_{t})\right)-\frac{\gamma(3-\gamma)}{2}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(V_{t-1}(s_{t})-V^{\pi_{t}}(s_{t})\right)

\displaystyle\overset{(\mathrm{i})}{\leq}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(V_{t-1}(s_{t})-V^{\pi_{t}}(s_{t})\right)-\frac{\gamma(3-\gamma)}{2}\sum_{t=2}^{T}\left(V_{t-1}(s_{t})-V^{\pi_{t}}(s_{t})\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(V_{t-1}(s_{t})-V^{\pi_{t}}(s_{t})\right)-\frac{\gamma(3-\gamma)}{2}\sum_{t=1}^{T-1}\left(V_{t}(s_{t+1})-V^{\pi_{t+1}}(s_{t+1})\right)

\displaystyle\overset{(\mathrm{ii})}{\leq}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\bigg(\frac{\gamma(3-\gamma)}{2}\left(V^{\star}(s_{t+1})-V^{\pi_{t+1}}(s_{t+1})\right)+Q_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})-Q^{\star}(s_{t},a_{t})+\zeta_{t}\bigg)-\frac{\gamma(3-\gamma)}{2}\sum_{t=1}^{T-1}\left(V_{t}(s_{t+1})-V^{\pi_{t+1}}(s_{t+1})\right)

\displaystyle\overset{(\mathrm{iii})}{\leq}C_{1}\left(\sqrt{\frac{SAT}{1-\gamma}\log^{3}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}+\frac{SA}{(1-\gamma)^{7}}\log^{7/2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}+\sqrt{\frac{SA\log^{2}T}{(1-\gamma)^{5}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\big(V_{t-1}(s_{t})-V^{\pi_{t}}(s_{t})\big)}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\bigg(\frac{\gamma(3-\gamma)}{2}\left(V_{t}(s_{t+1})-V^{\pi_{t+1}}(s_{t+1})\right)+\zeta_{t}\bigg)-\frac{\gamma(3-\gamma)}{2}\sum_{t=1}^{T-1}\left(V_{t}(s_{t+1})-V^{\pi_{t+1}}(s_{t+1})\right)

\displaystyle=C_{1}\left(\sqrt{\frac{SAT}{1-\gamma}\log^{3}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}+\frac{SA}{(1-\gamma)^{7}}\log^{7/2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}+\sqrt{\frac{SA\log^{2}T}{(1-\gamma)^{5}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\big(V_{t-1}(s_{t})-V^{\pi_{t}}(s_{t})\big)}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{\gamma(3-\gamma)}{2}\left(V^{\star}(s_{T+1})-V^{\pi_{T+1}}(s_{T+1})\right)+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\zeta_{t}

\displaystyle\overset{(\mathrm{iv})}{\leq}C_{1}\left(\sqrt{\frac{SAT}{1-\gamma}\log^{3}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}+\frac{SA}{(1-\gamma)^{7}}\log^{7/2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}+\sqrt{\frac{SA\log^{2}T}{(1-\gamma)^{5}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\big(V_{t-1}(s_{t})-V^{\pi_{t}}(s_{t})}\big)\right)

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{3}{2(1-\gamma)}+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\zeta_{t}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{SAT}{1-\gamma}\log^{3}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}+\frac{SA}{(1-\gamma)^{7}}\log^{7/2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}+\sqrt{\frac{SA\log^{2}T}{(1-\gamma)^{5}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\big(V_{t-1}(s_{t})-V^{\pi_{t}}(s_{t})\big)}+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\zeta_{t}.

(58)

Above, (i) is because by the optimistic property in Lemma
4
,
V_{t-1}(s_{t})\geq V^{\star}(s_{t})\geq V^{\pi_{t}}(s_{t})\geq 0
and thus
V_{t-1}(s_{t})-V^{\pi_{t}}(s_{t})\geq 0
. (ii) is obtained by replacing
\left(V_{t-1}(s_{t})-V^{\pi_{t}}(s_{t})\right)
with (
56
). (iii) is obtained by invoking Lemma
4
to replace
\sum_{t=1}^{T}\big(Q_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})-Q^{\star}(s_{t},a_{t})\big)
. (iv) is due to
V^{\star}(s_{T+1})-V^{\pi_{T+1}}(s_{T+1})\leq\frac{1}{1-\gamma}
and
\gamma\in(0,1)
.

Note
0<1-\frac{\gamma(3-\gamma)}{2}<1
since
\gamma\in(0,1)
. Dividing (
58
) by
1-\frac{\gamma(3-\gamma)}{2}
, we have

\displaystyle\quad\ \sum_{t=1}^{T}\big(V_{t-1}(s_{t})-V^{\pi_{t}}(s_{t})\big)

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{SAT\log^{3}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{3}}}+\frac{SA\log^{7/2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{8}}+\sqrt{\frac{SA\log^{2}T}{(1-\gamma)^{7}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\big(V_{t-1}(s_{t})-V^{\pi_{t}}(s_{t})\big)}+\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\zeta_{t},

(59)

in which the additional
\frac{1}{1-\gamma}
factor is due to the fact
1-\frac{\gamma(3-\gamma)}{2}\geq\frac{1-\gamma}{2}
.

We continue by invoking Lemma
5
on (
59
), which allows us to write (
59
) as

\displaystyle\quad\ \sum_{t=1}^{T}\big(V_{t-1}(s_{t})-V^{\pi_{t}}(s_{t})\big)

\displaystyle\leq C_{3}\Bigg(\sqrt{\frac{SAT\log^{3}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{3}}}+\frac{SA\log^{7/2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{8}}+\sqrt{\frac{SA\log^{2}T}{(1-\gamma)^{7}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(V_{t-1}(s_{t})-V^{\pi_{t}}(s_{t})\right)}

\displaystyle\qquad+\sqrt{\frac{SA\log\frac{T}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{3}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\big(V^{\star}(s_{t})-V^{\pi_{t}}(s_{t})\big)}\Bigg)

(60)

for some absolute constant
C_{3}>0
.

Finally, some algebraic manipulation on (
60
) can lead to the desired result in Theorem
1
. In particular, we can use Proposition
1
, which is an algebraic fact about quadratic inequalities like (
60
). The proof of Proposition
1
is provided in Appendix
C.3
.

Specifically, we can invoke Proposition
1
on (
60
) with

\displaystyle x

\displaystyle:=C_{3}\log T\sqrt{\frac{SA}{(1-\gamma)^{7}}},

\displaystyle y

\displaystyle:=C_{3}\Bigg(\sqrt{\frac{SAT\log^{3}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{3}}}+\frac{SA\log^{7/2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{8}}+\sqrt{\frac{SA\log\frac{T}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{3}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\big(V^{\star}(s_{t})-V^{\pi_{t}}(s_{t})\big)}\Bigg),

\displaystyle z

\displaystyle:=\sum_{t=1}^{T}\big(V_{t-1}(s_{t})-V^{\pi_{t}}(s_{t})\big)

in (
35
). (
36
) in Proposition
1
would give

\displaystyle\quad\ \sum_{t=1}^{T}\big(V^{\star}(s_{t})-V^{\pi_{t}}(s_{t})\big)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{t=1}^{T}\big(V_{t-1}(s_{t})-V^{\pi_{t}}(s_{t})\big)

\displaystyle\leq 2C_{3}\sqrt{\frac{SAT\log^{3}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{3}}}+(2C_{3}+C_{3}^{2})\frac{SA\log^{7/2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{8}}+2C_{3}\sqrt{\frac{SA\log\frac{T}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{3}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\big(V^{\star}(s_{t})-V^{\pi_{t}}(s_{t})\big)}\Bigg).

(61)

We apply Proposition
1
once again on (
61
) by letting

\displaystyle x

\displaystyle:=2C_{3}\sqrt{\frac{SA\log\frac{T}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{3}}},

\displaystyle y

\displaystyle:=2C_{3}\sqrt{\frac{SAT\log^{3}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{3}}}+(2C_{3}+C_{3}^{2})\frac{SA\log^{7/2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{8}},

\displaystyle z

\displaystyle:=\sum_{t=1}^{T}\big(V^{\star}(s_{t})-V^{\pi_{t}}(s_{t})\big)

in (
35
). (
36
) would give

\mathrm{Regret}(T)=\sum_{t=1}^{T}\big(V^{\star}(s_{t})-V^{\pi_{t}}(s_{t})\big)\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{SAT\log^{3}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{3}}}+\frac{SA\log^{7/2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{8}}

(62)

with probability at least
1-\delta
, which is as claimed in Theorem
1
.

Appendix E
Proof of Lemma
1

We can prove this lemma by induction.

For the base case, it is clear that
Q_{1}(s,a)=\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\geq Q^{\star}(s,a)
for all
(s,a)\in{\mathcal{S}}\times\mathcal{A}
. For the inductive step, assuming the inductive hypothesis
Q_{t}(s,a)\geq Q^{\star}(s,a)
all the way up to
t
for all
(s,a)\in{\mathcal{S}}\times\mathcal{A}
, we need to prove
Q_{t+1}(s,a)\geq Q^{\star}(s,a)
. To this end, given any
t\in[T]
and the inductive hypothesis at the
t
-th step, it suffices to prove for all
(s,a)\in{\mathcal{S}}\times\mathcal{A}
,

\min\{Q^{\mathrm{UCB}}_{t+1}(s,a),Q^{\mathrm{R}}_{t+1}(s,a)\}\geq Q^{\star}(s,a).

(63)

To prove (
63
), we need to examine the two arguments of the
\min
function separately and prove

Q^{\mathrm{UCB}}_{t+1}(s,a)\geq Q^{\star}(s,a).

(64)

and

Q^{\mathrm{R}}_{t+1}(s,a)\geq Q^{\star}(s,a).

(65)

respectively. In the proof of (
64
), we will use the abbreviation
N:=N_{t}(s,a)
for notational simplicity. In this notation, we can see that
(s,a):=(s_{t_{N}},a_{t_{N}})
.

\bullet
Proof of (
64
): First, let us prove the optimism of
Q^{\mathrm{UCB}}
in (
64
).

Let
b_{n}:=c_{b}\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{3}n}}
. We rewrite the subroutine
update-q-ucb
() in Line
2
of Algorithm
2
:

Q^{\mathrm{UCB}}_{t+1}(s,a)=Q^{\mathrm{UCB}}_{t_{N}+1}(s,a)=(1-\eta_{N})Q^{\mathrm{UCB}}_{t_{N}}(s,a)+\eta_{N}\left(r(s,a)+\gamma V_{t_{N}}(s_{t_{N}+1})+b_{N}\right).

This recursive relation can be expanded as

Q^{\mathrm{UCB}}_{t+1}(s,a)=\eta_{0}^{N}Q^{\mathrm{UCB}}_{1}(s,a)+\sum_{n=1}^{N}\eta_{n}^{N}\big(r(s,a)+\gamma V_{t_{n}}(s_{t_{n}+1})+b_{n}\big),

(66)

in which
\eta_{0}^{N}
and
\eta_{n}^{N}
are defined in Lemma
6
.

Using (
66
), we have

\displaystyle\quad Q^{\mathrm{UCB}}_{t+1}(s,a)-Q^{\star}(s,a)

\displaystyle=\eta_{0}^{N}\left(Q^{\mathrm{UCB}}_{1}(s,a)-Q^{\star}(s,a)\right)+\sum_{n=1}^{N}\eta_{n}^{N}\big(r(s,a)+\gamma V_{t_{n}}(s_{t_{n}+1})+b_{n}-Q^{\star}(s,a)\big)

\displaystyle=\eta_{0}^{N}\left(Q^{\mathrm{UCB}}_{1}(s,a)-Q^{\star}(s,a)\right)+\sum_{n=1}^{N}\eta_{n}^{N}\Big(\gamma\big(V_{t_{n}}(s_{t_{n}+1})-V^{\star}(s_{t_{n}+1})\big)+\gamma\left(P_{t_{n}}-P_{s,a}\right)V^{\star}+b_{n}\Big),

(67)

where the last step is by
Q^{\star}(s,a)=r(s,a)+\gamma P_{s,a}V^{\star}
and
V^{\star}(s_{t_{n}+1})=P_{t_{n}}V^{\star}
.

We can invoke Lemma 4.3 from
Jin et al., 2018
, which relies on Azuma-Hoeffding inequality, with only notational modification and obtain with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\left|\sum_{n=1}^{N}\eta_{n}^{N}\gamma\left(P_{t_{n}}-P_{s,a}\right)V^{\star}\right|\leq c_{b}\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{3}N}}\sum_{n=1}^{N}\eta_{n}^{N}\leq\sum_{n=1}^{N}\eta_{n}^{N}b_{n},

(68)

for all
(N,s,a)\in[T]\times{\mathcal{S}}\times\mathcal{A}
. This allows us to conclude that with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle\quad Q^{\mathrm{UCB}}_{t+1}(s,a)-Q^{\star}(s,a)

\displaystyle\geq\eta_{0}^{N}\left(Q^{\mathrm{UCB}}_{1}(s,a)-Q^{\star}(s,a)\right)+\sum_{n=1}^{N}\eta_{n}^{N}\Big(\gamma\big(V_{t_{n}}(s_{t_{n}+1})-V^{\star}(s_{t_{n}+1})\big)+\gamma\left(P_{t_{n}}-P_{s,a}\right)V^{\star}+b_{n}\Big)

\displaystyle\geq\eta_{0}^{N}\left(Q^{\mathrm{UCB}}_{1}(s,a)-Q^{\star}(s,a)\right)+\sum_{n=1}^{N}\eta_{n}^{N}\gamma\big(V_{t_{n}}(s_{t_{n}+1})-V^{\star}(s_{t_{n}+1})\big)

\displaystyle\geq\sum_{n=1}^{N}\eta_{n}^{N}\gamma\big(V_{t_{n}}(s_{t_{n}+1})-V^{\star}(s_{t_{n}+1})\big),

where the last step is because
Q^{\mathrm{UCB}}_{1}(s,a)=\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\geq Q^{\star}(s,a)
.

Therefore, we can prove (
64
)

Q^{\mathrm{UCB}}_{t+1}(s,a)-Q^{\star}(s,a)\geq\sum_{n=1}^{N}\eta_{n}^{N}\gamma\big(V_{t_{n}}(s_{t_{n}+1})-V^{\star}(s_{t_{n}+1})\big)\geq 0,

where the last inequality is a result of the inductive hypothesis:
V_{t}(s)\geq Q_{t}(s,\pi^{\star}(s))\geq Q^{\star}(s,\pi^{\star}(s))=V^{\star}(s)
, for all
(t,s,a)\in[T]\times{\mathcal{S}}\times\mathcal{A}
.

\bullet
Proof of (
65
): Next, let us prove the optimism of
Q^{\mathrm{R}}
in (
65
). Since only
Q_{t+1}^{\mathrm{R}}(s_{t},a_{t})
is updated at the
t
-th step with all other entries of
Q_{t}^{\mathrm{R}}
fixed, it suffices to check

Q^{\mathrm{R}}_{t+1}(s_{t},a_{t})\geq Q^{\star}(s_{t},a_{t}).

(69)

In the proof of (
65
), we will use the abbreviation
N_{t}:=N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})
and
t_{n}:=t_{n}(s_{t},a_{t})
for notational simplicity.

Step 1: decomposing
Q^{\mathrm{R}}_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})-Q^{\star}(s_{t},a_{t})
.

According to the update rule of
Q_{t}^{\mathrm{R}}
in Line
2
of Algorithm
2
, we have

\displaystyle\quad\ Q_{t+1}^{\mathrm{R}}(s_{t},a_{t})

\displaystyle=Q^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{N_{t}}+1}(s_{t},a_{t})

\displaystyle=(1-\eta_{N_{t}})Q^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{N_{t}}}(s_{t},a_{t})+\eta_{N_{t}}\left\{r(s_{t},a_{t})+\gamma\left(V_{t_{N_{t}}}(s_{t_{N_{t}}+1})-V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{N_{t}}}(s_{t_{N_{t}}+1})+\mu^{\mathrm{ref}}_{t_{N_{t}}+1}(s_{t},a_{t})\right)+b^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{N_{t}}+1}\right\}

\displaystyle=(1-\eta_{N_{t}})Q^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{N_{t}-1}+1}(s_{t},a_{t})+\eta_{N_{t}}\left\{r(s_{t},a_{t})+\gamma\left(V_{t_{N_{t}}}(s_{t_{N_{t}}+1})-V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{N_{t}}}(s_{t_{N_{t}}+1})+\mu^{\mathrm{ref}}_{t_{N_{t}}+1}(s_{t},a_{t})\right)+b^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{N_{t}}+1}\right\},

in which the first and last step follow from the fact
t_{N}=t_{N_{t}}=t
. Expanding this recursively, we have

\displaystyle Q_{t+1}^{\mathrm{R}}(s_{t},a_{t})

\displaystyle=\eta_{0}^{N_{t}}Q_{1}^{\mathrm{R}}(s_{t},a_{t})

\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t}}\eta_{n}^{N_{t}}\left\{r(s_{t},a_{t})+\gamma\left(V_{t_{n}}(s_{t_{n}+1})-V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}}(s_{t_{n}+1})+\mu^{\mathrm{ref}}_{t_{n}+1}(s_{t},a_{t})\right)+b^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}+1}\right\}.

(70)

On the other hand, since
\eta_{0}^{N_{t}}+\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t}}\eta_{n}^{N_{t}}=1
by (
30
), we can write
Q^{\star}(s_{t},a_{t})
as a sum weighted by
\{\eta^{N_{t}}_{n}\}_{n=0}^{N_{t}}
. This leads to

\displaystyle Q_{t+1}^{\mathrm{R}}(s_{t},a_{t})-Q^{\star}(s_{t},a_{t})=\eta_{0}^{N_{t}}\left(Q_{1}^{\mathrm{R}}(s_{t},a_{t})-Q^{\star}(s_{t},a_{t})\right)

\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t}}\eta_{n}^{N_{t}}\left\{r(s_{t},a_{t})+\gamma\left(V_{t_{n}}(s_{t_{n}+1})-V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}}(s_{t_{n}+1})+\mu^{\mathrm{ref}}_{t_{n}+1}(s_{t},a_{t})\right)+b^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}+1}-Q^{\star}(s_{t},a_{t})\right\}.

(71)

Moreover, to further expand (
E
), we can invoke the definition of
\mu^{\mathrm{ref}}
(which is a moving average of
V^{\mathrm{R}}
) and expand
Q^{\star}(s_{t},a_{t})
using the Bellman optimality equation (cf. (
2.1
)), which gives:

\displaystyle\quad\ r(s_{t},a_{t})+\gamma\left(V_{t_{n}}(s_{t_{n}+1})-V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}}(s_{t_{n}+1})+\mu^{\mathrm{ref}}_{t_{n}+1}(s_{t},a_{t})\right)+b^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}+1}-Q^{\star}(s_{t},a_{t})

\displaystyle=\gamma\left(V_{t_{n}}(s_{t_{n}+1})-V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}}(s_{t_{n}+1})+\frac{\sum_{i=1}^{n}V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{i}}(s_{t_{i}+1})}{n}-P_{s_{t},a_{t}}V^{\star}\right)+b^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}+1}

(72)

\displaystyle=\gamma\left(P_{t_{n}}\left\{V_{t_{n}}-V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}}\right\}+\frac{\sum_{i=1}^{n}P_{t_{n}}V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{i}}}{n}-P_{s_{t},a_{t}}V^{\star}\right)+b^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}+1}

\displaystyle=\gamma\left(P_{s_{t},a_{t}}\left\{V_{t_{n}}-V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}}\right\}+\frac{\sum_{i=1}^{n}P_{s_{t},a_{t}}V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{i}}}{n}-P_{s_{t},a_{t}}V^{\star}\right)+b^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}+1}+\xi_{t_{n}}

\displaystyle=\gamma P_{s_{t},a_{t}}\left(V_{t_{n}}-V^{\star}+\frac{\sum_{i=1}^{n}V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{i}}-V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}}}{n}\right)+b^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}+1}+\xi_{t_{n}},

(73)

in which we group the higher-order terms into a newly-defined term

\xi_{t_{n}}:=\gamma\big(P_{t_{n}}-P_{s_{t},a_{t}}\big)\big(V_{t_{n}}-V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}}\big)+\frac{\gamma}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\big(P_{t_{n}}-P_{s_{t},a_{t}}\big)V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{i}}.

(74)

Combining (
E
) and (
73
) leads to the following decomposition

\displaystyle\quad\ Q^{\mathrm{R}}_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})-Q^{\star}(s_{t},a_{t})

\displaystyle=\eta^{N_{t}}_{0}\left(Q^{\mathrm{R}}_{1}(s_{t},a_{t})-Q^{\star}(s_{t},a_{t})\right)+\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t}}\eta^{N_{t}}_{n}\left\{\gamma P_{s_{t},a_{t}}\left(V_{t_{n}}-V^{\star}+\frac{\sum_{i=1}^{n}V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{i}}-V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}}}{n}\right)+b^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}+1}+\xi_{t_{n}}\right\}.

(75)

Finally, we can notice that several terms in (
75
) can be dropped:

(1)

Our initialization satisfies
Q^{\mathrm{R}}_{1}(s,a)-Q^{\star}(s,a)\geq 0
for all
(s,a)\in{\mathcal{S}}\times\mathcal{A}
.

(2)

The inductive hypothesis implies that for any
n
such that
1\leq t_{n}\leq t
, one has
V_{t_{n}}\geq V^{\star}
.

(3)

For all
0\leq i\leq n
and any
s\in{\mathcal{S}}
, one has

V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{i}}(s)-V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}}(s)\geq 0,

(76)

because
V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t}(s)
is updated with
V_{t}(s)
and
V_{t}(s)
is non-increasing by its update rule.

Thus, (
75
) can be lower-bounded as follows:

Q^{\mathrm{R}}_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})-Q^{\star}(s_{t},a_{t})\geq\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t}}\eta^{N_{t}}_{n}\left(b^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}+1}+\xi_{t_{n}}\right).

(77)

To establish the desired result of optimism, it remains to show the right-hand side of (
77
) is non-negative. In particular, it suffices for us to show

\left|\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t}}\eta^{N_{t}}_{n}\xi_{t_{n}}\right|\leq\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t}}\eta^{N_{t}}_{n}b^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}+1}.

(78)

To justify (
78
), we will examine the two quantities that compose
\xi_{t_{n}}
. Let us denote them as
I_{1}
and
I_{2}
respectively:

\displaystyle I_{1}

\displaystyle:=\gamma\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t}}\eta_{n}^{N_{t}}\left(P_{t_{n}}-P_{s_{t},a_{t}}\right)\left(V_{t_{n}}-V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}}\right),

(79a)

\displaystyle I_{2}

\displaystyle:=\gamma\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t}}\frac{1}{n}\eta_{n}^{N_{t}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(P_{t_{n}}-P_{s_{t},a_{t}}\right)V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{i}}.

(79b)

In the following, we will bound
I_{1}
and
I_{2}
with Lemma
7
, which is a result of Freedman’s inequality.

Step 2: controlling
I_{1}
.

We can invoke Lemma
7
to control the term
I_{1}
defined in (
79a
).

We begin by constructing the necessary notation and verify the conditions for Lemma
7
. Consider any
N\in[T]
. Let

\displaystyle W_{i}:=V_{i}-V^{\mathrm{R}}_{i}\qquad\text{and}\qquad u_{i}(s,a,N):=\eta_{N_{i}(s,a)}^{N}\geq 0.

(80)

Clearly,
W_{i}
is deterministic at the end of the
(i-1)
-th step. Note that
N_{i}(s,a)
is also deterministic at the end of the
(i-1)
-th step, because
s_{t}
and
Q_{Z_{t-1}}
(and thus
a_{t}
) are already deterministic at the end of the
(i-1)
-th step. Moreover, we define an upper bound
C_{\mathrm{w}}
as follows:

\displaystyle\left\lVert W_{i}\right\rVert_{\infty}\leq\left\lVert V^{\mathrm{R}}_{i}\right\rVert_{\infty}+\left\lVert V_{i}\right\rVert_{\infty}\leq\frac{2}{1-\gamma}=:C_{\mathrm{w}}.

(81)

In addition, using (
29
) and (
32
) in Lemma
6
, we have

\displaystyle\eta_{N_{i}(s,a)}^{N}\leq\frac{6}{N(1-\gamma)},\qquad

\displaystyle\text{if }1\leq N_{i}(s,a)\leq N;

\displaystyle\eta_{N_{i}(s,a)}^{N}=0,\qquad

\displaystyle\text{if }N_{i}(s,a)>N,

which in turn allows us to define an upper bound
C_{\mathrm{u}}
:

\displaystyle\max_{N,s,a\in[T]\times{\mathcal{S}}\times\mathcal{A}}\eta_{N_{i}(s,a)}^{N}\leq\frac{6}{N(1-\gamma)}=:C_{\mathrm{u}}.

(82)

Lastly, we can verify

\displaystyle 0\leq\sum_{n=1}^{N}u_{t_{n}(s,a)}(s,a,N)=\sum_{n=1}^{N}\eta_{n}^{N}\leq 1

(83)

holds for all
(N,s,a)\in[T]\times{\mathcal{S}}\times\mathcal{A}
.

Hence, we can apply Lemma
7
with (
80
), (
81
), (
82
) and
(N,s,a)=(N_{t},s_{t},a_{t})
and conclude with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle|I_{1}|

\displaystyle=\left|\gamma\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t}}\eta_{n}^{N_{t}}\left(P_{t_{n}}-P_{s_{t},a_{t}}\right)\left(V_{t_{n}}-V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}}\right)\right|=\gamma\left|\sum_{i=1}^{t}X_{i}(s_{t},a_{t},N_{t})\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{C_{\mathrm{u}}\log^{2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}\sqrt{\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t}}u_{t_{n}}(s_{t},a_{t},N)\Var_{s_{t},a_{t}}\left(W_{t_{n}(s_{t},a_{t})}\right)}+\left(C_{\mathrm{u}}C_{\mathrm{w}}+\sqrt{\frac{C_{\mathrm{u}}}{N}}C_{\mathrm{w}}\right)\log^{2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{1}{N_{t}(1-\gamma)}\log^{2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}\sqrt{\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t}}\eta^{N_{t}}_{n}\Var_{s_{t},a_{t}}\left(V_{t_{n}}-V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}}\right)}+\frac{\log^{2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{N_{t}(1-\gamma)^{2}}

(84)

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{1}{N_{t}(1-\gamma)}\log^{2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}\sqrt{{\sigma}^{\mathrm{adv}}_{t_{N_{t}}+1}(s_{t},a_{t})-\left({\mu}^{\mathrm{adv}}_{t_{N_{t}}+1}(s_{t},a_{t})\right)^{2}}+\frac{\log^{2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(N_{t})^{3/4}(1-\gamma)^{2}}.

(85)

Above, the last inequality (
85
) requires additional work to establish. Its proof is deferred to Appendix
E.1
to streamline the presentation.

Step 3: controlling
I_{2}
.

Next, we turn to controlling the term
I_{2}
defined in (
79b
), which can be rearranged as

\displaystyle I_{2}=\gamma\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t}}\frac{1}{n}\eta_{n}^{N_{t}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(P_{t_{n}}-P_{s_{t},a_{t}}\right)V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{i}}=\gamma\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t}}\left(\sum_{n=i}^{N_{t}}\frac{\eta^{N_{t}}_{n}}{n}\right)\left(P_{t_{n}}-P_{s_{t},a_{t}}\right)V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{i}}.

(86)

In the following, we control
I_{2}
by invoking Lemma
7
again. Consider any
N\in[T]
. We abuse the notation by letting

\displaystyle W_{i}:=V^{\mathrm{R}}_{i}\qquad\text{and}\qquad u_{i}(s,a,N):=\sum_{n=N_{i}(s,a)}^{N}\frac{\eta_{n}^{N}}{n}\geq 0.

(87)

Moreover, define

\displaystyle\left\lVert W_{i}\right\rVert_{\infty}\leq\left\lVert V^{\mathrm{R}}_{i}\right\rVert_{\infty}\leq\frac{1}{1-\gamma}:=C_{\mathrm{w}}

(88)

and by (
31
) in Lemma
6
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{n=N_{i}(s,a)}^{N}\frac{\eta_{n}^{N}}{n}\leq\sum_{n=1}^{N}\frac{\eta^{N}_{n}}{n}\leq\frac{2}{N}:=C_{\mathrm{u}},

(89)

which immediately implies:

\displaystyle 0\leq\sum_{n=1}^{N}u_{t_{n}(s,a)}(s,a,N)\leq\sum_{n=1}^{N}\frac{2}{N}\leq 2.

(90)

Overall, the conditions for Lemma
7
are satisfied. Therefore, we can apply Lemma
7
with (
87
), (
88
), (
89
) and
(N,s,a)=(N_{t},s_{t},a_{t})
and conclude with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle|I_{2}|

\displaystyle=\left|\gamma\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t}}\left(\sum_{n=i}^{N_{t}}\frac{\eta^{N_{t}}_{n}}{n}\right)\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(P_{t_{n}}-P_{s_{t},a_{t}}\right)V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{i}}\right|=\gamma\left|\sum_{i=1}^{t}X_{i}(s_{t},a_{t},N_{t})\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{C_{\mathrm{u}}\log^{2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}\sqrt{\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t}}u_{t_{n}}(s_{t},a_{t},N)\Var_{s_{t},a_{t}}\left(W_{t_{n}(s_{t},a_{t})}\right)}+\left(C_{\mathrm{u}}C_{\mathrm{w}}+\sqrt{\frac{C_{\mathrm{u}}}{N}}C_{\mathrm{w}}\right)\log^{2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{1}{N_{t}}\log^{2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}\sqrt{\frac{1}{N_{t}}\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t}}\Var_{s_{t},a_{t}}\left(W_{t_{n}(s_{t},a_{t})}\right)}+\frac{1}{N_{t}(1-\gamma)}\log^{2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}

(91)

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{1}{N_{t}}\log^{2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}\sqrt{\sigma^{\mathrm{ref}}_{t_{N_{t}}+1}(s_{t},a_{t})-\left(\mu^{\mathrm{ref}}_{t_{N_{t}}+1}(s_{t},a_{t})\right)^{2}}+\frac{\log^{2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(N_{t})^{3/4}(1-\gamma)}

(92)

Above, the last inequality (
92
) requires additional work to establish. Its proof is deferred to Appendix
E.2
to streamline the presentation.

Step 4: combining
I_{1}
and
I_{2}
.

Summing up the results in (
85
) and (
92
), we can find an upper bound on
\left|\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t}}\eta^{N_{t}}_{n}\xi_{t_{n}}\right|
as follows:

\displaystyle\quad\ \left|\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t}}\eta^{N_{t}}_{n}\xi_{t_{n}}\right|\leq|I_{1}|+|I_{2}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{1}{N_{t}(1-\gamma)}\log^{2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}\sqrt{{\sigma}^{\mathrm{adv}}_{t_{N_{t}}+1}(s_{t},a_{t})-\left({\mu}^{\mathrm{adv}}_{t_{N_{t}}+1}(s_{t},a_{t})\right)^{2}}

\displaystyle\qquad+\sqrt{\frac{1}{N_{t}}\log^{2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}\sqrt{\sigma^{\mathrm{ref}}_{t_{N_{t}}+1}(s_{t},a_{t})-\left(\mu^{\mathrm{ref}}_{t_{N_{t}}+1}(s_{t},a_{t})\right)^{2}}+\frac{\log^{2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(N_{t})^{3/4}(1-\gamma)^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq B^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{N_{t}}+1}(s_{t},a_{t})+c_{b}\frac{\log^{2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(N_{t})^{3/4}(1-\gamma)^{2}}

(93)

for some sufficiently large absolute constant
c_{b}>0
. The last step follows from the definition of
B^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{N_{t}}+1}(s_{t},a_{t})
in Line
2
and
2
of Algorithm
2
.

With
\left|\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t}}\eta^{N_{t}}_{n}\xi_{t_{n}}\right|
dealt with, we still need to make some manipulation on
\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t}}\eta^{N_{t}}_{n}b^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}+1}
in order to establish (
78
). We do so by leveraging the following relation, which can be reasoned from the definitions of
b^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}+1}
and
\delta^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}+1}
in Line
2
and
2
of Algorithm
2
:

b^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}+1}=\Big(1-\frac{1}{\eta_{n}}\Big)B_{t_{n}}^{\mathrm{R}}(s_{t},a_{t})+\frac{1}{\eta_{n}}B_{t_{n}+1}^{\mathrm{R}}(s_{t},a_{t})+\frac{c_{b}}{n^{3/4}(1-\gamma)^{2}}\log^{2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}

(94)

by the fact
-\frac{1-\eta_{n}}{\eta_{n}}=1-\frac{1}{\eta_{n}}
.
Using (
94
) and the definition of
\eta_{n}^{N}
in (
29
), we have

\displaystyle\quad\ \sum_{n=1}^{N_{t}}\eta^{N_{t}}_{n}b^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}+1}

\displaystyle=\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t}}\eta_{n}\prod_{i=n+1}^{N_{t}}(1-\eta_{i})\left(\left(1-\frac{1}{\eta_{n}}\right)B_{t_{n}}^{\mathrm{R}}(s_{t},a_{t})+\frac{1}{\eta_{n}}B_{t_{n}+1}^{\mathrm{R}}(s_{t},a_{t})\right)+c_{b}\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t}}\frac{\eta_{n}^{N_{t}}}{n^{3/4}(1-\gamma)^{2}}\log^{2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}

\displaystyle=\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t}}\prod_{i=n+1}^{N_{t}}(1-\eta_{i})\left(-\left(1-\eta_{n}\right)B_{t_{n}}^{\mathrm{R}}(s_{t},a_{t})+B_{t_{n}+1}^{\mathrm{R}}(s_{t},a_{t})\right)+c_{b}\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t}}\frac{\eta_{n}^{N_{t}}}{n^{3/4}(1-\gamma)^{2}}\log^{2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}

\displaystyle=\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t}}\left(\prod_{i=n+1}^{N_{t}}(1-\eta_{i})B_{t_{n}+1}^{\mathrm{R}}(s_{t},a_{t})-\prod_{i=n}^{N_{t}}(1-\eta_{i})B_{t_{n}}^{\mathrm{R}}(s_{t},a_{t})\right)+c_{b}\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t}}\frac{\eta_{n}^{N_{t}}}{n^{3/4}(1-\gamma)^{2}}\log^{2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}

\displaystyle\overset{\mathrm{(i)}}{=}\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t}}\prod_{i=n+1}^{N_{t}}(1-\eta_{i})B_{t_{n}+1}^{\mathrm{R}}(s_{t},a_{t})-\sum_{n=2}^{N_{t}}\prod_{i=n}^{N_{t}}(1-\eta_{i})B_{t_{n}}^{\mathrm{R}}(s_{t},a_{t})+c_{b}\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t}}\frac{\eta_{n}^{N_{t}}}{n^{3/4}(1-\gamma)^{2}}\log^{2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}

\displaystyle\overset{\mathrm{(ii)}}{=}\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t}}\prod_{i=n+1}^{N_{t}}(1-\eta_{i})B_{t_{n}+1}^{\mathrm{R}}(s_{t},a_{t})-\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t}-1}\prod_{i=n+1}^{N_{t}}(1-\eta_{i})B_{t_{n}+1}^{\mathrm{R}}(s_{t},a_{t})+c_{b}\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t}}\frac{\eta_{n}^{N_{t}}}{n^{3/4}(1-\gamma)^{2}}\log^{2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}

\displaystyle=B^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{N_{t}}+1}(s_{t},a_{t})+c_{b}\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t}}\frac{\eta_{n}^{N_{t}}}{n^{3/4}(1-\gamma)^{2}}\log^{2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}.

(95)

Above, (i) is due to
B^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{1}}(s_{t},a_{t})=0
. (ii) can be obtained as follows:

\displaystyle\sum_{n=2}^{N_{t}}\prod_{i=n}^{N_{t}}(1-\eta_{i})B_{t_{n}}^{\mathrm{R}}(s_{t},a_{t})

\displaystyle=\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t}-1}\prod_{i=n+1}^{N_{t}}(1-\eta_{i})B_{t_{n+1}}^{\mathrm{R}}(s_{t},a_{t})=\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t}-1}\prod_{i=n+1}^{N_{t}}(1-\eta_{i})B_{t_{n}+1}^{\mathrm{R}}(s_{t},a_{t}),

in which the summation is re-indexed over the first equality by replacing
n
with
n+1
, and the second equality is true because the state-action pair

(s_{t},a_{t})
has not been visited between the
(t_{n}+1)
-th step and the
(t_{n+1}-1)
-th step.

Furthermore, (
95
) allows us to conclude

B^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{N_{t}}+1}(s_{t},a_{t})+c_{b}\frac{\log^{2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(N_{t})^{3/4}(1-\gamma)^{2}}\leq\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t}}\eta^{N_{t}}_{n}b^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}+1}\leq B^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{N_{t}}+1}(s_{t},a_{t})+2c_{b}\frac{\log^{2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(N_{t})^{3/4}(1-\gamma)^{2}},

(96)

because (
31
) of Lemma
6
implies

\frac{1}{(N_{t})^{3/4}}\leq\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t}}\frac{\eta^{N_{t}}_{n}}{n^{3/4}}\leq\frac{2}{(N_{t})^{3/4}}.

Bringing (
96
) into (
93
) gives

\left|\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t}}\eta^{N_{t}}_{n}\xi_{t_{n}}\right|\leq B^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{N_{t}}+1}(s_{t},a_{t})+c_{b}\frac{\log^{2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(N_{h}^{k})^{3/4}(1-\gamma)^{2}}\leq\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t}}\eta^{N_{t}}_{n}b^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}+1}

(97)

as claimed in (
78
).

Finally, by proving (
78
), we have proven the induction step (
69
) and thus concluded the proof of
Q_{t}(s,a)\geq Q^{\star}(s,a)
.

To prove the optimism in
V_{t}
, given (
1
), we can easily see from
V_{t}
’s update rule that for all
(t,s,a)\in[T]\times{\mathcal{S}}\times\mathcal{A}
,

V_{t}(s)\geq Q_{t}(s,\pi^{\star}(s))\geq Q^{\star}(s,\pi^{\star}(s))=V^{\star}(s).

E.1
Proof of Inequality (
85
)

As it turns out, it suffices to examine the following term in order to prove (
85
):

\displaystyle I_{3}:=\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t}}\eta^{N_{t}}_{n}\Var_{s_{t},a_{t}}\left(V_{t_{n}}-{V}^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}}\right)-{\sigma}^{\mathrm{adv}}_{t_{N_{t}}+1}(s_{t},a_{t})+\left({\mu}^{\mathrm{adv}}_{t_{N_{t}}+1}(s_{t},a_{t})\right)^{2}.

(98)

In this section, we would like to show an upper bound on
I_{3}
.

Before we proceed, recall that the update rules of
\mu^{\mathrm{adv}}_{t_{n+1}}
and
{\sigma}^{\mathrm{adv}}_{t_{n+1}}
in Line
2
and
2
of Algorithm
2
are

\displaystyle\mu^{\mathrm{adv}}_{t_{n+1}}(s_{t},a_{t})

\displaystyle=\mu^{\mathrm{adv}}_{t_{n}+1}(s_{t},a_{t})=(1-\eta_{n})\mu^{\mathrm{adv}}_{t_{n}}(s_{t},a_{t})+\eta_{n}\left(V_{t_{n}}(s_{t_{n}+1})-V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}}(s_{t_{n}+1})\right),

\displaystyle\sigma^{\mathrm{adv}}_{t_{n+1}}(s_{t},a_{t})

\displaystyle=\sigma^{\mathrm{adv}}_{t_{n}+1}(s_{t},a_{t})=(1-\eta_{n})\sigma^{\mathrm{adv}}_{t_{n}}(s_{t},a_{t})+\eta_{n}\left(V_{t_{n}}(s_{t_{n}+1})-V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}}(s_{t_{n}+1})\right)^{2}.

We can expand these equations recursively and write

\displaystyle\mu^{\mathrm{adv}}_{t_{N_{t}}+1}(s_{t},a_{t})

\displaystyle=\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t}}\eta_{n}^{N_{t}}\left(V_{t_{n}}(s_{t_{n}+1})-V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}}(s_{t_{n}+1})\right)=\sum_{n=1}^{{N_{t}}}\eta_{n}^{N_{t}}P_{t_{n}}\left(V_{t_{n}}-{V}^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}}\right),

(99a)

\displaystyle{\sigma}^{\mathrm{adv}}_{t_{N_{t}}+1}(s_{t},a_{t})

\displaystyle=\sum_{n=1}^{{N_{t}}}\eta_{n}^{N_{t}}\left(V_{t_{n}}(s_{t_{n}+1})-V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}}(s_{t_{n}+1})\right)^{2}=\sum_{n=1}^{{N_{t}}}\eta_{n}^{N_{t}}P_{t_{n}}\left(V_{t_{n}}-{V}^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}}\right)^{2}.

(99b)

Noting that
\sum_{n=1}^{{N_{t}}}\eta_{n}^{N_{t}}=1
, we can use Jensen’s inequality and reason from (
99a
) and (
99b
) that:

\displaystyle{\sigma}^{\mathrm{adv}}_{t_{N_{t}}+1}(s_{t},a_{t})\geq\left(\mu^{\mathrm{adv}}_{t_{N_{t}}+1}(s_{t},a_{t})\right)^{2}.

(100)

Recall by the definition of variance in (
28
), we have

\Var_{s_{t},a_{t}}\left(V_{t_{n}}-{V}^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}}\right)=P_{s_{t},a_{t}}\left(V_{t_{n}}-{V}^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}}\right)^{2}-\left(P_{s_{t},a_{t}}\left(V_{t_{n}}-{V}^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}}\right)\right)^{2}.

Bringing this into (
100
), we can decompose
I_{3}
as follows

\displaystyle I_{3}

\displaystyle=\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t}}\eta^{N_{t}}_{n}P_{s_{t},a_{t}}\left(V_{t_{n}}-{V}^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}}\right)^{2}-\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t}}\eta^{N_{t}}_{n}P_{t_{n}}\left(V_{t_{n}}-{V}^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\left(\sum_{n=1}^{{N_{t}}}\eta_{n}^{N_{t}}P_{t_{n}}\left(V_{t_{n}}-{V}^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}}\right)\right)^{2}-\sum_{n=1}^{{N_{t}}}\eta_{n}^{N_{t}}\left(P_{s_{t},a_{t}}\left(V_{t_{n}}-{V}^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}}\right)\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\underbrace{\left|\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t}}\eta^{N_{t}}_{n}\left(P_{s_{t},a_{t}}-P_{t_{n}}\right)\left(V_{t_{n}}-{V}^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}}\right)^{2}\right|}_{:=I_{3,1}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\underbrace{\left(\sum_{n=1}^{{N_{t}}}\eta_{n}^{N_{t}}P_{t_{n}}\left(V_{t_{n}}-{V}^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}}\right)\right)^{2}-\sum_{n=1}^{{N_{t}}}\eta_{n}^{N_{t}}\left(P_{s_{t},a_{t}}\left(V_{t_{n}}-{V}^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}}\right)\right)^{2}}_{:=I_{3,2}}.

(101)

It remains to control
I_{3,1}
and
I_{3,2}
in (
101
) separately.

Step 1: controlling
I_{3,1}
.

Now, let us control the term
I_{3,1}
defined in (
101
) by invoking Lemma
7
. Consider any
N\in[T]
. We set

\displaystyle W_{i}:=\left(V_{i}-{V}^{\mathrm{R}}_{i}\right)^{2}\qquad\text{and}\qquad u_{i}(s,a,N):=\eta_{N_{i}(s,a)}^{N}.

(102)

Moreover, define

\displaystyle\left\lVert W_{i}\right\rVert_{\infty}\leq\left(\left\lVert V^{\mathrm{R}}_{i}\right\rVert_{\infty}+\left\lVert V_{i}\right\rVert_{\infty}\right)^{2}\leq\frac{4}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}:=C_{\mathrm{w}},

(103)

and same as (
82
), we have

\displaystyle\max_{N,s,a\in[T]\times{\mathcal{S}}\times\mathcal{A}}\eta_{N_{i}(s,a)}^{N}\leq\frac{8}{N(1-\gamma)}:=C_{\mathrm{u}}.

(104)

Clearly, we can verify

\displaystyle 0\leq\sum_{n=1}^{N}u_{t_{n}(s,a)}(s,a,N)=\sum_{n=1}^{N}\eta_{n}^{N}\leq 1

(105)

holds for all
(N,s,a)\in[T]\times{\mathcal{S}}\times\mathcal{A}
, and the conditions for Lemma
7
are satisfied.

Therefore, with
(N,s,a)=(N_{t},s_{t},a_{t})
, we can apply Lemma
7
with (
102
), (
103
) and (
104
) to conclude with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle|I_{3,1}|

\displaystyle=\left|\sum_{n=1}^{{N_{t}}}\eta_{n}^{N_{t}}\left(P_{t_{n}}-P_{s_{t},a_{t}}\right)\left(V_{t_{n}}-{V}^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}}\right)^{2}\right|

\displaystyle=\left|\sum_{i=1}^{t}X_{i}(s_{t},a_{t},N_{t})\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{C_{\mathrm{u}}\log^{2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}\sqrt{\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t}}u_{t_{n}}(s_{t},a_{t},N_{t})\Var_{s_{t},a_{t}}\left(W_{t_{n}}\right)}+\left(C_{\mathrm{u}}C_{\mathrm{w}}+\sqrt{\frac{C_{\mathrm{u}}}{N}}C_{\mathrm{w}}\right)\log^{2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{1}{N_{t}(1-\gamma)}\log^{2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}\sqrt{\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t}}\eta^{N_{t}}_{n}\Var_{s_{t},a_{t}}\left(\left(V_{t_{n}}-{V}^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}}\right)^{2}\right)}+\frac{\log^{2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{N_{t}(1-\gamma)^{3}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{1}{N_{t}(1-\gamma)^{5}}\log^{2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}+\frac{1}{N_{t}(1-\gamma)^{3}}\log^{2}\frac{SAT}{\delta},

(106)

in which the last step follows from
\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t}}\eta^{N_{t}}_{n}\leq 1
and
\Var_{s_{t},a_{t}}\left(\left(V_{t_{n}}-{V}^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}}\right)^{2}\right)\leq\left\lVert\left(V_{t_{n}}-{V}^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}}\right)^{4}\right\rVert_{\infty}\leq\frac{16}{(1-\gamma)^{4}}
.

Step 2: controlling
I_{3,2}
.

We can use Jensen’s inequality and write
I_{3,2}
as

\displaystyle I_{3,2}

\displaystyle=\left(\sum_{n=1}^{{N_{t}}}\eta_{n}^{N_{t}}P_{t_{n}}\left(V_{t_{n}}-{V}^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}}\right)\right)^{2}-\sum_{n=1}^{{N_{t}}}\eta_{n}^{N_{t}}\left(P_{s_{t},a_{t}}\big(V_{t_{n}}-{V}^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}}\big)\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\sum_{n=1}^{{N_{t}}}\eta_{n}^{N_{t}}P_{t_{n}}\left(V_{t_{n}}-{V}^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}}\right)\right)^{2}-\left\{\sum_{n=1}^{{N_{t}}}\eta_{n}^{N_{t}}\right\}\left\{\sum_{n=1}^{{N_{t}}}\eta_{n}^{N_{t}}\left(P_{s_{t},a_{t}}\left(V_{t_{n}}-{V}^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}}\right)\right)^{2}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\sum_{n=1}^{{N_{t}}}\eta_{n}^{N_{t}}P_{t_{n}}\left(V_{t_{n}}-{V}^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}}\right)\right)^{2}-\left(\sum_{n=1}^{{N_{t}}}\left(\eta_{n}^{N_{t}}\right)^{1/2}\left(\eta_{n}^{N_{t}}\right)^{1/2}P_{s_{t},a_{t}}\left(V_{t_{n}}-{V}^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}}\right)\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=\left(\sum_{n=1}^{{N_{t}}}\eta_{n}^{N_{t}}P_{t_{n}}\left(V_{t_{n}}-{V}^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}}\right)\right)^{2}-\left(\sum_{n=1}^{{N_{t}}}\eta_{n}^{N_{t}}P_{s_{t},a_{t}}\left(V_{t_{n}}-{V}^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}}\right)\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left\{\sum_{n=1}^{{N_{t}}}\eta_{n}^{N_{t}}\left(P_{t_{n}}-P_{s_{t},a_{t}}\right)\left(V_{t_{n}}-{V}^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}}\right)\right\}\left\{\sum_{n=1}^{{N_{t}}}\eta_{n}^{N_{t}}\left(P_{t_{n}}+P_{s_{t},a_{t}}\right)\left(V_{t_{n}}-{V}^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}}\right)\right\}.

(107)

With this upper bound on
I_{3,2}
, we now aim to show

I_{3,2}\leq C_{3,2}\left(\sqrt{\frac{1}{N_{t}(1-\gamma)^{5}}\log^{2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}+\frac{1}{N_{t}(1-\gamma)^{3}}\log^{2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}\right),

(108)

for some universal constant
C_{3,2}>0
.

Since (
108
) is trivially true if
I_{3,2}\leq 0
, it suffices to consider the case where
I_{3,2}>0
only. To proceed, notice that the first factor in the product in (
107
) is exactly
I_{1}
in (
79a
), which can be bounded with our previous result (
84
):

\displaystyle|I_{1}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{1}{N_{t}(1-\gamma)}\log^{2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}\sqrt{\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t}}\eta^{N_{t}}_{n}\Var_{s,a}\left(V_{t_{n}}-V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}}\right)}+\frac{\log^{2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{N_{t}(1-\gamma)^{2}}

\displaystyle\overset{\mathrm{(i)}}{\lesssim}\sqrt{\frac{1}{N_{t}(1-\gamma)^{3}}\log^{2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}\sqrt{\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t}}\eta^{N_{t}}_{n}}+\frac{\log^{2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{N_{t}(1-\gamma)^{2}}

\displaystyle\overset{\mathrm{(ii)}}{\lesssim}\sqrt{\frac{1}{N_{t}(1-\gamma)^{3}}\log^{2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}+\frac{1}{N_{t}(1-\gamma)^{2}}\log^{2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}

(109)

with probability at least
1-\delta
. Above, (i) arises from

\Var_{s,a}\big(V_{t_{n}}-V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}}\big)\leq\left\lVert\left(V_{t_{n}}-V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}}\right)^{2}\right\rVert_{\infty}\leq\frac{4}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}
and (ii) is true due to
\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t}}\eta^{N_{t}}_{n}\leq 1
.

On the other hand, the second factor in the product in (
107
) can be simply bounded as follows:

\displaystyle\left|\sum_{n=1}^{{N_{t}}}\eta_{n}^{N_{t}}\left(P_{t_{n}}+P_{s_{t},a_{t}}\right)\left(V_{t_{n}}-{V}^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}}\right)\right|\leq\sum_{n=1}^{{N_{t}}}\eta_{n}^{N_{t}}\left(\left\lVert P_{t_{n}}\right\rVert_{1}+\left\lVert P_{s_{t},a_{t}}\right\rVert_{1}\right)\left\lVert V_{t_{n}}-{V}^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}}\right\rVert_{\infty}\leq\frac{2}{1-\gamma},

(110)

in which we use the elementary facts
\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t}}\eta^{N_{t}}_{n}\leq 1
,
\left\lVert V_{t_{n}}-{V}^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}}\right\rVert_{\infty}\leq\frac{1}{1-\gamma}
and
\left\lVert P_{t_{n}}\right\rVert_{1}=\left\lVert P_{s_{t},a_{t}}\right\rVert_{1}=1
.

Substituting (
109
) and (
110
) back into (
107
), we can obtain the desired bound (
108
) for the case
I_{3,2}>0
.
Combined this with the trivial case of
I_{3,2}\leq 0
, we have established (
108
).

Step 3: combining the preceding bounds.

Let us bring the bounds (
106
) and (
108
) into (
101
). This gives

\displaystyle I_{3}\leq|I_{3,1}|+I_{3,2}\leq C_{3,3}\left\{\sqrt{\frac{1}{N_{t}(1-\gamma)^{5}}\log^{2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}+\frac{1}{N_{t}(1-\gamma)^{3}}\log^{2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}\right\}

for some absolute constant
C_{3,3}>0
. Writing
I_{3}
with its definition in (
98
), rearranging the inequality results, and taking square root on both sides, we have

\displaystyle\left\{\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t}}\eta^{N_{t}}_{n}\Var_{s_{t},a_{t}}\left(V_{t_{n}}-{V}^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}}\right)\right\}^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq\left\{{\sigma}^{\mathrm{adv}}_{t_{N_{t}}+1}(s_{t},a_{t})-\left({\mu}^{\mathrm{adv}}_{t_{N_{t}}+1}(s_{t},a_{t})\right)^{2}\right\}^{1/2}

\displaystyle\quad+\sqrt{C_{3,3}}\left(\sqrt{\frac{1}{N_{t}(1-\gamma)^{5}}\log^{2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}+\frac{1}{N_{t}(1-\gamma)^{3}}\log^{2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq\left\{{\sigma}^{\mathrm{adv}}_{t_{N_{t}}+1}(s_{t},a_{t})-\left({\mu}^{\mathrm{adv}}_{t_{N_{t}}+1}(s_{t},a_{t})\right)^{2}\right\}^{1/2}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{\left(N_{t}\right)^{1/4}(1-\gamma)^{5/4}}\log^{1/2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}+\frac{1}{\left(N_{t}\right)^{1/2}(1-\gamma)^{3/2}}\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}.

in which we also use the result of (
100
).

Substitution of this into (
84
) leads to the desired result (
85
).

E.2
Proof of Inequality (
92
)

Similar to the proof of (
85
), it suffices to examine the following term in order to prove (
92
):

\displaystyle I_{4}:=\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t}}\eta^{N_{t}}_{n}\Var_{s_{t},a_{t}}\left({V}^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}}\right)-{\sigma}^{\mathrm{ref}}_{t_{N_{t}}+1}(s_{t},a_{t})+\left({\mu}^{\mathrm{ref}}_{t_{N_{t}}+1}(s_{t},a_{t})\right)^{2}.

(111)

In this section, we would like to show an upper bound on
I_{4}
.

Before we proceed, recall that the update rules of
\mu^{\mathrm{ref}}_{t_{n+1}}
and
{\sigma}^{\mathrm{ref}}_{t_{n+1}}
in Line
2
and
2
of Algorithm
2
are

\displaystyle{\mu}^{\mathrm{ref}}_{t_{n+1}}(s_{t},a_{t})

\displaystyle={\mu}^{\mathrm{ref}}_{t_{n}+1}(s_{t},a_{t})=\left(1-\frac{1}{n}\right){\mu}^{\mathrm{ref}}_{t_{n}}(s_{t},a_{t})+\frac{1}{n}{V}^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}}(s_{t_{n}+1}),

\displaystyle{\sigma}^{\mathrm{ref}}_{t_{n+1}}(s_{t},a_{t})

\displaystyle={\sigma}^{\mathrm{ref}}_{t_{n}+1}(s_{t},a_{t})=\left(1-\frac{1}{n}\right){\sigma}^{\mathrm{ref}}_{t_{n}}(s_{t},a_{t})+\frac{1}{n}\left({V}^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}}(s_{t_{n}+1})\right)^{2},

We can expand these equations recursively and write

\displaystyle{\mu}^{\mathrm{ref}}_{t_{N_{t}}+1}(s_{t},a_{t})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{N_{t}}\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t}}{V}_{t_{n}}^{\mathrm{R}}(s_{t_{n}+1})=\frac{1}{N_{t}}\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t}}P_{t_{n}}{V}_{t_{n}}^{\mathrm{R}},

(112a)

\displaystyle{\sigma}^{\mathrm{ref}}_{t_{N_{t}}+1}(s_{t},a_{t})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{N_{t}}\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t}}\left({V}_{t_{n}}^{\mathrm{R}}(s_{t_{n}+1})\right)^{2}=\frac{1}{N_{t}}\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t}}P_{t_{n}}\left({V}_{t_{n}}^{\mathrm{R}}\right)^{2}.

(112b)

Noting that
\sum_{n=1}^{{N_{t}}}\eta_{n}^{N_{t}}=1
, we can use Jensen’s inequality and reason from (
112a
) and (
112b
) that:

\displaystyle{\sigma}^{\mathrm{ref}}_{t_{N_{t}}+1}(s_{t},a_{t})\geq\left({\mu}^{\mathrm{ref}}_{t_{N_{t}}+1}(s_{t},a_{t})\right)^{2}.

(113)

Recall by the definition of variance in (
28
), we have

\Var_{s_{t},a_{t}}\left({V}^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}}\right)=P_{s_{t},a_{t}}\left({V}^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}}\right)^{2}-\left(P_{s_{t},a_{t}}\left({V}^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}}\right)\right)^{2}.

Combined this with (
113
), we can decompose
I_{4}
as follows

\displaystyle I_{4}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{{N_{t}}}\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t}}\left(P_{s_{t},a_{t}}\left({V}^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}}\right)^{2}-\left(P_{s_{t},a_{t}}\left({V}^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}}\right)\right)^{2}\right)-\frac{1}{N_{t}}\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t}}P_{t_{n}}\left({V}_{t_{n}}^{\mathrm{R}}\right)^{2}+\left(\frac{1}{N_{t}}\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t}}P_{t_{n}}{V}_{t_{n}}^{\mathrm{R}}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=\underbrace{\frac{1}{{N_{t}}}\sum_{n=1}^{{N_{t}}}\left(P_{s_{t},a_{t}}-P_{t_{n}}\right)\left({V}^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}}\right)^{2}}_{:=\,I_{4,1}}+\underbrace{\left(\frac{1}{N_{t}}\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t}}P_{t_{n}}{V}_{t_{n}}^{\mathrm{R}}\right)^{2}-\frac{1}{{N_{t}}}\sum_{n=1}^{{N_{t}}}\left(P_{s_{t},a_{t}}{V}^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}}\right)^{2}}_{:=\,I_{4,2}}.

(114)

It remains to control
I_{4,1}
and
I_{4,2}
in (
114
) separately.

Step 1: controlling
I_{4,1}
.

Now, let us control
I_{4,1}
defined in (
114
) by invoking Lemma
7
. Consider any
N\in[T]
. We set

\displaystyle W_{i}:=\left({V}^{\mathrm{R}}_{i}\right)^{2}\qquad\text{and}\qquad u_{i}(s,a,N):=\frac{1}{N}.

(115)

Moreover, define

\displaystyle|u_{i}(s,a,N)|=\frac{1}{N}:=C_{\mathrm{u}}\qquad\text{and}\qquad\left\lVert W_{i}\right\rVert_{\infty}\leq\left\lVert V_{i}^{\mathrm{R}}\right\rVert_{\infty}^{2}\leq\frac{1}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}:=C_{\mathrm{w}}.

(116)

Clearly, we can verify

\displaystyle\sum_{n=1}^{N}u_{t_{n}(s,a)}(s,a,N)=\sum_{n=1}^{N}\frac{1}{N}=1

(117)

holds for all
(N,s,a)\in[T]\times{\mathcal{S}}\times\mathcal{A}
, and the conditions for Lemma
7
are satisfied.

Therefore, we can apply Lemma
7
with (
115
), (
116
) and
(N,s,a)=(N_{t},s_{t},a_{t})
and conclude with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle|I_{4,1}|

\displaystyle=\left|\sum_{n=1}^{{N_{t}}}\eta_{n}^{N_{t}}\left(P_{t_{n}}-P_{s_{t},a_{t}}\right)\left({V}^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}}\right)^{2}\right|

\displaystyle=\left|\sum_{i=1}^{t}X_{i}(s_{t},a_{t},N_{t})\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{C_{\mathrm{u}}\log^{2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}\sqrt{\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t}}u_{t_{n}}(s_{t},a_{t},N_{t})\Var_{s_{t},a_{t}}\left(W_{t_{n}}\right)}+\left(C_{\mathrm{u}}C_{\mathrm{w}}+\sqrt{\frac{C_{\mathrm{u}}}{N}}C_{\mathrm{w}}\right)\log^{2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{\log^{2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{{N_{t}(1-\gamma)^{4}}}}+\frac{\log^{2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{{N_{t}(1-\gamma)^{2}}},

(118)

in which the last inequality results from
\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t}}\eta^{N_{t}}_{n}\leq 1
and the fact
\Var_{s_{t},a_{t}}\left(\left(V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}}\right)^{2}\right)\leq\left\lVert\left({V}^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}}\right)^{4}\right\rVert_{\infty}\leq\frac{1}{(1-\gamma)^{4}}
.

Step 2: controlling
I_{4,2}
.

We can use Jensen’s inequality and write
I_{4,2}
as

\displaystyle I_{4,2}

\displaystyle=\left(\sum_{n=1}^{{N_{t}}}\eta_{n}^{N_{t}}P_{t_{n}}{V}^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}}\right)^{2}-\sum_{n=1}^{{N_{t}}}\eta_{n}^{N_{t}}\left(P_{s_{t},a_{t}}{V}^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\sum_{n=1}^{{N_{t}}}\eta_{n}^{N_{t}}P_{t_{n}}{V}^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}}\right)^{2}-\left\{\sum_{n=1}^{{N_{t}}}\eta_{n}^{N_{t}}\right\}\left\{\sum_{n=1}^{{N_{t}}}\eta_{n}^{N_{t}}\left(P_{s_{t},a_{t}}{V}^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}}\right)^{2}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\sum_{n=1}^{{N_{t}}}\eta_{n}^{N_{t}}P_{t_{n}}{V}^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}}\right)^{2}-\left(\sum_{n=1}^{{N_{t}}}\left(\eta_{n}^{N_{t}}\right)^{1/2}\left(\eta_{n}^{N_{t}}\right)^{1/2}P_{s_{t},a_{t}}{V}^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=\left(\sum_{n=1}^{{N_{t}}}\eta_{n}^{N_{t}}P_{t_{n}}{V}^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}}\right)^{2}-\left(\sum_{n=1}^{{N_{t}}}\eta_{n}^{N_{t}}P_{s_{t},a_{t}}{V}^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left\{\sum_{n=1}^{{N_{t}}}\eta_{n}^{N_{t}}\left(P_{t_{n}}-P_{s_{t},a_{t}}\right){V}^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}}\right\}\left\{\sum_{n=1}^{{N_{t}}}\eta_{n}^{N_{t}}\left(P_{t_{n}}+P_{s_{t},a_{t}}\right){V}^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}}\right\}.

(119)

With this upper bound on
I_{4,2}
, we now aim to show

I_{4,2}\leq C_{4,2}\left(\sqrt{\frac{1}{{N_{t}}(1-\gamma)^{4}}\log^{2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}+\frac{1}{{N_{t}(1-\gamma)^{2}}}\log^{2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}\right),

(120)

for some universal constant
C_{4,2}>0
.

Since (
120
) is trivially true if
I_{4,2}\leq 0
, it suffices to consider the case where
I_{4,2}>0
only. To proceed, we use Lemma
7
on the first factor in the product in (
119
). Let us abuse the notation and set

\displaystyle W_{i}:={V}^{\mathrm{R}}_{i}\qquad\text{and}\qquad u_{i}(s,a,N):=\frac{1}{N},

as well as

\displaystyle|u_{i}(s,a,N)|=\frac{1}{N}:=C_{\mathrm{u}}\qquad\text{and}\qquad\left\lVert W_{i}\right\rVert_{\infty}\leq\frac{1}{1-\gamma}:=C_{\mathrm{w}}.

In the same way as our proof for the upper bound on
I_{4,1}
, we Lemma
7
with
(N,s,a)=(N_{t},s_{t},a_{t})
and have

\left|\frac{1}{{N_{t}}}\sum_{n=1}^{{N_{t}}}\left(P_{t_{n}}-P_{s_{t},a_{t}}\right){V}_{t_{n}}^{\mathrm{R}}\right|\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{\log^{2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{{N_{t}}(1-\gamma)^{2}}}+\frac{\log^{2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{{N_{t}}(1-\gamma)}

(121)

with probability at least
1-\delta
.

On the other hand, the second factor in the product in (
119
) can be simply bounded as follows:

\displaystyle\left|\sum_{n=1}^{{N_{t}}}\eta_{n}^{N_{t}}\left(P_{t_{n}}+P_{s_{t},a_{t}}\right){V}^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}}\right|\leq\sum_{n=1}^{{N_{t}}}\eta_{n}^{N_{t}}\left(\left\lVert P_{t_{n}}\right\rVert_{1}+\left\lVert P_{s_{t},a_{t}}\right\rVert_{1}\right)\left\lVert{V}^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}}\right\rVert_{\infty}\leq\frac{2}{1-\gamma},

(122)

in which we use the elementary facts
\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t}}\eta^{N_{t}}_{n}\leq 1
,
\left\lVert{V}^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}}\right\rVert_{\infty}\leq\frac{1}{1-\gamma}
and
\left\lVert P_{t_{n}}\right\rVert_{1}=\left\lVert P_{s_{t},a_{t}}\right\rVert_{1}=1
.

Substituting (
121
) and (
122
) back into (
119
), we can obtain the desired bound (
120
) for the case
I_{4,2}>0
.
Combined this with the trivial case of
I_{4,2}\leq 0
, we have established (
120
).

Step 3: combining the preceding bounds.

Let us bring the bounds (
118
) and (
120
) into (
114
). This gives

\displaystyle I_{4}\leq|I_{4,1}|+I_{4,2}\leq C_{4,3}\left\{\sqrt{\frac{1}{N_{t}(1-\gamma)^{4}}\log^{2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}+\frac{1}{N_{t}(1-\gamma)^{2}}\log^{2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}\right\}

for some absolute constant
C_{4,3}>0
. Writing
I_{4}
with its definition in (
111
) and rearranging the inequality results, and taking square root on both sides, we have

\displaystyle\quad\ \left\{\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t}}\eta^{N_{t}}_{n}\Var_{s_{t},a_{t}}\left({V}^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}}\right)\right\}^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq\left\{{\sigma}^{\mathrm{ref}}_{t_{N_{t}}+1}(s_{t},a_{t})-\left({\mu}^{\mathrm{ref}}_{t_{N_{t}}+1}(s_{t},a_{t})\right)^{2}\right\}^{1/2}+\sqrt{C_{4,3}}\left(\sqrt{\frac{1}{N_{t}(1-\gamma)^{4}}\log^{2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}+\frac{1}{N_{t}(1-\gamma)^{2}}\log^{2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq\left\{{\sigma}^{\mathrm{ref}}_{t_{N_{t}}+1}(s_{t},a_{t})-\left({\mu}^{\mathrm{ref}}_{t_{N_{t}}+1}(s_{t},a_{t})\right)^{2}\right\}^{1/2}+\frac{1}{\left(N_{t}\right)^{1/4}(1-\gamma)}\log^{1/2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}+\frac{1}{\left(N_{t}\right)^{1/2}(1-\gamma)}\log\frac{SAT}{\delta},

in which we also use the result of (
100
).

Substitution into (
91
) leads to the desired result (
92
).

Appendix F
Proof of Lemma
5

Let us decompose
\zeta_{t}
(defined in (
57
)) into three component terms and examine each of them separately:

\displaystyle\zeta_{t}:=\underbrace{V_{t-1}(s_{t})-Q_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}_{\zeta_{t,1}}+\gamma\underbrace{\left(P_{s_{t},a_{t}}-P_{t}\right)\left(V^{\star}-V^{\pi_{t}}\right)}_{\zeta_{t,2}}+\gamma\underbrace{\left(V^{\pi_{t+1}}(s_{t+1})-V^{\pi_{t}}(s_{t+1})\right)}_{\zeta_{t,3}}.

(123)

We can find upper bounds for each of these three terms as follows:

\sum_{t=1}^{T}\zeta_{t,1}\lesssim\frac{SA}{1-\gamma}\log T+\frac{(SA)^{3/4}T^{1/4}\log^{5/4}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{5/4}}+\sqrt{\frac{SA\log^{2}T}{1-\gamma}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\big(V_{t}(s_{t+1})-V^{\pi_{t+1}}(s_{t+1})\big)},

(124)

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\zeta_{t,2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{SA\log\frac{T}{\delta}}{1-\gamma}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\big(V^{\star}(s_{t+1})-V^{\pi_{t+1}}(s_{t+1})\big)}+\frac{\left(SA\right)^{5/8}T^{1/8}}{(1-\gamma)^{11/8}}\left(\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}\right)^{9/8}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\frac{(SA)^{1/2}T^{1/4}}{(1-\gamma)^{5/4}}\left(\log\frac{T}{\delta}\right)^{3/4}+\frac{SA}{1-\gamma}\log\frac{T}{\delta},

(125)

\sum_{t=1}^{T}\zeta_{t,3}\lesssim\frac{SA}{1-\gamma}\log T+\frac{(SA)^{3/4}T^{1/4}\log^{5/4}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{5/4}}+\sqrt{\frac{SA\log^{2}T}{1-\gamma}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\big(V_{t}(s_{t+1})-V^{\pi_{t+1}}(s_{t+1})\big)}.

(126)

The proofs for these inequalities are deferred to Appendix
F.1
,
F.2
and
F.3
.

Combining (
124
), (
125
) and (
126
), we can obtain the final bound: with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\zeta_{t}

\displaystyle=\sum_{t=1}^{T}\zeta_{t,1}+\zeta_{t,2}+\zeta_{t,3}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{SA}{1-\gamma}\log T+\frac{(SA)^{3/4}T^{1/4}\log^{5/4}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{5/4}}+\sqrt{\frac{SA\log^{2}T}{1-\gamma}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\big(V_{t}(s_{t+1})-V^{\pi_{t+1}}(s_{t+1})\big)}

\displaystyle\quad+\sqrt{\frac{SA\log\frac{T}{\delta}}{1-\gamma}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\big(V^{\star}(s_{t+1})-V^{\pi_{t+1}}(s_{t+1})\big)}+\frac{\left(SA\right)^{5/8}T^{1/8}}{(1-\gamma)^{11/8}}\left(\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}\right)^{9/8}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{(SA)^{1/2}T^{1/4}}{(1-\gamma)^{5/4}}\left(\log\frac{T}{\delta}\right)^{3/4}+\frac{SA}{1-\gamma}\log\frac{T}{\delta}

\displaystyle\overset{(\mathrm{i})}{\lesssim}\frac{SA}{1-\gamma}\log T+\frac{(SA)^{3/4}T^{1/4}\log^{5/4}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{5/4}}+\sqrt{\frac{SA\log^{2}T}{1-\gamma}\sum_{t=2}^{T}\big(V_{t-1}(s_{t})-V^{\pi_{t}}(s_{t})\big)}

\displaystyle\quad+\sqrt{\frac{SA\log\frac{T}{\delta}}{1-\gamma}\sum_{t=2}^{T}\big(V^{\star}(s_{t})-V^{\pi_{t}}(s_{t})\big)}+\frac{\left(SA\right)^{5/8}T^{1/8}}{(1-\gamma)^{11/8}}\left(\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}\right)^{9/8}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{(SA)^{1/2}T^{1/4}}{(1-\gamma)^{5/4}}\left(\log\frac{T}{\delta}\right)^{3/4}+\frac{SA}{1-\gamma}\log\frac{T}{\delta}

\displaystyle\overset{(\mathrm{ii})}{\leq}\frac{SA}{1-\gamma}\log T+\frac{(SA)^{3/4}T^{1/4}\log^{5/4}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{5/4}}+\sqrt{\frac{SA\log^{2}T}{1-\gamma}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\big(V_{t-1}(s_{t})-V^{\pi_{t}}(s_{t})\big)}

\displaystyle\quad+\sqrt{\frac{SA\log\frac{T}{\delta}}{1-\gamma}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\big(V^{\star}(s_{t})-V^{\pi_{t}}(s_{t})\big)}+\frac{\left(SA\right)^{5/8}T^{1/8}}{(1-\gamma)^{11/8}}\left(\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}\right)^{9/8}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{(SA)^{1/2}T^{1/4}}{(1-\gamma)^{5/4}}\left(\log\frac{T}{\delta}\right)^{3/4}+\frac{SA}{1-\gamma}\log\frac{T}{\delta}

\displaystyle\overset{(\mathrm{iii})}{\lesssim}\sqrt{\frac{SAT}{1-\gamma}\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}}+\frac{SA\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}+\frac{SA}{(1-\gamma)^{5/2}}\log^{2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}+\frac{(SA)^{1/2}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\log\frac{T}{\delta}+\frac{SA}{1-\gamma}\log\frac{T}{\delta}

\displaystyle\quad+\sqrt{\frac{SA\log^{2}T}{1-\gamma}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\big(V_{t}(s_{t+1})-V^{\pi_{t+1}}(s_{t+1})\big)}+\sqrt{\frac{SA\log\frac{T}{\delta}}{1-\gamma}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\big(V^{\star}(s_{t+1})-V^{\pi_{t+1}}(s_{t+1})\big)}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{SAT}{1-\gamma}\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}}+\frac{SA\log^{2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{5/2}}+\sqrt{\frac{SA\log^{2}T}{1-\gamma}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\big(V_{t}(s_{t+1})-V^{\pi_{t+1}}(s_{t+1})\big)}

\displaystyle\quad+\sqrt{\frac{SA\log\frac{T}{\delta}}{1-\gamma}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\big(V^{\star}(s_{t+1})-V^{\pi_{t+1}}(s_{t+1})\big)},

(127)

in which (i) is by upper-bounding
V_{T}(s_{T+1})-V^{\pi_{T+1}}(s_{T+1})
and
V^{\star}(s_{T+1})-V^{\pi_{T+1}}(s_{T+1})
with the addition of
\frac{2}{1-\gamma}
; (ii) is due to
V_{0}(s_{1})-V^{\pi_{1}}(s_{1})\geq 0
and
V^{\star}(s_{1})-V^{\pi_{1}}(s_{1})\geq 0
, which can be obtained from the fact
V_{0}(s)=\frac{1}{1-\gamma}
and
V^{\star}(s)\geq V^{\pi_{1}}(s)
for all
s\in{\mathcal{S}}
; (iii) is by the basic inequality
2ab\leq a^{2}+b^{2}
.

F.1
Bounding
\zeta_{t,1}

First, we define some quantities to streamline our analysis.

For every
(s,a)\in{\mathcal{S}}\times\mathcal{A}
, let
\tau_{i}(s,a)
denote the step index that
\theta(s,a)>\frac{1}{1-\gamma}
for the
i
-th time, that is, Line
1
in Algorithm
1
(equivalently, Line
3
in Algorithm
3
) is triggered for the
i
-th time. (Note that it is the index of the step in which
\theta(s,a)>\frac{1}{1-\gamma}
occurs and not the time index of the
\theta
that gets updated in this step. The updated
\theta
is
\theta_{t+1}
and the update happens after Line
3
.) Mathematically, this is

\tau_{i}(s,a):=\min\left\{t>\tau_{i-1}(s,a)~:~\theta_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})+Q_{M_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}(s_{t},a_{t})-Q_{t+1}(s_{t},a_{t})>\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\right\}

(128)

with
\tau_{0}(s,a)=0
.

For every
(s,a)\in{\mathcal{S}}\times\mathcal{A}
, let
K_{t}(s,a)
be the total number of times such that
\theta(s,a)>\frac{1}{1-\gamma}
until the end of
t
-th step. Mathematically, this is

K_{t}(s,a):=\max\left\{i~:~\tau_{i}(s,a)\leq t\right\}.

(129)

For notational simplicity, we abbreviate
\tau_{i}(s,a)
as
\tau_{i}
,
K_{t}(s,a)
as
K_{t}
and
N_{t}(s,a)
as
N_{t}
when the context is clear.

We can express
\zeta_{t,1}
with
K_{t}(s,a)
after the following manipulation:

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\zeta_{t,1}

\displaystyle=\sum_{t=1}^{T}\big(V_{t-1}(s_{t})-Q_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})\big)

\displaystyle\overset{(\mathrm{i})}{\leq}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\big(Q_{Z_{t-1}}(s_{t},a_{t})-Q_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})\big)

\displaystyle\overset{(\mathrm{ii})}{=}\sum_{s,a}\sum_{i=0}^{K_{T}(s,a)}\sum_{n=N_{\tau_{i}}}^{N_{\tau_{i+1}}-1}\left(Q_{Z_{t_{n}-1}}(s,a)-Q_{t_{n}}(s,a)\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{s,a}\sum_{i=0}^{K_{T}(s,a)}\sum_{n=N_{\tau_{i}}}^{N_{\tau_{i+1}}-1}\left(Q_{Z_{t_{n}-1}}(s,a)-Q_{Z_{t_{n}+1}}(s,a)+Q_{Z_{t_{n}+1}}(s,a)-Q_{t_{n}}(s,a)\right)

\displaystyle\overset{(\mathrm{iii})}{\leq}\sum_{s,a}\sum_{i=0}^{K_{T}(s,a)}\frac{3}{1-\gamma}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\sum_{s,a}K_{T}(s,a).

(130)

In the inequalities above, (i) is because
V_{t-1}(s_{t})\leq V_{Z_{t-1}}(s_{t})=Q_{Z_{t-1}}(s_{t},a_{t})
by the monotonicity and
a_{t}
’s selection rule in Line
3
of Algorithm
3
. (ii) is because we can partition the overall length-
T
trajectory into
K_{T}(s,a)
intervals for each
(s,a)
-pair. (iii) is obtained by combining (
131
) and (
132
), which are bounds on the two halves of the quantity of interest respectively. Since
Q_{Z_{t_{n}-1}}(s,a)\neq Q_{Z_{t_{n}+1}}(s,a)
at most twice in each interval
N_{\tau_{i}}\leq n\leq N_{\tau_{i+1}}-1
, we can use the fact
\left\lVert Q_{Z_{t_{n}-1}}-Q_{Z_{t_{n}+1}}\right\rVert_{\infty}\leq\frac{1}{1-\gamma}
at these two points and have

\displaystyle\quad\ \sum_{s,a}\sum_{i=0}^{K_{T}(s,a)}\sum_{n=N_{\tau_{i}}}^{N_{\tau_{i+1}}-1}\left(Q_{Z_{t_{n}-1}}(s,a)-Q_{Z_{t_{n}+1}}(s,a)\right)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{s,a}\sum_{i=0}^{K_{T}(s,a)}\frac{2}{1-\gamma}.

(131)

On the other hand, the condition in Line
3
of Algorithm
3
tells us that

\displaystyle\quad\ \sum_{s,a}\sum_{i=0}^{K_{T}(s,a)}\sum_{n=N_{\tau_{i}}}^{N_{\tau_{i+1}}-1}\left(Q_{Z_{t_{n}+1}}(s,a)-Q_{t_{n}}(s,a)\right)

\displaystyle\overset{(\mathrm{a})}{\leq}\sum_{s,a}\sum_{i=0}^{K_{T}(s,a)}\sum_{n=N_{\tau_{i}}}^{N_{\tau_{i+1}}-1}\left(Q_{\tau_{i}+1}(s,a)-Q_{t_{n}}(s,a)\right)

\displaystyle\overset{(\mathrm{b})}{\leq}\sum_{s,a}\sum_{i=0}^{K_{T}(s,a)}\sum_{n=N_{\tau_{i}}}^{N_{\tau_{i+1}}-1}\left(Q_{\tau_{i}+1}(s,a)-Q_{t_{n}+1}(s,a)\right)

\displaystyle\overset{(\mathrm{c})}{\leq}\sum_{s,a}\sum_{i=0}^{K_{T}(s,a)}\frac{1}{1-\gamma},

(132)

in which (a) is because
Z
is updated when Line
3
of Algorithm
3
is triggered by any
(s,a)
-pair, which is more often than
\tau_{i}
that depends on a specific
(s,a)
-pair. (b) is by the monotonicity of
Q_{t}
. (c) is due to
\theta_{\tau_{i+1}}\leq\frac{1}{1-\gamma}
. This is because the condition in Line
3
of Algorithm
3
is only triggered after the addition of
Q_{M_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}(s_{t},a_{t})-Q_{t+1}(s_{t},a_{t})
to
\theta_{t}
when
t=\tau_{i+1}
.

In the following, we switch our focus to bounding
\sum_{s,a}K_{T}(s,a)
. Let us define a new quantity

\rho_{i}(s,a):=(1-\gamma)\left(Q_{\tau_{i}(s,a)+1}(s,a)-Q^{\star}(s,a)\right).

(133)

We can observe

\rho_{i}(s,a)\geq\rho_{i+1}(s,a)

(134)

by the monotonicity of
Q_{t}
. We can obtain another property about
\rho_{i}(s,a)
, which will be used in later analysis:

\displaystyle\quad\left(\rho_{i}(s,a)-\rho_{i+1}(s,a)\right)\left(N_{\tau_{i+1}}-N_{\tau_{i}}\right)

\displaystyle=(1-\gamma)\left(Q_{\tau_{i}+1}(s,a)-Q_{\tau_{i+1}+1}(s,a)\right)\left(N_{\tau_{i+1}}-N_{\tau_{i}}\right)

\displaystyle\geq 1,

(135)

in which the inequality is obtained via the following logic:
\theta_{t}(s,a)+Q_{M_{t}(s,a)}(s,a)-Q_{t+1}(s,a)
in the condition Line
3
of Algorithm
3
tells us
\theta_{\tau_{i+1}}(s,a)+Q_{M_{\tau_{i+1}}(s,a)}(s,a)-Q_{\tau_{i+1}+1}(s,a)\geq\frac{1}{1-\gamma}
. This quantity can be viewed as a summation of
(N_{\tau_{i+1}}-N_{\tau_{i}})
non-negative summands in the form of
Q_{M_{t}(s,a)}(s,a)-Q_{t+1}(s,a)
, with
t
being every step index when
(s,a)
is visited between step
\tau_{i}+1
and step
\tau_{i+1}
.
Q_{M_{\tau_{i}}(s,a)}(s,a)-Q_{\tau_{i+1}+1}(s,a)=Q_{\tau_{i}+1}(s,a)-Q_{\tau_{i+1}+1}(s,a)
is the largest among these summands. The product of the largest summand and the total number of summands is larger than the summation itself.

To continue our analysis, we introduce a lemma that provided an upper bound on
Q_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})-Q^{\star}(s_{t},a_{t})
, whose proof is deferred to Appendix
F.1.1
. Notice that Lemma
9
is just a looser version of Lemma
4
. The reason that we introduce Lemma
9
instead of invoking Lemma
4
is that a looser bound suffices here, and the proof of Lemma
4
actually relies on Lemma
5
.

Lemma 9
.

Fix
\delta\in(0,1)
. Suppose that
c_{b}
is chosen to be a sufficiently large universal constant in Algorithm
1
. Then with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}Q_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})-Q^{\star}(s_{t},a_{t})\leq\frac{\gamma(3-\gamma)}{2}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(V_{t}(s_{t+1})-V^{\pi_{t+1}}(s_{t+1})\right)+8c_{b}\sqrt{\frac{SAT}{(1-\gamma)^{3}}\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}}+\frac{SA}{1-\gamma}.

(136)

Thus, we can prove

\displaystyle\quad\sum_{s,a}\sum_{i=0}^{K_{T}(s,a)}\frac{\rho_{i+1}(s,a)}{\rho_{i}(s,a)-\rho_{i+1}(s,a)}

\displaystyle\overset{(\mathrm{i})}{\leq}\sum_{s,a}\sum_{i=0}^{K_{T}(s,a)}\rho_{i+1}(s,a)\left(N_{\tau_{i+1}}-N_{\tau_{i}}\right)

\displaystyle\overset{(\mathrm{ii})}{=}(1-\gamma)\sum_{s,a}\sum_{i=0}^{K_{T}(s,a)}\sum_{n=N_{\tau_{i}}}^{N_{\tau_{i+1}}-1}\left(Q_{\tau_{i+1}+1}(s,a)-Q^{\star}(s,a)\right)

\displaystyle\overset{(\mathrm{iii})}{\leq}(1-\gamma)\sum_{s,a}\sum_{i=0}^{K_{T}(s,a)}\sum_{n=N_{\tau_{i}}}^{N_{\tau_{i+1}}-1}\left(Q_{t_{n}}(s,a)-Q^{\star}(s,a)\right)

\displaystyle=(1-\gamma)\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(Q_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})-Q^{\star}(s_{t},a_{t})\right)

\displaystyle\overset{(\mathrm{iv})}{\lesssim}SA+\sqrt{\frac{SAT\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{1-\gamma}}+(1-\gamma)\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(V_{t}(s_{t+1})-V^{\pi_{t+1}}(s_{t+1})\right).

(137)

Above, (i) is due to (
135
). (ii) is by the definition of
\rho_{i+1}(s,a)
in (
133
). (iii) is by the monotonicity of
Q_{t}
. (iv) is by Lemma
9
and the fact
\frac{\gamma(3-\gamma)}{2}\leq 1
.

Finally, we are ready to bound
\sum_{s,a}K_{T}(s,a)
. Define a sufficiently large index set of
\frac{1}{\rho_{i}(s,a)-\rho_{i+1}(s,a)}
that are on the same order. Mathematically, this is

\mathcal{I}(s,a):=\left\{i\leq K_{T}(s,a)~:~\frac{1}{\rho_{i}(s,a)-\rho_{i+1}(s,a)}\asymp\kappa(s,a)\right\},

(138)

in which
\kappa(s,a)
satisfies
|\mathcal{I}(s,a)|\gtrsim\frac{K_{T}(s,a)}{\log T}
. Before continuing, let us explain the reason why there exists such a set
\mathcal{I}(s,a)
:

By (
135
), we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\rho_{i}(s,a)-\rho_{i+1}(s,a)}\leq N_{\tau_{i+1}}-N_{\tau_{i}}\leq T.

(139)

Moreover,
\left\lVert Q_{\tau_{i}+1}-Q_{\tau_{i+1}+1}\right\rVert_{\infty}\leq\frac{1}{1-\gamma}
, and this implies

\rho_{i}(s,a)-\rho_{i+1}(s,a)\leq 1.

(140)

Thus, we can conclude

1\leq\frac{1}{\rho_{i}(s,a)-\rho_{i+1}(s,a)}\leq T.

Consider a sequence of sets
\{\mathcal{I}_{m}(s,a)\}_{m=1}^{\lceil\log T\rceil}
, each element of which is defined as

\mathcal{I}_{m}(s,a):=\left\{i\leq K_{T}(s,a)~:~\frac{T}{2^{m}}<\frac{1}{\rho_{i}(s,a)-\rho_{i+1}(s,a)}\leq\frac{T}{2^{m-1}}\right\}.

(141)

By the pigeonhole principle, there exists at least one set in this sequence with cardinality at least
\frac{K_{T}(s,a)}{\log T}
. Note that
\frac{1}{\rho_{i}(s,a)-\rho_{i+1}(s,a)}
’s in the same set are within a factor of
2
.

With such set
\mathcal{I}(s,a)
, we can prove

\displaystyle\quad\sum_{s,a}\sum_{i=0}^{K_{T}(s,a)}\frac{\rho_{i+1}(s,a)}{\rho_{i}(s,a)-\rho_{i+1}(s,a)}

\displaystyle\geq\sum_{s,a}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}(s,a)}\frac{\rho_{i+1}(s,a)}{\rho_{i}(s,a)-\rho_{i+1}(s,a)}

\displaystyle\overset{(\mathrm{i})}{\asymp}\sum_{s,a}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}(s,a)}\kappa(s,a)\rho_{i+1}(s,a)

\displaystyle\overset{(\mathrm{ii})}{=}\sum_{s,a}\sum_{\overset{i_{n}\in\mathcal{I}(s,a)}{i_{1}>i_{2}>\cdots>i_{|\mathcal{I}(s,a)|}}}\kappa(s,a)\rho_{i_{n}+1}(s,a)

\displaystyle\overset{(\mathrm{iii})}{\geq}\sum_{s,a}\sum_{\overset{i_{n}\in\mathcal{I}(s,a)}{i_{1}>i_{2}>\cdots>i_{|\mathcal{I}(s,a)|}}}\left(\kappa(s,a)\rho_{i_{n}}(s,a)-1\right)

\displaystyle\overset{(\mathrm{iv})}{=}\sum_{s,a}\kappa(s,a)\left(\sum_{j=n}^{|\mathcal{I}(s,a)|}\rho_{i_{j}}(s,a)-\rho_{i_{j+1}}(s,a)\right)-\left(\sum_{s,a}\sum_{\overset{i_{n}\in\mathcal{I}(s,a)}{i_{1}>i_{2}>\cdots>i_{|\mathcal{I}(s,a)|}}}1\right)

\displaystyle\overset{(\mathrm{v})}{\geq}\sum_{s,a}\kappa(s,a)\left(\sum_{j=n}^{|\mathcal{I}(s,a)|}\rho_{i_{j}}(s,a)-\rho_{i_{j}+1}(s,a)\right)-\left(\sum_{s,a}\sum_{\overset{i_{n}\in\mathcal{I}(s,a)}{i_{1}>i_{2}>\cdots>i_{|\mathcal{I}(s,a)|}}}1\right)

\displaystyle\overset{(\mathrm{vi})}{\asymp}\sum_{s,a}\sum_{\overset{i_{n}\in\mathcal{I}(s,a)}{i_{1}>i_{2}>\cdots>i_{|\mathcal{I}(s,a)|}}}\left(\kappa(s,a)\frac{n}{\kappa(s,a)}-1\right)

\displaystyle\gtrsim\sum_{s,a}\sum_{i=1}^{|\mathcal{I}(s,a)|-1}i

\displaystyle\geq\sum_{s,a}\big(\frac{1}{4}|\mathcal{I}(s,a)|^{2}-\frac{1}{4}\big)

\displaystyle\asymp\sum_{s,a}\max\left\{|\mathcal{I}(s,a)|^{2}-1,0\right\}.

(142)

Above, (i) is by the definition of
\mathcal{I}(s,a)
in (
138
). In (ii), we order the elements in
\mathcal{I}(s,a)
descendingly and denote the
n
-th largest element with
i_{n}
. (iii) is due to (
140
). In (iv), We can let
\rho_{|\mathcal{I}(s,a)|+1}(s,a)=0
. (v) is due to the monotonicity of
Q_{t}
. (vi) is by the definition of
\mathcal{I}(s,a)
.

By some algebra and (
137
) and (
142
), we can conclude

\displaystyle\quad\ \sum_{s,a}\big(|\mathcal{I}(s,a)|-1\big)

\displaystyle\overset{(\mathrm{i})}{\leq}\sqrt{SA\sum_{s,a}\big(|\mathcal{I}(s,a)|-1\big)^{2}}

\displaystyle\overset{(\mathrm{ii})}{\lesssim}\sqrt{SA\sum_{s,a}\max\left\{|\mathcal{I}(s,a)|^{2}-1,0\right\}}

\displaystyle\overset{(\mathrm{iii})}{\lesssim}\sqrt{SA}\cdot\sqrt{SA+\sqrt{\frac{SAT\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{1-\gamma}}+(1-\gamma)\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(V_{t}(s_{t+1})-V^{\pi_{t+1}}(s_{t+1})\right)}

\displaystyle\overset{(\mathrm{iv})}{\leq}SA+\frac{(SA)^{3/4}T^{1/4}\log^{1/4}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{1/4}}+\sqrt{(1-\gamma)SA\sum_{t=1}^{T}\big(V_{t}(s_{t+1})-V^{\pi_{t+1}}(s_{t+1})\big)}.

(143)

Above, (i) and (ii) are by the fact that
(\sum_{i=1}^{n}a_{i})^{2}\leq n\sum_{i=1}^{n}a_{i}^{2}
. (iii) is obtained by combining (
137
) and (
142
). (iv) is by the algebraic fact
\sqrt{a+b}\leq\sqrt{a}+\sqrt{b}
.

Finally, by the definition in (
138
), we have

\displaystyle\quad\ \sum_{s,a}K_{T}(s,a)\lesssim\sum_{s,a}|\mathcal{I}(s,a)|\log T

\displaystyle\lesssim SA\log T+\frac{(SA)^{3/4}T^{1/4}\log^{5/4}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{1/4}}+\log T\sqrt{(1-\gamma)SA\sum_{t=1}^{T}\big(V_{t}(s_{t+1})-V^{\pi_{t+1}}(s_{t+1})\big)},

(144)

and by (
130
), we can obtain the desired final bound

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\zeta_{t,1}\lesssim\frac{SA}{1-\gamma}\log T+\frac{(SA)^{3/4}T^{1/4}\log^{5/4}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{5/4}}+\sqrt{\frac{SA\log^{2}T}{1-\gamma}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\big(V_{t}(s_{t+1})-V^{\pi_{t+1}}(s_{t+1})\big)}.

(145)

F.1.1
Proof of Lemma
9

Proof.

To prove this lemma, it suffices to find an upper bound on
Q^{\mathrm{UCB}}_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})-Q^{\star}(s_{t},a_{t})
. Let us again adopt the abbreviation
N_{t}:=N_{t}(s,a)
when
(s,a)
is clear from the context. By (
E
) and (
68
), for any
(s,a)\in{\mathcal{S}}\times\mathcal{A}
,

\displaystyle\quad\ Q^{\mathrm{UCB}}_{t}(s,a)-Q^{\star}(s,a)

\displaystyle=\eta_{0}^{N_{t-1}}\left(Q^{\mathrm{UCB}}_{1}(s,a)-Q^{\star}(s,a)\right)+\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t-1}}\eta_{n}^{N_{t-1}}\left(\gamma\left(V^{t_{n}}(s_{t_{n}+1})-V^{\star}(s_{t_{n}+1})\right)+\gamma\left(P_{t_{n}}-P_{s,a}\right)V^{\star}+b_{n}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\eta_{0}^{N_{t-1}}}{1-\gamma}+\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t-1}}\eta_{n}^{N_{t-1}}\Big(\gamma\big(V^{t_{n}}(s_{t_{n}+1})-V^{\star}(s_{t_{n}+1})\big)+2b_{n}\Big)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\eta_{0}^{N_{t-1}}}{1-\gamma}+4c_{b}\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{3}N_{t-1}}}+\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t-1}}\eta_{n}^{N_{t-1}}\gamma\left(V^{t_{n}}(s_{t_{n}+1})-V^{\star}(s_{t_{n}+1})\right),

(146)

in which the last inequality is because
\sum_{n=1}^{N}\frac{\eta_{n}^{N}}{\sqrt{N}}\leq\frac{2}{\sqrt{N}}
by (
31
) in Lemma
6
.

Now, we are ready to work towards the desired upper bound:

\displaystyle\quad\ \sum_{t=1}^{T}Q_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})-Q^{\star}(s_{t},a_{t})

\displaystyle\overset{(\mathrm{i})}{\leq}\sum_{t=1}^{T}Q^{\mathrm{UCB}}_{t}(s,a)-Q^{\star}(s,a)

\displaystyle\overset{(\mathrm{ii})}{\leq}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\frac{\eta_{0}^{N_{t-1}(s_{t},a_{t})}}{1-\gamma}+4c_{b}\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{3}N_{t-1}(s_{t},a_{t})}}+\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t-1}(s_{t},a_{t})}\eta_{n}^{N_{t-1}(s_{t},a_{t})}\gamma\left(V^{t_{n}}(s_{t_{n}+1})-V^{\star}(s_{t_{n}+1})\right)

(147)

\displaystyle\overset{(\mathrm{iii})}{\leq}\frac{SA}{1-\gamma}+8c_{b}\sqrt{\frac{SAT\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{3}}}+\frac{\gamma(3-\gamma)}{2}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\big(V_{t}(s_{t+1})-V^{\star}(s_{t+1})\big).

(148)

In the inequalities above, (i) is because for any
(s,a)\in{\mathcal{S}}\times\mathcal{A}
,
Q^{\mathrm{UCB}}_{t}(s,a)\geq Q_{t}(s,a)
by Line
3
of Algorithm
3
. (ii) is by (
146
). (iii) is obtained by the upper bounds on each of the three terms in (
147
). These three terms can be bounded as follows:

The first term in (
147
) can be bounded with
\frac{SA}{1-\gamma}
due to (
29
) and the fact that for each
(s,a)\in{\mathcal{S}}\times\mathcal{A}
,
N_{t-1}(s,a)=0
for only one instance of
t
and will become a positive integer thereafter.

For the second term in (
147
), since
\sum_{(s,a)}N_{T-1}(s,a)\leq T
, we have

\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sqrt{\frac{1}{N_{t-1}(s_{t},a_{t})}}\leq\sum_{(s,a)}\sum_{n=1}^{N_{T-1}(s,a)}\sqrt{\frac{1}{n}}\leq\sum_{(s,a)}2\sqrt{N_{T-1}(s,a)}\leq 2\sqrt{SAT}.

Lastly, for the third term in (
147
), we have

\displaystyle\quad\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t-1}(s_{t},a_{t})}\eta_{n}^{N_{t-1}(s_{t},a_{t})}\gamma\left(V_{t_{n}(s_{t},a_{t})}(s_{t_{n}(s_{t},a_{t})+1})-V^{\star}(s_{t_{n}(s_{t},a_{t})+1})\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{(s,a)}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{T-1}(s,a)}\sum_{n=1}^{k}\eta_{n}^{k}\gamma\left(V_{t_{n}(s,a)}(s_{t_{n}(s,a)+1})-V^{\star}(s_{t_{n}(s,a)+1})\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{(s,a)}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{T-1}(s,a)}\sum_{n=k}^{N_{T-1}(s,a)}\eta_{k}^{n}\gamma\left(V_{t_{k}(s,a)}(s_{t_{k}(s,a)+1})-V^{\star}(s_{t_{k}(s,a)+1})\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{n=N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}^{N_{T-1}(s_{t},a_{t})}\eta_{N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}^{n}\gamma\big(V_{t}(s_{t+1})-V^{\star}(s_{t+1})\big)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\gamma(3-\gamma)}{2}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\big(V_{t}(s_{t+1})-V^{\star}(s_{t+1})\big),

in which the penultimate step is obtained because
t=t_{k}(s,a)
implies
k=N_{t}(s,a)
, and the last step is because
\sum_{n=N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}^{N_{T-1}(s_{t},a_{t})}\eta_{N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}^{n}\leq\frac{3-\gamma}{2}
by Lemma
6
.
∎

F.2
Bounding
\zeta_{t,2}

We will prove (
152
) with Lemma
8
. Set

\displaystyle W_{i}\coloneqq V^{\star}-V^{\pi_{i}}\qquad\text{and}\qquad u_{i}(s_{i},a_{i})\coloneqq 1.

Since
V^{\mathrm{R}}_{i}(s),V^{\star}(s)\in[0,\frac{1}{1-\gamma}]
, it can be seen that

\displaystyle\left|u_{i}(s_{i},a_{i})\right|=1\eqqcolon C_{\mathrm{u}}\qquad\text{and}\qquad\left\lVert W_{i}\right\rVert_{\infty}\leq\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\eqqcolon C_{\mathrm{w}}.

By Lemma
8
, with probability at least
1-\delta/2
, one has

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\zeta_{t,2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(P_{s_{t},a_{t}}-P_{t}\right)\left(V^{\star}-V^{\pi_{t}}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{C_{\mathrm{u}}^{2}C_{\mathrm{w}}SA\sum_{i=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}_{i-1}\left[P_{i}W_{i}\right]\log\frac{T}{\delta}}+C_{\mathrm{u}}C_{\mathrm{w}}SA\log\frac{T}{\delta}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\frac{SA}{1-\gamma}\sum_{i=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}_{i-1}\left[P_{i}\left(V^{\star}-V^{\pi_{i}}\right)\right]\log\frac{T}{\delta}}+\frac{SA}{1-\gamma}\log\frac{T}{\delta}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\frac{SA}{1-\gamma}\left\{\sum_{t=1}^{T}P_{s_{t},a_{t}}\left(V^{\star}-V^{\pi_{t}}\right)\right\}\log\frac{T}{\delta}}+\frac{SA}{1-\gamma}\log\frac{T}{\delta},

(149)

in which the last step is because
V^{\star}-V^{\pi_{t}}
is deterministic when conditioned on the
(i-1)
-th step.

In order to obtain the desired bound, more manipulation is needed on
\sum_{t=1}^{T}P_{s_{t},a_{t}}\big(V^{\star}-V^{\pi_{t}}\big)
in (
149
). Towards this end, we first invoke the Azuma-Hoeffding inequality (Theorem
2
), which leads to

\displaystyle\left|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(P_{t}-P_{s_{t},a_{t}}\right)\left(V^{\star}-V^{\pi_{t}}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{T}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\log\frac{1}{\delta}},

(150)

with probability at least
1-\delta/2
, because
V^{\star}-V^{\pi_{t}}
is deterministic when conditioned on the
(i-1)
-th step and
\left\lVert V^{\star}-V^{\pi_{t}}\right\rVert_{\infty}\leq\frac{1}{1-\gamma}
.

With (
150
) established, we can conclude

\displaystyle\quad\ \sum_{t=1}^{T}P_{s_{t},a_{t}}\left(V^{\star}-V^{\pi_{t}}\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{t=1}^{T}P_{t}\left(V^{\star}-V^{\pi_{t}}\right)+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(P_{s_{t},a_{t}}-P_{t}\right)\left(V^{\star}-V^{\pi_{t}}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{t=1}^{T}\big(V^{\star}(s_{t+1})-V^{\pi_{t}}(s_{t+1})\big)+\sqrt{\frac{T}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\log\frac{1}{\delta}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{t=1}^{T}\big(V^{\star}(s_{t+1})-V^{\pi_{t+1}}(s_{t+1})\big)+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\big(V^{\pi_{t+1}}(s_{t+1})-V^{\pi_{t}}(s_{t+1})\big)+\sqrt{\frac{T}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\log\frac{1}{\delta}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{t=1}^{T}\big(V^{\star}(s_{t+1})-V^{\pi_{t+1}}(s_{t+1})\big)+\left(SAT\right)^{1/4}\left(\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}\right)^{5/4}\left(\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\right)^{7/4}+\sqrt{\frac{T}{(1-\gamma)^{3}}\log\frac{T}{\delta}},

(151)

with probability at least
1-\delta/2
. The last step is obtained by applying (
124
) and the fact
\left\lVert V_{t}-V^{\pi_{t+1}}\right\rVert_{\infty}\leq\frac{1}{1-\gamma}
.

Finally, substituting (
151
) into (
149
) gives the desired bound

\displaystyle\quad\ \sum_{t=1}^{T}\zeta_{t,2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{SA}{1-\gamma}\left\{\sum_{t=1}^{T}P_{s_{t},a_{t}}\left(V^{\star}-V^{\pi_{t}}\right)\right\}\log\frac{T}{\delta}}+\frac{SA}{1-\gamma}\log\frac{T}{\delta}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{SA}{1-\gamma}\left\{\sum_{t=1}^{T}\big(V^{\star}(s_{t+1})-V^{\pi_{t+1}}(s_{t+1})\big)+\frac{\left(SAT\right)^{1/4}}{(1-\gamma)^{7/4}}\left(\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}\right)^{5/4}+\sqrt{\frac{T}{(1-\gamma)^{3}}\log\frac{T}{\delta}}\right\}\log\frac{T}{\delta}}+\frac{SA}{1-\gamma}\log\frac{T}{\delta}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\frac{SA\log\frac{T}{\delta}}{1-\gamma}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\big(V^{\star}(s_{t+1})-V^{\pi_{t+1}}(s_{t+1})\big)}+\frac{\left(SA\right)^{5/8}T^{1/8}}{(1-\gamma)^{11/8}}\left(\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}\right)^{9/8}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\frac{(SA)^{1/2}T^{1/4}}{(1-\gamma)^{5/4}}\left(\log\frac{T}{\delta}\right)^{3/4}+\frac{SA}{1-\gamma}\log\frac{T}{\delta}

(152)

with probability at least
1-\delta
.

F.3
Bounding
\zeta_{t,3}

By the definition of
K_{t}(s,a)
in (
129
), we can write

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\zeta_{t,3}

\displaystyle=\gamma\sum_{t=1}^{T}\big(V^{\pi_{t+1}}(s_{t+1})-V^{\pi_{t}}(s_{t+1})\big)

\displaystyle\leq\gamma\sum_{s,a}\sum_{i=0}^{K_{T}(s,a)}\frac{1}{1-\gamma}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\sum_{s,a}K_{T}(s,a),

(153)

in which the inequality is obtained using the total number of times that policy
\pi_{t}
in Algorithm
3
switches over
T
steps and the fact that
\left\lVert V^{\pi_{t+1}}-V^{\pi_{t}}\right\rVert_{\infty}\leq\frac{1}{1-\gamma}
.

By the upper bound on
\sum_{s,a}K_{T}(s,a)
in (
144
), we can obtain the desired final bound

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\zeta_{t,3}\lesssim\frac{SA}{1-\gamma}\log T+\frac{(SA)^{3/4}T^{1/4}\log^{5/4}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{5/4}}+\sqrt{\frac{SA\log^{2}T}{1-\gamma}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\big(V_{t}(s_{t+1})-V^{\pi_{t+1}}(s_{t+1})\big)}.

(154)

F.4
Crude Regret Bound as a Consequence of Lemma
5

Note that Lemma
5
can give rise to a crude bound on
\mathrm{Regret}(T)
, which we will need in the proof of Theorem
1
. It can be summarized in a lemma as follows:

Lemma 10
.

Choose any
\delta\in(0,1)
. Suppose that
c_{b}
is chosen to be a sufficiently large universal constant in Algorithm
1
. Then there exists some absolute constant
C_{0}>0
such that Algorithm
1
achieves

\mathrm{Regret}(T)\leq\sum_{t=1}^{T}\big(V_{t-1}(s_{t})-V^{\pi_{t}}(s_{t})\big)\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{SAT\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{5}}}+\frac{SA\log^{2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{7/2}}

(155)

with probability at least
1-\delta
.

Proof of Lemma
10
.

By the optimistic property of our value function estimates in Lemma
1
, we can

\mathrm{Regret}(T)=\sum_{t=1}^{T}\big(V^{\star}(s_{t})-V^{\pi_{t}}(s_{t})\big)\leq\sum_{t=1}^{T}\big(V_{t-1}(s_{t})-V^{\pi_{t}}(s_{t})\big).

In the inequality, since
V_{0}(s)
is undefined in our algorithm, we can simply let
V_{0}(s)=\frac{1}{1-\gamma}
for all
s\in{\mathcal{S}}
.

We can follow the regret decomposition in (
56
) and obtain the following inequality:

\displaystyle V_{t-1}(s_{t})-V^{\pi_{t}}(s_{t})\leq\frac{\gamma(3-\gamma)}{2}\left(V^{\star}(s_{t+1})-V^{\pi_{t+1}}(s_{t+1})\right)+Q_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})-Q^{\star}(s_{t},a_{t})+\zeta_{t},

(156)

in which
\zeta_{t}
has the same definition as (
57
):

\zeta_{t}:=V_{t-1}(s_{t})-Q_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})+\gamma\left(P_{s_{t},a_{t}}-P_{t}\right)\left(V^{\star}-V^{\pi_{t}}\right)+\gamma\left(V^{\pi_{t+1}}(s_{t+1})-V^{\pi_{t}}(s_{t+1})\right).

Invoking Lemma
9
and rearranging (
56
), we obtain a recursion as follows:

\displaystyle\quad\left(1-\frac{\gamma(3-\gamma)}{2}\right)\sum_{t=1}^{T}\big(V_{t-1}(s_{t})-V^{\pi_{t}}(s_{t})\big)

\displaystyle=\sum_{t=1}^{T}\big(V_{t-1}(s_{t})-V^{\pi_{t}}(s_{t})\big)-\frac{\gamma(3-\gamma)}{2}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\big(V_{t-1}(s_{t})-V^{\pi_{t}}(s_{t})\big)

\displaystyle\overset{(\mathrm{i})}{\leq}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\big(V_{t-1}(s_{t})-V^{\pi_{t}}(s_{t})\big)-\frac{\gamma(3-\gamma)}{2}\sum_{t=2}^{T}\big(V_{t-1}(s_{t})-V^{\pi_{t}}(s_{t})\big)

\displaystyle=\sum_{t=1}^{T}\big(V_{t-1}(s_{t})-V^{\pi_{t}}(s_{t})\big)-\frac{\gamma(3-\gamma)}{2}\sum_{t=1}^{T-1}\big(V_{t}(s_{t+1})-V^{\pi_{t+1}}(s_{t+1})\big)

\displaystyle\overset{(\mathrm{ii})}{\leq}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\frac{\gamma(3-\gamma)}{2}\big(V^{\star}(s_{t+1})-V^{\pi_{t+1}}(s_{t+1})\big)+Q_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})-Q^{\star}(s_{t},a_{t})+\zeta_{t}-\frac{\gamma(3-\gamma)}{2}\sum_{t=1}^{T-1}\big(V_{t}(s_{t+1})-V^{\pi_{t+1}}(s_{t+1})\big)

\displaystyle\overset{(\mathrm{iii})}{\leq}8c_{b}\sqrt{\frac{SAT}{(1-\gamma)^{3}}\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}}+\frac{SA}{1-\gamma}+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\frac{\gamma(3-\gamma)}{2}\big(V_{t}(s_{t+1})-V^{\pi_{t+1}}(s_{t+1})\big)+\zeta_{t}

\displaystyle\qquad-\frac{\gamma(3-\gamma)}{2}\sum_{t=1}^{T-1}\big(V_{t}(s_{t+1})-V^{\pi_{t+1}}(s_{t+1})\big)

\displaystyle=8c_{b}\sqrt{\frac{SAT}{(1-\gamma)^{3}}\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}}+\frac{SA}{1-\gamma}+\frac{\gamma(3-\gamma)}{2}\big(V^{\star}(s_{T+1})-V^{\pi_{T+1}}(s_{T+1})\big)+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\zeta_{t}

\displaystyle\overset{(\mathrm{iv})}{\leq}8c_{b}\sqrt{\frac{SAT}{(1-\gamma)^{3}}\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}}+\frac{SA}{1-\gamma}+\frac{3}{2(1-\gamma)}+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\zeta_{t}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{SAT}{(1-\gamma)^{3}}\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}}+\frac{SA}{1-\gamma}+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\zeta_{t}.

(157)

Above, (i) is because by the optimistic property in Lemma
4
,
V_{t-1}(s_{t})\geq V^{\star}(s_{t})\geq V^{\pi_{t}}(s_{t})\geq 0
and thus
V_{t-1}(s_{t})-V^{\pi_{t}}(s_{t})\geq 0
. (ii) is obtained by replacing
\left(V_{t-1}(s_{t})-V^{\pi_{t}}(s_{t})\right)
with (
156
). (iii) is obtained by invoking Lemma
9
to replace
\sum_{t=1}^{T}Q_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})-Q^{\star}(s_{t},a_{t})
. (iv) is due to
V^{\star}(s_{T+1})-V^{\pi_{T+1}}(s_{T+1})\leq\frac{1}{1-\gamma}
and
\gamma\in(0,1)
.

Note
0<1-\frac{\gamma(3-\gamma)}{2}<1
since
\gamma\in(0,1)
. Dividing (
58
) by
1-\frac{\gamma(3-\gamma)}{2}
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\big(V_{t-1}(s_{t})-V^{\pi_{t}}(s_{t})\big)\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{SAT}{(1-\gamma)^{5}}\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}}+\frac{SA}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}+\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\zeta_{t},

(158)

in which the additional
\frac{1}{1-\gamma}
factor is due to the fact
1-\frac{\gamma(3-\gamma)}{2}\geq\frac{1-\gamma}{2}
.

After applying Lemma
5
, (
158
) can be written as

\displaystyle\quad\ \sum_{t=1}^{T}\big(V_{t-1}(s_{t})-V^{\pi_{t}}(s_{t})\big)

\displaystyle\leq C_{5}\Bigg(\sqrt{\frac{SAT\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{5}}}+\frac{SA\log^{2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{7/2}}+\sqrt{\frac{\log^{2}T}{(1-\gamma)^{3}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\big(V_{t-1}(s_{t})-V^{\pi_{t}}(s_{t})\big)}

\displaystyle\qquad+\sqrt{\frac{SA\log\frac{T}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{3}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\big(V^{\star}(s_{t})-V^{\pi_{t}}(s_{t})\big)}\Bigg)

\displaystyle\leq C_{5}\Bigg(\sqrt{\frac{SAT\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{5}}}+\frac{SA\log^{2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{7/2}}+\sqrt{\frac{\log^{2}T}{(1-\gamma)^{3}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\big(V_{t-1}(s_{t})-V^{\pi_{t}}(s_{t})\big)}

\displaystyle\qquad+\sqrt{\frac{SA\log\frac{T}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{3}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\big(V_{t-1}(s_{t})-V^{\pi_{t}}(s_{t})\big)}\Bigg)

(159)

for some absolute constant
C_{5}>0
. The last inequality is by the optimism of
V_{t-1}
by Lemma
1
.

Finally, we can invoke Proposition
1
on (
159
) by letting

\displaystyle x

\displaystyle:=C_{5}\left(\frac{\log T}{(1-\gamma)^{3/2}}+\sqrt{\frac{SA\log\frac{T}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{3}}}\right),

\displaystyle y

\displaystyle:=C_{5}\left(\sqrt{\frac{SAT\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{5}}}+\frac{SA\log^{2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{7/2}}\right),

\displaystyle z

\displaystyle:=\sum_{t=1}^{T}\big(V_{t-1}(s_{t})-V^{\pi_{t}}(s_{t})\big)

in (
35
). (
36
) would give

\displaystyle\quad\ \sum_{t=1}^{T}\big(V_{t-1}(s_{t})-V^{\pi_{t}}(s_{t})\big)

\displaystyle\leq 2C_{5}\sqrt{\frac{SAT\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{5}}}+2(C_{5}+C_{5}^{2})\frac{SA\log^{2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{7/2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{SAT\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{5}}}+\frac{SA\log^{2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{7/2}},

(160)

which is as claimed in Lemma
10
.
∎

Appendix G
Proof of Lemma
2

G.1
Proof of Inequality (
14
)

Before proving the pessimism in
V_{t}^{\mathrm{LCB}}
