, we focus on proving

Q^{\mathrm{LCB}}_{t}(s,a)\leq Q^{\star}(s,a)\qquad\text{for all }(s,a,t)\in{\mathcal{S}}\times\mathcal{A}\times[T].

(161)

Let us proceed by induction. We start with the base case. By our initialization, we have

0=Q^{\mathrm{LCB}}_{1}(s,a)\leq Q^{\star}(s,a);

hence, (
161
) trivially holds.

For the induction step, let us assume the inductive hypothesis that (
161
) holds all the way up to
t
for all
(s,a)\in{\mathcal{S}}\times\mathcal{A}
. We now show the statement holds for the
(t+1)
-th step as well. Recall that only
Q_{t+1}^{\mathrm{LCB}}(s_{t},a_{t})
is updated at the
t
-th step with all other entries of
Q_{t}^{\mathrm{LCB}}
fixed, so it suffices to check

Q^{\mathrm{LCB}}_{t+1}(s_{t},a_{t})\leq Q^{\star}(s_{t},a_{t}).

(162)

Again, we adopt the shorthand notation
N_{t}:=N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})
and
t_{n}:=t_{n}(s_{t},a_{t})
when it is clear from the context.

Recall
b_{n}:=c_{b}\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{3}n}}
. We rewrite the subroutine
update-q-lcb
() in Line
2
of Algorithm
2
:

Q^{\mathrm{LCB}}_{t+1}(s_{t},a_{t})=Q^{\mathrm{LCB}}_{t_{N}+1}(s_{t},a_{t})=(1-\eta_{N})Q^{\mathrm{LCB}}_{t_{N}}(s_{t},a_{t})+\eta_{N}\left(r(s_{t},a_{t})+\gamma V_{t_{N}}^{\mathrm{LCB}}(s_{t_{N}+1})-b_{N}\right).

This recursive relation can be expanded as

Q^{\mathrm{LCB}}_{t+1}(s_{t},a_{t})=\eta_{0}^{N}Q^{\mathrm{LCB}}_{1}(s_{t},a_{t})+\sum_{n=1}^{N}\eta_{n}^{N}\big(r(s_{t},a_{t})+\gamma V_{t_{n}}^{\mathrm{LCB}}(s_{t_{n}+1})-b_{n}\big),

(163)

in which
\eta_{0}^{N}
and
\eta_{n}^{N}
are defined in Lemma
6
.

Using (
163
), we have

\displaystyle\quad\ Q^{\mathrm{LCB}}_{t+1}(s_{t},a_{t})-Q^{\star}(s_{t},a_{t})

\displaystyle=\eta_{0}^{N}\left(Q^{\mathrm{LCB}}_{1}(s_{t},a_{t})-Q^{\star}(s_{t},a_{t})\right)+\sum_{n=1}^{N}\eta_{n}^{N}\left(r(s_{t},a_{t})+\gamma V_{t_{n}}^{\mathrm{LCB}}(s_{t_{n}+1})-b_{n}-Q^{\star}(s_{t},a_{t})\right)

\displaystyle=\eta_{0}^{N}\left(Q^{\mathrm{LCB}}_{1}(s_{t},a_{t})-Q^{\star}(s_{t},a_{t})\right)+\sum_{n=1}^{N}\eta_{n}^{N}\Big(\gamma\left(V_{t_{n}}^{\mathrm{LCB}}(s_{t_{n}+1})-V^{\star}(s_{t_{n}+1})\right)+\gamma\left(P_{t_{n}}-P_{s_{t},a_{t}}\right)V^{\star}-b_{n}\Big),

(164)

where the last step is by
Q^{\star}(s,a)=r(s,a)+\gamma P_{s,a}V^{\star}
and
V^{\star}(s_{t_{n}+1})=P_{t_{n}}V^{\star}
.

Recall in the proof for optimism (lemma
1
), by (
68
), we have with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\left|\sum_{n=1}^{N}\eta_{n}^{N}\gamma\left(P_{t_{n}}-P_{s_{t},a_{t}}\right)V^{\star}\right|\leq c_{b}\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{3}N}}\sum_{n=1}^{N}\eta_{n}^{N}\leq\sum_{n=1}^{N}\eta_{n}^{N}b_{n},

for all
(N,s,a)\in[T]\times{\mathcal{S}}\times\mathcal{A}
. We can employ this inequality on (
G.1
) this time and obtain that with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle\quad\ Q^{\mathrm{LCB}}_{t+1}(s_{t},a_{t})-Q^{\star}(s_{t},a_{t})

\displaystyle=\eta_{0}^{N}\left(Q^{\mathrm{LCB}}_{1}(s_{t},a_{t})-Q^{\star}(s_{t},a_{t})\right)+\sum_{n=1}^{N}\eta_{n}^{N}\Big(\gamma\left(V_{t_{n}}^{\mathrm{LCB}}(s_{t_{n}+1})-V^{\star}(s_{t_{n}+1})\right)+\gamma\left(P_{t_{n}}-P_{s_{t},a_{t}}\right)V^{\star}-b_{n}\Big)

\displaystyle\leq\eta_{0}^{N}\left(Q^{\mathrm{LCB}}_{1}(s_{t},a_{t})-Q^{\star}(s_{t},a_{t})\right)+\gamma\sum_{n=1}^{N}\eta_{n}^{N}\left(V_{t_{n}}^{\mathrm{LCB}}(s_{t_{n}+1})-V^{\star}(s_{t_{n}+1})\right)

\displaystyle\leq\gamma\sum_{n=1}^{N}\eta_{n}^{N}\left(V_{t_{n}}^{\mathrm{LCB}}(s_{t_{n}+1})-V^{\star}(s_{t_{n}+1})\right),

where the last step is due to our initialization
Q^{\mathrm{UCB}}_{1}(s,a)=0\leq Q^{\star}(s,a)
.

Lastly, the inductive hypothesis gives
V_{t}^{\mathrm{LCB}}(s)\leq V^{\star}(s)
for any
(s,a)\in{\mathcal{S}}\times\mathcal{A}
, so we can conclude

Q^{\mathrm{LCB}}_{t+1}(s,a)-Q^{\star}(s,a)\leq\sum_{n=1}^{N}\eta_{n}^{N}\gamma\left(V_{t_{n}}^{\mathrm{LCB}}(s_{t_{n}+1})-V^{\star}(s_{t_{n}+1})\right)\leq 0.

At this point, we have finished the proof for the induction step and thus the proof for (
161
).

Now, let us prove the pessimism in
V^{\mathrm{LCB}}
. Given the pessimism in
Q^{\mathrm{LCB}}
in (
161
), we have

\displaystyle\max_{a}Q_{t}^{\mathrm{LCB}}(s,a)\leq\max_{a}Q^{\star}(s,a)=V^{\star}(s)\qquad\text{for all }(t,s)\in[T]\times\mathcal{S}.

(165)

On the other hand, the construction of
V_{t}^{\mathrm{LCB}}
in Line
3
of Algorithm
3
can be written as

V_{t}^{\mathrm{LCB}}(s)\leq\max\left\{\max_{j:j\leq t}\max_{a}Q^{\mathrm{LCB}}_{j}(s,a),\ \max_{j:j<t}V_{j}^{\mathrm{LCB}}(s)\right\}.

Combined with the initialization
V_{1}^{\mathrm{LCB}}(s)=0
for all
s\in{\mathcal{S}}
, we can conclude with the desired pessimism of
V^{\mathrm{LCB}}
via a simple induction:

\displaystyle V_{t}^{\mathrm{LCB}}(s)\leq V^{\star}(s)\ \text{for all }(t,s)\in[T]\times\mathcal{S}.

(166)

G.2
Proof of Inequality (
2
)

The proof of (
2
) essentially follows the result of Lemma
11
, which provides an upper bound on any weighted sum of our algorithm’s value estimates.

Lemma 11
.

Assume there exists a constant
c_{b}>0
such that for all
(t,s,a)\in[T]\times{\mathcal{S}}\times\mathcal{A}
, it holds that

\displaystyle 0

\displaystyle\leq Q_{t+1}(s,a)-Q^{\mathrm{LCB}}_{t+1}(s,a)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\eta^{N_{t}(s,a)}_{0}}{1-\gamma}+\gamma\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t}(s,a)}\eta^{N_{t}(s,a)}_{n}\left(V_{t_{n}}(s_{t_{n}+1})-V^{\mathrm{LCB}}_{t_{n}}(s_{t_{n}+1})\right)+4c_{b}\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{3}N_{t}(s,a)}},

(167)

in which we use the abbreviation
t_{n}:=t_{n}(s,a)
.

For every sequence
\{w_{t}\}_{t=1}^{\infty}
such that
0\leq w_{t}\leq w
for all
t
and
\sum_{t=1}^{\infty}w_{t}\leq W
, one has

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}

\displaystyle w_{t}\left(V_{t}(s_{t+1})-V_{t}^{\mathrm{LCB}}(s_{t+1})\right)\leq C_{\mathrm{v},1}\frac{ew\left(SA\right)^{3/4}T^{1/4}}{(1-\gamma)^{9/4}}\left(\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}\right)^{5/4}+C_{\mathrm{v},1}\frac{eSAw}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\log T

\displaystyle\quad+8ec_{b}\sqrt{\frac{SAwW\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{5}}}+C_{\mathrm{v},1}ew\sqrt{\frac{SA\log^{2}T}{(1-\gamma)^{3}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\big(V_{t}(s_{t+1})-V^{\pi_{t+1}}(s_{t+1})\big)}+eW

(168)

for some absolute constant
C_{\mathrm{v},1}>0
.

The proof of Lemma
11
is deferred to Appendix
G.2.1
. We can easily prove (
2
) with Lemma
11
. Let us specify

w_{t}=\mathds{1}\left\{V_{t}(s_{t+1})-V_{t}^{\mathrm{LCB}}(s_{t+1})>3\right\}.

(169)

Clearly, this gives us

\displaystyle 3W=3\sum_{t=1}^{T}w_{t}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{t=1}^{T}w_{t}\left(V_{t}(s_{t+1})-V_{t}^{\mathrm{LCB}}(s_{t+1})\right).

(170)

If we assume (
167
) is true, we can bring (
11
) into the right-hand side of (
170
). We can rearrange and rescale the inequality to obtain

\displaystyle\frac{1}{3-e}(3W-eW)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{3-e}\Bigg(C_{\mathrm{v},1}\frac{ew\left(SA\right)^{3/4}T^{1/4}}{(1-\gamma)^{9/4}}\left(\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}\right)^{5/4}+C_{\mathrm{v},1}\frac{eSAw}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\log T+8ec_{b}\sqrt{\frac{SAwW\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{5}}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad+C_{\mathrm{v},1}ew\sqrt{\frac{SA\log^{2}T}{(1-\gamma)^{3}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\big(V_{t}(s_{t+1})-V^{\pi_{t+1}}(s_{t+1})\big)}\Bigg).

(171)

Invoking Proposition
1
on (
171
) with

\displaystyle x

\displaystyle:=\frac{8e}{3-e}c_{b}\sqrt{\frac{SAw\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{5}}},

\displaystyle y

\displaystyle:=\frac{ew}{3-e}C_{\mathrm{v},1}\Bigg(\frac{\left(SA\right)^{3/4}T^{1/4}}{(1-\gamma)^{9/4}}\left(\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}\right)^{5/4}+\frac{SA}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\log T+\sqrt{\frac{SA\log^{2}T}{(1-\gamma)^{3}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\big(V_{t}(s_{t+1})-V^{\pi_{t+1}}(s_{t+1})\big)}\Bigg),

\displaystyle z

\displaystyle:=\sqrt{W},

we can conclude with the inequality:

\displaystyle\quad\ \sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathds{1}\left\{V_{t}(s_{t+1})-V_{t}^{\mathrm{LCB}}(s_{t+1})>3\right\}\leq W

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{w\left(SA\right)^{3/4}T^{1/4}}{(1-\gamma)^{9/4}}\left(\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}\right)^{5/4}+\frac{SAw}{(1-\gamma)^{5}}\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}+w\sqrt{\frac{SA\log^{2}T}{(1-\gamma)^{3}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\big(V_{t}(s_{t+1})-V^{\pi_{t+1}}(s_{t+1})\big)}

\displaystyle\overset{(\mathrm{i})}{=}\frac{\left(SA\right)^{3/4}T^{1/4}}{(1-\gamma)^{9/4}}\left(\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}\right)^{5/4}+\frac{SA}{(1-\gamma)^{5}}\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}+\sqrt{\frac{SA\log^{2}T}{(1-\gamma)^{3}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\big(V_{t}(s_{t+1})-V^{\pi_{t+1}}(s_{t+1})\big)}

\displaystyle\overset{(\mathrm{ii})}{\lesssim}\frac{\left(SA\right)^{3/4}T^{1/4}}{(1-\gamma)^{9/4}}\left(\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}\right)^{5/4}+\frac{SA}{(1-\gamma)^{5}}\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}+\sqrt{\frac{SA\log^{2}T}{(1-\gamma)^{3}}\sum_{t=2}^{T}\big(V_{t-1}(s_{t})-V^{\pi_{t}}(s_{t})\big)}

\displaystyle\overset{(\mathrm{iii})}{\leq}\frac{\left(SA\right)^{3/4}T^{1/4}}{(1-\gamma)^{9/4}}\left(\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}\right)^{5/4}+\frac{SA}{(1-\gamma)^{5}}\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}+\sqrt{\frac{SA\log^{2}T}{(1-\gamma)^{3}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\big(V_{t-1}(s_{t})-V^{\pi_{t}}(s_{t})\big)},

(172)

in which (i) is because
w=1
by our definition of
w_{t}
in (
169
); (ii) is due to
\left\lVert V_{T}-V^{\pi_{T+1}}\right\rVert_{\infty}\leq\frac{1}{1-\gamma}
; (iii) is due to
V_{0}(s_{1})-V^{\pi_{1}}(s_{1})\geq 0
, which can be obtained from the fact
V_{0}(s)=\frac{1}{1-\gamma}
and
V^{\star}(s)\geq V^{\pi_{1}}(s)
for all
s\in{\mathcal{S}}
.

At this point, we can easily prove (
2
), as (
172
) immediately leads to

\displaystyle\quad\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(V_{t}(s_{t+1})-V_{t}^{\mathrm{LCB}}(s_{t+1})\right)\mathds{1}\left\{V_{t}(s_{t+1})-V_{t}^{\mathrm{LCB}}(s_{t+1})>3\right\}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathds{1}\left\{V_{t}(s_{t+1})-V_{t}^{\mathrm{LCB}}(s_{t+1})>3\right\}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\left(SA\right)^{3/4}T^{1/4}}{(1-\gamma)^{13/4}}\left(\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}\right)^{5/4}+\frac{SA}{(1-\gamma)^{6}}\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}+\sqrt{\frac{SA\log^{2}T}{(1-\gamma)^{5}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\big(V_{t-1}(s_{t})-V^{\pi_{t}}(s_{t})\big)},

(173)

which is the final result in (
2
) that we desire.

As the last step of proving (
2
), we need to return to the justification of (
167
) that allows for our invocation of Lemma
11
earlier. The first inequality in (
167
) is easy to prove, for Lemma
1
and Lemma
2
tells us
Q_{t}(s,a)\geq Q^{\star}(s,a)\geq Q_{t}^{\mathrm{LCB}}(s,a)
for all
(t,s,a)\in[T]\times{\mathcal{S}}\times\mathcal{A}
. Thus, it only remains to prove the second inequality in (
167
), which is an upper bound on
Q_{t+1}(s,a)-Q^{\mathrm{LCB}}_{t+1}(s,a)
.

Let us abbreviate
N_{t}:=N_{t}(s,a)
temporarily. Since the update rule of
Q_{t+1}
in Line
3
of Algorithm
3
implies
Q_{t+1}(s,a)\leq Q^{\mathrm{UCB}}_{t+1}(s,a)
, we can instead focus on finding an upper bound for the following quantity:

\displaystyle Q_{t+1}(s,a)-Q^{\mathrm{LCB}}_{t+1}(s,a)\leq Q^{\mathrm{UCB}}_{t+1}(s,a)-Q^{\mathrm{LCB}}_{t+1}(s,a)=Q^{\mathrm{UCB}}_{t_{N_{t}}+1}(s,a)-Q^{\mathrm{LCB}}_{t_{N_{t}}+1}(s,a).

(174)

Towards this, the update rules of
Q^{\mathrm{UCB}}_{t}
and
Q^{\mathrm{LCB}}_{t}
in Line
2
and
2
of Algorithm
2
allow us to write

\displaystyle\quad\ Q^{\mathrm{UCB}}_{t_{N_{t}}+1}(s,a)-Q^{\mathrm{LCB}}_{t_{N_{t}}+1}(s,a)

\displaystyle=(1-\eta_{N_{t}})Q_{t_{N_{t}}}^{\mathrm{UCB}}(s,a)+\eta_{N_{t}}\left(r(s,a)+\gamma V_{t_{N_{t}}}(s_{t_{N_{t}}+1})+c_{b}\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{3}N_{t}}}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad-(1-\eta_{N_{t}})Q_{t_{N_{t}}}^{\mathrm{LCB}}(s,a)-\eta_{N_{t}}\left(r(s,a)+\gamma V^{\mathrm{LCB}}_{t_{N_{t}}}(s_{t_{N_{t}}+1})-c_{b}\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{3}N_{t}}}\right)

\displaystyle=(1-\eta_{N_{t}})\left(Q_{t_{N_{t}}}^{\mathrm{UCB}}(s,a)-Q_{t_{N_{t}}}^{\mathrm{LCB}}(s,a)\right)+\eta_{N_{t}}\left(\gamma V_{t_{N_{t}}}(s_{t_{N_{t}}+1})-\gamma V^{\mathrm{LCB}}_{t_{N_{t}}}(s_{t_{N_{t}}+1})+2c_{b}\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{3}N_{t}}}\right)

\displaystyle=(1-\eta_{N_{t}})\left(Q_{t_{N_{t}-1}+1}^{\mathrm{UCB}}(s,a)-Q_{t_{N_{t}-1}+1}^{\mathrm{LCB}}(s,a)\right)+\eta_{N_{t}}\left(\gamma V_{t_{N_{t}}}(s_{t_{N_{t}}+1})-\gamma V^{\mathrm{LCB}}_{t_{N_{t}}}(s_{t_{N_{t}}+1})+2c_{b}\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{3}N_{t}}}\right)

(175)

\displaystyle=\eta^{N_{t}}_{0}\left(Q_{1}^{\mathrm{UCB}}(s,a)-Q_{1}^{\mathrm{LCB}}(s,a)\right)+\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t}}\eta^{N_{t}}_{n}\left(\gamma V_{t_{n}}(s_{t_{n}+1})-\gamma V^{\mathrm{LCB}}_{t_{n}}(s_{t_{n}+1})+2c_{b}\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{3}n}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\eta^{N_{t}}_{0}}{1-\gamma}+\gamma\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t}}\eta^{N_{t}}_{n}\left(V_{t_{n}}(s_{t_{n}+1})-V^{\mathrm{LCB}}_{t_{n}}(s_{t_{n}+1})\right)+4c_{b}\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{3}N_{t}}},

(176)

in which the penultimate line is obtained by expanding
Q_{t_{N_{t}-1}+1}^{\mathrm{UCB}}(s,a)-Q_{t_{N_{t}-1}+1}^{\mathrm{LCB}}(s,a)
recursively using the recursive relation from (
175
), and the last line is due to our initialization
0=Q_{1}^{\mathrm{LCB}}(s,a)\leq Q_{1}^{\mathrm{UCB}}(s,a)=\frac{1}{1-\gamma}
and the following fact

\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t}}\eta^{N_{t}}_{n}c_{b}\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{3}n}}\leq 2c_{b}\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{3}N_{t}}},

which is an immediate consequence of (
31
) in Lemma
6
.

Finally, (
176
) combined with (
174
) establishes the second inequality in the condition (
167
). With (
167
) proven, we have justified our invocation of Lemma
11
and thus concluded the proof of the inequality (
2
).

G.2.1
Proof of Lemma
11

We can decompose the quantity of interest into two terms and bound them separately:

\displaystyle\quad\sum_{t=1}^{T}w_{t}\left(V_{t}(s_{t+1})-V_{t}^{\mathrm{LCB}}(s_{t+1})\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{t=1}^{T}w_{t}\left(V_{t}(s_{t+1})-Q_{t+1}(s_{t+1},a_{t+1})+Q_{t+1}(s_{t+1},a_{t+1})-V_{t}^{\mathrm{LCB}}(s_{t+1})\right)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{t=1}^{T}w_{t}\Big(\left(V_{t}(s_{t+1})-Q_{t+1}(s_{t+1},a_{t+1})\right)+\left(Q_{t+1}(s_{t+1},a_{t+1})-Q_{t+1}^{\mathrm{LCB}}(s_{t+1},a_{t+1})\right)\Big),

(177)

in which the last step is due to
V_{t}^{\mathrm{LCB}}(s)\leq V_{t+1}^{\mathrm{LCB}}(s)\leq\max_{a}Q_{t+1}^{\mathrm{LCB}}(s,a)
for any
s\in{\mathcal{S}}
, which can be seen from the update rule of
V^{\mathrm{LCB}}
.

In the following, let us bound the two halves of the qunatity in (
177
) separately. Notice that the first half coincides with
\zeta_{t,1}
in Lemma
5
(defined in (
123
)). Thus, there exists some absolute constant
C_{\mathrm{v},1}>0
such that we have

\displaystyle\quad\ \sum_{t=1}^{T}w_{t}\big(V_{t}(s_{t+1})-Q_{t+1}(s_{t+1},a_{t+1})\big)

\displaystyle=V_{T}(s_{T+1})-Q_{T+1}(s_{T+1},a_{T+1})+\sum_{t=1}^{T-1}w_{t}\big(V_{t}(s_{t+1})-Q_{t+1}(s_{t+1},a_{t+1})\big)

\displaystyle\overset{(\mathrm{i})}{\leq}\frac{1}{1-\gamma}+\sum_{t=2}^{T}w_{t-1}\big(V_{t-1}(s_{t})-Q_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})\big)

\displaystyle\overset{(\mathrm{ii})}{\leq}\frac{1}{1-\gamma}+\sum_{t=1}^{T}w_{t-1}\big(V_{t-1}(s_{t})-Q_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})\big)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{1-\gamma}+\sum_{t=1}^{T}w_{t}\zeta_{t,1}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{1-\gamma}+w\sum_{t=1}^{T}\zeta_{t,1}

\displaystyle\overset{(\mathrm{iii})}{\lesssim}\frac{wSA}{1-\gamma}\log T+\frac{w\left(SA\right)^{3/4}T^{1/4}}{(1-\gamma)^{5/4}}\left(\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}\right)^{5/4}+w\sqrt{\frac{SA\log^{2}T}{1-\gamma}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\big(V_{t}(s_{t+1})-V^{\pi_{t+1}}(s_{t+1})\big)}

\displaystyle\leq C_{\mathrm{v},1}\left(\frac{wSA}{1-\gamma}\log T+\frac{w\left(SA\right)^{3/4}T^{1/4}}{(1-\gamma)^{5/4}}\left(\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}\right)^{5/4}+w\sqrt{\frac{SA\log^{2}T}{1-\gamma}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\big(V_{t}(s_{t+1})-V^{\pi_{t+1}}(s_{t+1})\big)}\right),

(178)

Above, (i) is due to
V_{T}(s_{T+1})\leq\frac{1}{1-\gamma}
and
Q_{T+1}(s_{T+1},a_{T+1})\geq Q^{\star}(s_{T+1},a_{T+1})\geq 0
. (ii) is due to
V_{0}(s_{1})-Q_{1}(s_{1},a_{1})\geq 0
, as we set
V_{0}(s)=\frac{1}{1-\gamma}
for any
s\in{\mathcal{S}}
in the proof of Lemma
5
. (iii) is by (
124
).
C_{\mathrm{v},1}>0
is some absolute constant.

Adopting the abbreviation
N_{t}:=N_{t}(s_{t+1},a_{t+1})
and
t_{n}:=t_{n}(s_{t+1},a_{t+1})
hereafter, we can expand the second half of (
177
) using (
167
) as follows:

\displaystyle\quad\ \sum_{t=1}^{T}w_{t}\left(Q_{t+1}(s_{t+1},a_{t+1})-Q_{t+1}^{\mathrm{LCB}}(s_{t+1},a_{t+1})\right)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{t=1}^{T}\frac{w_{t}\eta^{N_{t}}_{0}}{1-\gamma}+\gamma w_{t}\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t}}\eta^{N_{t}}_{n}\left(V_{t_{n}}(s_{t_{n}+1})-V^{\mathrm{LCB}}_{t_{n}}(s_{t_{n}+1})\right)+4c_{b}w_{t}\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{3}N_{t}}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{SAw}{1-\gamma}+\gamma\sum_{t=1}^{T}\widetilde{w}_{t}\left(V_{t}(s_{t+1})-V^{\mathrm{LCB}}_{t}(s_{t+1})\right)+8c_{b}\sqrt{\frac{SAwW\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{3}}}.

(179)

The last step is due to the following two chain of inequalities (
180
) and (
181
):

\displaystyle\quad\ 4c_{b}\sum_{t=1}^{T}w_{t}\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{3}N_{t}}}

\displaystyle=4c_{b}\sum_{(s,a)\in{\mathcal{S}}\times\mathcal{A}}\sum_{n=1}^{N_{T}(s,a)}w_{t_{n}}\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{3}n}}

\displaystyle\leq 4c_{b}\sum_{(s,a)\in{\mathcal{S}}\times\mathcal{A}}\sum_{n=1}^{\bar{N}_{T}(s,a)/w}w\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{3}n}}

\displaystyle\leq 4c_{b}\sum_{(s,a)\in{\mathcal{S}}\times\mathcal{A}}\int_{n=1}^{\bar{N}_{T}(s,a)/w}w\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{3}n}}

\displaystyle\leq 8c_{b}\sum_{(s,a)\in{\mathcal{S}}\times\mathcal{A}}\sqrt{\frac{w\bar{N}_{T}(s,a)\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{3}}}

\displaystyle\leq 8c_{b}\sqrt{\sum_{(s,a)\in{\mathcal{S}}\times\mathcal{A}}\frac{w\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{3}}}\sqrt{\sum_{(s,a)\in{\mathcal{S}}\times\mathcal{A}}\bar{N}_{T}(s,a)}

\displaystyle=8c_{b}\sqrt{\frac{SAwW\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{3}}},

(180)

in which we define
\bar{N}_{T}(s,a):=\sum_{t=1}^{T}w_{t}\ind_{(s_{t},a_{t})=(s,a)}
, and

\displaystyle\quad\ \gamma\sum_{t=1}^{T}w_{t}\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t}}\eta^{N_{t}}_{n}\left(V_{t_{n}}(s_{t_{n}+1})-V^{\mathrm{LCB}}_{t_{n}}(s_{t_{n}+1})\right)

\displaystyle\leq\gamma\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(V_{t}(s_{t+1})-V^{\mathrm{LCB}}_{t}(s_{t+1})\right)\sum_{n=N_{t+1}}^{N_{T}}w_{t_{n}}\eta^{n}_{N_{t}},

(181)

in which we define
\widetilde{w}_{t}:=\sum_{n=N_{t+1}}^{N_{T}}w_{t_{n}}\eta^{n}_{N_{t}}
. Note that
\widetilde{w}_{t}\leq\frac{(3-\gamma)}{2}w
by (
34
) in Lemma
6
.

Combining (
178
) and (
179
), we have

\displaystyle\quad\ \sum_{t=1}^{T}w_{t}\left(V_{t}(s_{t+1})-V_{t}^{\mathrm{LCB}}(s_{t+1})\right)

\displaystyle\leq C_{\mathrm{v},1}\frac{w\left(SA\right)^{3/4}T^{1/4}}{(1-\gamma)^{5/4}}\left(\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}\right)^{5/4}+C_{\mathrm{v},1}\frac{SAw}{1-\gamma}\log T+8c_{b}\sqrt{\frac{SAwW\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{3}}}

\displaystyle\quad+C_{\mathrm{v},1}w\sqrt{\frac{SA\log^{2}T}{1-\gamma}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\big(V_{t}(s_{t+1})-V^{\pi_{t+1}}(s_{t+1})\big)}+\gamma\sum_{t=1}^{T}\widetilde{w}_{t}\left(V_{t}(s_{t+1})-V^{\mathrm{LCB}}_{t}(s_{t+1})\right)

(182)

\displaystyle\overset{(\mathrm{i})}{\leq}C_{\mathrm{v},1}\frac{ew\left(SA\right)^{3/4}T^{1/4}}{(1-\gamma)^{9/4}}\left(\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}\right)^{5/4}+C_{\mathrm{v},1}\frac{eSAw}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\log T+8ec_{b}\sqrt{\frac{SAwW\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{5}}}

\displaystyle\quad+C_{\mathrm{v},1}ew\sqrt{\frac{SA\log^{2}T}{(1-\gamma)^{3}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\big(V_{t}(s_{t+1})-V^{\pi_{t+1}}(s_{t+1})\big)}+\gamma^{H_{\mathrm{v}}}\frac{eW}{1-\gamma}

\displaystyle\leq C_{\mathrm{v},1}\frac{ew\left(SA\right)^{3/4}T^{1/4}}{(1-\gamma)^{9/4}}\left(\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}\right)^{5/4}+C_{\mathrm{v},1}\frac{eSAw}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\log T+8ec_{b}\sqrt{\frac{SAwW\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{5}}}

\displaystyle\quad+C_{\mathrm{v},1}ew\sqrt{\frac{SA\log^{2}T}{(1-\gamma)^{3}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\big(V_{t}(s_{t+1})-V^{\pi_{t+1}}(s_{t+1})\big)}+eW,

(183)

where (i) is obtained by applying (
182
) recursively for
H_{\mathrm{v}}:=\lceil\frac{\ln\frac{1}{1-\gamma}}{\ln\frac{1}{\gamma}}\rceil
times and the fact that
(1+\frac{1-\gamma}{2})^{H_{\mathrm{v}}}<(1+\frac{1-\gamma}{2})^{\lceil\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\rceil}<(1+\frac{1-\gamma}{2})^{\frac{2}{1-\gamma}}<e
, which is true since
\gamma\geq\frac{1}{2}
and
H_{\mathrm{v}}<\lceil\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\rceil
. We also use the fact
\left\lVert V_{t}-V_{t}^{\mathrm{LCB}}\right\rVert_{\infty}\leq\frac{1}{1-\gamma}
.

Appendix H
Proof of Lemma
3

H.1
Proof of Inequality (
16
)

In the proof of this lemma, let us adopt the shorthand notation
t_{i}:=t_{i}(s)
to denote the index of the time step in which state
s
is visited for the
i
-th time. We can use this notation for any state
s
that has been visited at least once during the
T
steps of our algorithm’s execution. Note that for each
s
, both
V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t}(s)
and
V_{t}(s)
are only updated when
t=t_{i}+1
with indices
i
from
\{i~:~1\leq t_{i}\leq T\}
. Thus, to establish (
16
), it suffices to show that for any
s
and its corresponding
1\leq t_{i}\leq T
,

\displaystyle\left|V_{t_{i}+1}(s)-V_{t_{i}+1}^{\mathrm{R}}(s)\right|\leq 6.

(184)

In the following, let us fix any
s
and prove (
184
) by looking at three partition scenarios separately.

Case 1 of (
184
).

Suppose that
t_{i}
satisfies either Line
3
or Line
3
of Algorithm
3
, i.e., either

\displaystyle V_{t_{i}+1}(s)-V_{t_{i}+1}^{\mathrm{LCB}}(s)>3

(185)

or

\displaystyle V_{t_{i}+1}(s)-V_{t_{i}+1}^{\mathrm{LCB}}(s)\leq 3\qquad\text{and}\qquad u^{\mathrm{ref}}_{t_{i}}(s)=\mathsf{True}.

(186)

Either of these two conditions would trigger the update of the reference value function and thus result in

V_{t_{i}+1}(s)=V_{t_{i}+1}^{\mathrm{R}}(s),

which satisfies (
184
) trivially.

Case 2 of (
184
).

Suppose that
t_{i_{0}}
is the first time such that both (
185
) and (
186
) are violated, i.e.,

\displaystyle{i_{0}}\coloneqq\min\left\{j\mid V_{t_{j}+1}(s)-V_{t_{j}+1}^{\mathrm{LCB}}(s)\leq 3~\text{ and }~u^{\mathrm{ref}}_{t_{j}}(s)=\mathsf{False}\right\}.

(187)

In the following, let us show (
184
) is true when
i=i_{0}
.

First, notice that the definition (
187
) (first time both (
185
) and (
186
) are violated) suggests that the reference was updated last time
s
was visited before the
t_{i_{0}}
-th step, i.e.,
V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{i_{0}-1}+1}
was updated in the
t_{i_{0}-1}
-th step. Notice that since
s
is not visited during the interval when the step index
t
satisfies
t_{i_{0}-1}+1\leq t\leq t_{i_{0}}-1
,
V_{t+1}(s)
is not updated during this period, nor is the block between Line
3
-
3
of Algorithm
3
triggered (which would update the reference
V_{t+1}^{\mathrm{R}}(s)
). Thus, we can conclude

\displaystyle V_{t_{i_{0}}}^{\mathrm{R}}(s)=V_{t_{i_{0}-1}+1}^{\mathrm{R}}(s)=V_{t_{i_{0}-1}+1}(s)=V_{t_{i_{0}}}(s).

(188)

Furthermore, since the reference
V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{i_{0}-1}+1}
was updated in the
t_{i_{0}-1}
-th step, this indicates either (
185
) or (
186
) is satisfied in this step. If (
185
) is satisfied, then Line
3
and
3
of Algorithm
3
tell us that
\mathsf{True}=u^{\mathrm{ref}}_{t_{i_{0}-1}+1}(s)
and Line
3
is never triggered between Step
t_{i_{0}-1}+1
and Step
t_{i_{0}}-1
, which implies
u^{\mathrm{ref}}_{t_{i_{0}}}(s)=u^{\mathrm{ref}}_{t_{i_{0}-1}+1}(s)=\mathsf{True}
. However, this contradicts with the assumption
u^{\mathrm{ref}}_{t_{i_{0}}}(s)=\mathsf{False}
in (
187
). Hence, only (
186
) is true at the
t_{i_{0}-1}
-th step, which allows us to conclude

\displaystyle V_{t_{i_{0}}}(s)-V_{t_{i_{0}}}^{\mathrm{LCB}}(s)=V_{t_{i_{0}-1}+1}(s)-V_{t_{i_{0}-1}+1}^{\mathrm{LCB}}(s)\leq 3.

(189)

On the other hand, the definition (
187
) suggests that
V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{i_{0}}+1}(s)
is not updated by the update block between Line
3
-
3
at Step
t_{i_{0}}
, so we have

\displaystyle V_{t_{i_{0}}+1}^{\mathrm{R}}(s)=V_{t_{i_{0}}}^{\mathrm{R}}(s).

(190)

Finally, combining (
188
), (
189
) and (
190
), we can derive that

\displaystyle V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{i_{0}}+1}(s)-V_{t_{i_{0}}+1}(s)

\displaystyle\overset{\mathrm{(i)}}{=}V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{i_{0}}}(s)-V_{t_{i_{0}}+1}(s)\overset{\mathrm{(ii)}}{=}V_{t_{i_{0}}}(s)-V_{t_{i_{0}}+1}(s)

(191)

\displaystyle\overset{\mathrm{(iii)}}{\leq}V_{t_{i_{0}}}(s)-V^{\mathrm{LCB}}_{t_{i_{0}}}(s)\overset{\mathrm{(iv)}}{\leq}3,

(192)

where (i) is due to (
190
); (ii) is due to (
188
); (iii) holds since
V_{t_{i_{0}}+1}(s)\geq V^{\star}(s)\geq V^{\mathrm{LCB}}_{t_{i_{0}}}(s)
; (iv) follows from (
189
).
In addition, let us point out that (
191
), together with the monotonicity of
V_{t}(s)
, implies

\displaystyle V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{i_{0}}+1}(s)-V_{t_{i_{0}}+1}(s)=V_{t_{i_{0}}}(s)-V_{t_{i_{0}}+1}(s)\geq 0.

(193)

By (
192
) and (
193
), we can arrive at the desired bound (
184
) with
i=i_{0}
.

Case 3 of (
184
).

For the last case, consider any
i>i_{0}
(that does not satisfy Case 1). Then it must be true that
V_{t_{i}+1}(s)-V_{t_{i}+1}^{\mathrm{LCB}}(s)\leq 3
and
u^{\mathrm{ref}}_{t_{i}}(s)=\mathsf{False}
, due to the monotonicity of
V
and
V^{\mathrm{LCB}}
and our algorithmic design that
u^{\mathrm{ref}}(s)
stays False once set to False.

It then follows that

\displaystyle V_{t_{i}+1}^{\mathrm{R}}(s)

\displaystyle\overset{(\mathrm{i})}{\leq}V_{t_{i_{0}}+1}^{\mathrm{R}}(s)\overset{(\mathrm{ii})}{\leq}V_{t_{i_{0}}+1}(s)+3\overset{(\mathrm{iii})}{\leq}V_{t_{i_{0}}+1}^{\mathrm{LCB}}(s)+6\overset{(\mathrm{iv})}{\leq}V^{\star}(s)+6\overset{(\mathrm{v})}{\leq}V_{t_{i}+1}(s)+6.

(194)

Here, (i) holds due to the monotonicity of
V_{t}
and thus the monotonicity of
V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t}
(
V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t}
can only be updated from
V_{t}
); (ii) is a consequence of (
192
); (iii) comes from the definition of
i_{0}
in (
187
); (iv) arises from the pessimism of
V^{\mathrm{LCB}}
to
V^{\star}
(see Lemma
2
); (v) is valid since the optimism gives
V_{t_{i}+1}(s)\geq V^{\star}(s)
(see Lemma
1
).

On the other hand, since
V_{t}
is non-increasing in
t
and
V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t}
can only be updated from
V_{t}
, we have that
V_{t_{i}+1}(s)\leq V_{t_{i}+1}^{\mathrm{R}}(s)
, which, combined with (
194
), gives

0\leq V_{t_{i}+1}^{\mathrm{R}}(s)-V_{t_{i}+1}(s)\leq 6

and thus justifies (
184
) for this case.

Overall, taking the three aforementioned cases in conjunction, we can establishe (
184
) and thus (
16
) completely.

H.2
Proof of Inequality (
3
)

First, let us suppose that

\displaystyle V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t}(s_{t+1})-V^{\mathrm{R}}_{T}(s_{t+1})\neq 0

(195)

holds for some
t<T
. Let
t
be the largest number such that (
195
) holds. Then it implies two possible scenarios:

Case 1.

Suppose the following event is true

V_{t+1}(s_{t+1})-V_{t+1}^{\mathrm{LCB}}(s_{t+1})\leq 3\quad\text{ and }\quad u^{\mathrm{ref}}_{t}(s_{t+1})=\mathsf{False}.

(196)

Then for any
t^{\prime}\geq t+1
, one necessarily has

\displaystyle V_{t^{\prime}}(s_{t+1})-V_{t^{\prime}}^{\mathrm{LCB}}(s_{t+1})\leq V_{t+1}(s_{t+1})-V_{t+1}^{\mathrm{LCB}}(s_{t+1})\leq 3

(197)

by the monotonicity of
V_{t}
and
V_{t}^{\mathrm{LCB}}
. In addition, (
197
) and our assumption
u^{\mathrm{ref}}_{t}(s_{t+1})=\mathsf{False}
together can imply

\displaystyle u^{\mathrm{ref}}_{t^{\prime}}(s_{t+1})=u^{\mathrm{ref}}_{t}(s_{t+1})=\mathsf{False},

(198)

because both conditions in Line
3
and Line
3
of Algorithm
3
would be violated at the
(t+1)
-th step and stay so thereafter.

(
197
) and (
198
) imply that the reference
V^{\mathrm{R}}
will no longer be updated after the
t
-th step, thus indicating that

\displaystyle V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t}=V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t+1}=\cdots=V^{\mathrm{R}}_{T}.

(199)

However, this contradicts with the assumption (
195
), so by the contrapositive, (
195
) would imply this case is impossible.

Case 2.

As the complement event of Case 1, if the condition in (
196
) is false, then it implies that either

V_{t+1}(s_{t+1})-V_{t+1}^{\mathrm{LCB}}(s_{t+1})>3,

(200)

or

\displaystyle V_{t+1}(s_{t+1})-V_{t+1}^{\mathrm{LCB}}(s_{t+1})\leq 3\quad\text{and}\quad u^{\mathrm{ref}}_{t}(s_{t+1})=\mathsf{True}.

(201)

In summary, we have just shown that (
195
) can only occur if either (
200
) or (
201
) holds. In light of this, we can decompose the quantity of interest with respect to these two cases as follows:

\displaystyle\quad\ \sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(V_{t}^{\mathrm{R}}(s_{t+1})-V_{T}^{\mathrm{R}}(s_{t+1})\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(V_{t}^{\mathrm{R}}(s_{t+1})-V_{T}^{\mathrm{R}}(s_{t+1})\right)\ind\left\{V_{t}^{\mathrm{R}}(s_{t+1})-V_{T}^{\mathrm{R}}(s_{t+1})\neq 0\right\}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(V_{t}^{\mathrm{R}}(s_{t+1})-V_{T}^{\mathrm{R}}(s_{t+1})\right)\ind\left\{V_{t+1}(s_{t+1})-V_{t+1}^{\mathrm{LCB}}(s_{t+1})\leq 3\text{ and }u^{\mathrm{ref}}_{t}(s_{t+1})=\mathsf{True}\right\}

\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(V_{t}(s_{t+1})-V_{t}^{\mathrm{LCB}}(s_{t+1})\right)\ind\left\{V_{t+1}(s_{t+1})-V_{t+1}^{\mathrm{LCB}}(s_{t+1})>3\right\},

(202)

where the inequality is because
V_{t}(s_{t+1})\geq V_{t}^{\mathrm{R}}(s_{t+1})
, which is due to the monotonicity of
V_{t}
and the fact that
V^{\mathrm{R}}
can only be updated from
V
, as well as the fact that

V^{\mathrm{R}}_{T}(s_{t})\geq V^{\star}(s_{t})\geq V^{\mathrm{LCB}}_{t}(s_{t}),

which is due to the optimism of
V
and thus
V^{\mathrm{R}}
and the pessimism of
V^{\mathrm{LCB}}_{t}
in Lemma
1
and Lemma
2
respectively.

The first term in (
202
) can be easily bounded because for all
s\in{\mathcal{S}}
,

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\ind\left\{V_{t+1}(s)-V_{t+1}^{\mathrm{LCB}}(s)\leq 3\text{ and }u^{\mathrm{ref}}_{t}(s)=\mathsf{True}\right\}\leq 1.

(203)

This is because for each
s\in{\mathcal{S}}
, the above condition is satisfied at most once, in view of the monotonicity property of
V_{t}
and
V_{t}^{\mathrm{LCB}}
(their gap only grows smaller over time) as well as the update rule of
u_{\mathrm{ref}}
in Algorithm
3
. Consequently, one has

\displaystyle\quad\ \sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(V_{t}^{\mathrm{R}}(s_{t+1})-V_{T}^{\mathrm{R}}(s_{t+1})\right)\ind\left\{V_{t+1}(s_{t+1})-V_{t+1}^{\mathrm{LCB}}(s_{t+1})\leq 3\text{ and }u^{\mathrm{ref}}_{t}(s_{t+1})=\mathsf{True}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\ind\left\{V_{t+1}(s_{t+1})-V_{t+1}^{\mathrm{LCB}}(s_{t+1})\leq 3\text{ and }u^{\mathrm{ref}}_{t}(s_{t+1})=\mathsf{True}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\sum_{s\in\mathcal{S}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\ind\left\{V_{t+1}(s)-V_{t+1}^{\mathrm{LCB}}(s)\leq 3\text{ and }u^{\mathrm{ref}}_{t}(s)=\mathsf{True}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\sum_{s\in\mathcal{S}}1=\frac{S}{1-\gamma},

where the first inequality holds since
\left\lVert V_{t}^{\mathrm{R}}-V_{T}^{\mathrm{R}}\right\rVert_{\infty}\leq\frac{1}{1-\gamma}
.

To complete this proof, it remains to bound the second term in (
202
). By the monotonicity in
V_{t}
and
V_{t}^{\mathrm{LCB}}
,

\displaystyle\quad\ \sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(V_{t}(s_{t+1})-V_{t}^{\mathrm{LCB}}(s_{t+1})\right)\mathds{1}\left\{V_{t+1}(s_{t+1})-V_{t+1}^{\mathrm{LCB}}(s_{t+1})>3\right\}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(V_{t}(s_{t+1})-V_{t}^{\mathrm{LCB}}(s_{t+1})\right)\mathds{1}\left\{V_{t}(s_{t+1})-V_{t}^{\mathrm{LCB}}(s_{t+1})>3\right\}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\left(SA\right)^{3/4}T^{1/4}}{(1-\gamma)^{13/4}}\left(\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}\right)^{5/4}+\frac{SA}{(1-\gamma)^{6}}\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}+\sqrt{\frac{SA\log^{2}T}{(1-\gamma)^{5}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\big(V_{t-1}(s_{t})-V^{\pi_{t}}(s_{t})\big)},

(204)

where the last inequality is by (
2
) in Lemma
2
and
\left\lVert V_{t}-V_{t}^{\mathrm{LCB}}\right\rVert_{\infty}\leq\frac{1}{1-\gamma}
.

Finally, combining (
203
) and (
204
), we can conclude

\displaystyle\quad\ \sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(V_{t}^{\mathrm{R}}(s_{t+1})-V_{T}^{\mathrm{R}}(s_{t+1})\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\left(SA\right)^{3/4}T^{1/4}}{(1-\gamma)^{13/4}}\left(\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}\right)^{5/4}+\frac{SA}{(1-\gamma)^{6}}\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}+\sqrt{\frac{SA\log^{2}T}{(1-\gamma)^{5}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\big(V_{t-1}(s_{t})-V^{\pi_{t}}(s_{t})\big)}

(205)

as claimed.

Appendix I
Proof of Lemma
4

For notational simplicity, let us adopt the shorthand notation
t_{n}:=t_{n}(s_{t},a_{t})
throughout this section. Recall the update rule of
Q^{\mathrm{R}}_{t+1}(s_{t},a_{t})
in Line
2
of Algorithm
2
. We can derive

\displaystyle\quad\ Q^{\mathrm{R}}_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})-Q^{\star}(s_{t},a_{t})

\displaystyle=Q^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{N_{t-1}(s_{t},a_{t})}+1}(s_{t},a_{t})-Q^{\star}(s_{t},a_{t})

\displaystyle=\eta^{N_{t-1}(s_{t},a_{t})}_{0}\left(Q^{\mathrm{R}}_{1}(s_{t},a_{t})-Q^{\star}(s_{t},a_{t})\right)+\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t-1}(s_{t},a_{t})}\eta^{N_{t-1}(s_{t},a_{t})}_{n}b^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}+1}

\displaystyle\qquad+\gamma\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t-1}(s_{t},a_{t})}\eta^{N_{t-1}(s_{t},a_{t})}_{n}\left(V_{t_{n}}(s_{t_{n}+1})-V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}}(s_{t_{n}+1})+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\Big(V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{i}}(s_{t_{i}+1})-P_{s_{t},a_{t}}V^{\star}\Big)\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\eta^{N_{t-1}(s_{t},a_{t})}_{0}}{1-\gamma}+\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t-1}(s_{t},a_{t})}\eta^{N_{t-1}(s_{t},a_{t})}_{n}b^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}+1}

\displaystyle\qquad+\gamma\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t-1}(s_{t},a_{t})}\eta^{N_{t-1}(s_{t},a_{t})}_{n}\left(V_{t_{n}}(s_{t_{n}+1})-V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}}(s_{t_{n}+1})+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\Big(V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{i}}(s_{t_{i}+1})-P_{s_{t},a_{t}}V^{\star}\Big)\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\eta^{N_{t-1}(s_{t},a_{t})}_{0}}{1-\gamma}+B^{\mathrm{R}}_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})+2c_{b}\frac{\log^{2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{\left(N_{t-1}(s_{t},a_{t})\right)^{3/4}(1-\gamma)^{2}}

\displaystyle\qquad+\gamma\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t-1}(s_{t},a_{t})}\eta^{N_{t-1}(s_{t},a_{t})}_{n}\left(V_{t_{n}}(s_{t_{n}+1})-V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}}(s_{t_{n}+1})+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\Big(V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{i}}(s_{t_{i}+1})-P_{s_{t},a_{t}}V^{\star}\Big)\right),

where the penultimate line follows from our initialization
Q^{\mathrm{R}}_{1}(s_{t},a_{t})=\frac{1}{1-\gamma}
and the last line follows from (
96
) with
B^{\mathrm{R}}_{N_{t-1}+1}=B^{\mathrm{R}}_{t}
. With the inequality above, we can sum over
1\leq t\leq T
and have

\displaystyle\quad\ \sum_{t=1}^{T}Q^{\mathrm{R}}_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})-Q^{\star}(s_{t},a_{t})

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\frac{\eta^{N_{t-1}(s_{t},a_{t})}_{0}}{1-\gamma}+B^{\mathrm{R}}_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})+2c_{b}\frac{\log^{2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{\left(N_{t-1}(s_{t},a_{t})\right)^{3/4}(1-\gamma)^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad+\gamma\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t-1}(s_{t},a_{t})}\eta^{N_{t-1}(s_{t},a_{t})}_{n}\left(V_{t_{n}}(s_{t_{n}+1})-V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}}(s_{t_{n}+1})+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{i}}(s_{t_{i}+1})-P_{s_{t},a_{t}}V^{\star}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\underbrace{\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\frac{\eta^{N_{t-1}(s_{t},a_{t})}_{0}}{1-\gamma}+B^{\mathrm{R}}_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})+2c_{b}\frac{\log^{2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{\left(N_{t-1}(s_{t},a_{t})\right)^{3/4}(1-\gamma)^{2}}\right)}_{(\mathrm{I})}

\displaystyle\qquad+\underbrace{\gamma\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t-1}(s_{t},a_{t})}\eta^{N_{t-1}(s_{t},a_{t})}_{n}\Big(V_{t_{n}}(s_{t_{n}+1})-V^{\star}(s_{t_{n}+1})\Big)}_{(\mathrm{II})}

\displaystyle\qquad+\underbrace{\gamma\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t-1}(s_{t},a_{t})}\eta^{N_{t-1}(s_{t},a_{t})}_{n}\left(V^{\star}(s_{t_{n}+1})-V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}}(s_{t_{n}+1})+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{i}}(s_{t_{i}+1})-P_{s_{t},a_{t}}V^{\star}\right)}_{(\mathrm{III})}.

(206)

To finish the proof of this lemma, we control the three terms in (
206
) separately.

(I) in (
206
)
: We can bound (I) by making two observations. First, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\eta^{N_{t-1}(s_{t},a_{t})}_{0}\leq\sum_{(s,a)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}}\sum_{n=1}^{N_{T-1}(s,a)}\eta^{n}_{0}\leq SA,

(207)

where the last inequality follows since
\eta_{0}^{n}=0
for all
n>0
. Then, we can observe that

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\frac{1}{\left(N_{t-1}(s_{t},a_{t})\right)^{3/4}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{(s,a)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}}\sum_{n=1}^{N_{T-1}(s,a)}\frac{1}{n^{3/4}}\leq\sum_{(s,a)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}}4\left(N_{T-1}(s,a)\right)^{1/4}

\displaystyle\leq 4\left(\sum_{(s,a)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}}1\right)^{3/4}\left(\sum_{(s,a)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}}N_{T-1}(s,a)\right)^{1/4}\leq 4(SA)^{3/4}T^{1/4},

(208)

where the penultimate inequality is due to Hölder’s inequality.

Applying the above two bounds to (I) yields

\displaystyle\quad\ \sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\frac{\eta^{N_{t-1}(s_{t},a_{t})}_{0}}{1-\gamma}+B^{\mathrm{R}}_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})+2c_{b}\frac{\log^{2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(N_{t-1}(s_{t},a_{t}))^{3/4}(1-\gamma)^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{SA}{1-\gamma}+8c_{b}(SA)^{3/4}T^{1/4}\frac{1}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\log^{2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}+\sum_{t=1}^{T}B^{\mathrm{R}}_{t}(s_{t},a_{t}).

(209)

(II) in (
206
)
: It can be obtained via some algebra that

\displaystyle\quad\ \gamma\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t-1}(s_{t},a_{t})}\eta^{N_{t-1}(s_{t},a_{t})}_{n}\big(V_{t_{n}}(s_{t_{n}+1})-V^{\star}(s_{t_{n}+1})\big)

\displaystyle=\gamma\sum_{l=1}^{T}\sum_{N=N_{l}(s_{l},a_{l})}^{N_{T-1}(s_{l},a_{l})}\eta_{N_{l}(s_{l},a_{l})}^{N}\big(V_{l}(s_{l+1})-V^{\star}(s_{l+1})\big)

\displaystyle\leq\gamma\left(1+\frac{1-\gamma}{2}\right)\sum_{t=1}^{T}\big(V_{t}(s_{t+1})-V^{\star}(s_{t+1})\big).

(210)

Above, the second line replaces
t_{n}
(resp.
n
) with
l
(resp.
N_{l}(s_{l},a_{l})
), and the last line is due to (
34
) in Lemma
6
) and replaces
l
with
t
again.

(III) in (
206
)
: (III) can derived in the following manner:

\displaystyle\quad\ \gamma\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t-1}(s_{t},a_{t})}\eta^{N_{t-1}(s_{t},a_{t})}_{n}\left(V^{\star}(s_{t_{n}+1})-V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}}(s_{t_{n}+1})+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\Big(V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{i}}(s_{t_{i}+1})-P_{s_{t},a_{t}}V^{\star}\Big)\right)

\displaystyle=\gamma\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t-1}(s_{t},a_{t})}\eta^{N_{t-1}(s_{t},a_{t})}_{n}\left(\left(P_{t_{n}}-P_{s_{t},a_{t}}\right)\left(V^{\star}-V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}}\right)+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\Big(V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{i}}(s_{t_{i}+1})-P_{s_{t},a_{t}}V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}}\Big)\right)

\displaystyle=\gamma\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{N=N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}^{N_{T-1}(s_{t},a_{t})}\eta_{N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}^{N}\left(\left(P_{t}-P_{s_{t},a_{t}}\right)\left(V^{\star}-V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t}\right)+\frac{\sum_{i=1}^{N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}\left(V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{i}}(s_{t_{i}+1})-P_{s_{t},a_{t}}V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t}\right)}{N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}\right).

Here, the second line is obtained by
V^{\star}(s_{t_{n}+1})-V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}}(s_{t_{n}+1})=P_{t_{n}}\left(V^{\star}-V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}}\right)
, and the third line is by a simple rearrangement of terms and replacement of
t_{n}
(resp.
n
) with
t
(resp.
N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})
).

Furthermore, we can rearrange the terms in (
206
) and write

\displaystyle\quad\ \sum_{t=1}^{T}\big(Q^{\mathrm{R}}_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})-Q^{\star}(s_{t},a_{t})\big)

\displaystyle\leq\gamma\left(1+\frac{1-\gamma}{2}\right)\sum_{t=1}^{T}\big(V_{t}(s_{t+1})-V^{\star}(s_{t+1})\big)+\frac{SA}{1-\gamma}+8c_{b}(SA)^{3/4}T^{1/4}\frac{1}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}+\underbrace{\sum_{t=1}^{T}B^{\mathrm{R}}_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}_{\mathcal{R}_{1}}

\displaystyle\quad+\underbrace{\gamma\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{N=N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}^{N_{T-1}(s_{t},a_{t})}\eta_{N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}^{N}\left[\left(P_{t}-P_{s_{t},a_{t}}\right)\left(V^{\star}-V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t}\right)+\frac{\sum_{i=1}^{N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}\left(V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{i}}(s_{t_{i}+1})-P_{s_{t},a_{t}}V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t}\right)}{N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}\right]}_{\mathcal{R}_{2}},

(211)

where the upper bounds of
\mathcal{R}_{1}
and
\mathcal{R}_{2}
are derived in Appendix
I.1
and
I.2
respectively.

Overall, taking the obtained upper bounds on
\mathcal{R}_{1}
and
\mathcal{R}_{2}
, we can conclude with probability
1-\delta
that

\displaystyle\quad\ \sum_{t=1}^{T}\big(Q^{\mathrm{R}}_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})-Q^{\star}(s_{t},a_{t})\big)

\displaystyle\leq\gamma\left(1+\frac{1-\gamma}{2}\right)\sum_{t=1}^{T}\big(V_{t}(s_{t+1})-V^{\star}(s_{t+1})\big)+C_{\mathrm{r},1}\Bigg(\sqrt{\frac{SAT}{1-\gamma}\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}}+\frac{SA}{(1-\gamma)^{15/4}}\log^{7/2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}\Bigg)

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{SA}{1-\gamma}+8c_{b}(SA)^{3/4}T^{1/4}\frac{1}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}

\displaystyle\qquad+C_{\mathrm{r},2}\Bigg(\sqrt{\frac{SAT}{1-\gamma}\log^{3}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}+\frac{SA}{(1-\gamma)^{7}}\log^{5/2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}+\sqrt{\frac{SA\log^{2}T}{(1-\gamma)^{5}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\big(V_{t-1}(s_{t})-V^{\pi_{t}}(s_{t})\big)}\Bigg)

\displaystyle\leq\gamma\left(1+\frac{1-\gamma}{2}\right)\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(V_{t}(s_{t+1})-V^{\star}(s_{t+1})\right)+C_{\mathrm{r},1}\Bigg(\sqrt{\frac{SAT}{1-\gamma}\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}}+\frac{SA}{(1-\gamma)^{15/4}}\log^{7/2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}\Bigg)

\displaystyle\qquad+8c_{b}\sqrt{\frac{SAT}{1-\gamma}\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}}+8c_{b}\frac{SA}{(1-\gamma)^{7/2}}\log^{3/2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}

\displaystyle\qquad+C_{\mathrm{r},2}\Bigg(\sqrt{\frac{SAT}{1-\gamma}\log^{3}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}+\frac{SA}{(1-\gamma)^{7}}\log^{5/2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}+\sqrt{\frac{SA\log^{2}T}{(1-\gamma)^{5}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\big(V_{t-1}(s_{t})-V^{\pi_{t}}(s_{t})\big)}\Bigg)

\displaystyle\leq\gamma\left(1+\frac{1-\gamma}{2}\right)\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(V_{t}(s_{t+1})-V^{\star}(s_{t+1})\right)+C_{1}\Bigg(\sqrt{\frac{SAT}{1-\gamma}\log^{3}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}+\frac{SA}{(1-\gamma)^{7}}\log^{7/2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}

\displaystyle\qquad+\sqrt{\frac{SA\log^{2}T}{(1-\gamma)^{5}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\big(V_{t-1}(s_{t})-V^{\pi_{t}}(s_{t})\big)}\Bigg),

(212)

in which the penultimate step uses
2ab\leq a^{2}+b^{2}
, which follows from Cauchy-Schwarz.
C_{1}>0
is a sufficiently large absolute constant.

At this point, we have proven the desired result.

I.1
Bounding
\mathcal{R}_{1}

By the definition of
B^{\mathrm{R}}_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})
in Line
2
of Algorithm
2
, we can decompose
\mathcal{R}_{1}
as follows:

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{1}

\displaystyle=\underbrace{c_{b}\sqrt{\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sqrt{\frac{\sigma^{\mathrm{adv}}_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})-\left(\mu^{\mathrm{adv}}_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})\right)^{2}}{N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}}}_{\mathcal{R}_{1,1}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\underbrace{c_{b}\sqrt{\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sqrt{\frac{\sigma^{\mathrm{ref}}_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})-\left(\mu^{\mathrm{ref}}_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})\right)^{2}}{N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}}}_{\mathcal{R}_{1,2}}.

(213)

We proceed by bounding the two terms in (
213
) separately.

Step 1: bounding
\mathcal{R}_{1,1}
.

Some manipulation on the summation part of
\mathcal{R}_{1,1}
gives

\displaystyle\quad\ \sum_{t=1}^{T}\sqrt{\frac{\sigma^{\mathrm{adv}}_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})-\left(\mu^{\mathrm{adv}}_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})\right)^{2}}{N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sqrt{\frac{\sigma^{\mathrm{adv}}_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}{N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sqrt{\frac{\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}\eta_{n}^{N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}\left(V_{t_{n}}(s_{t_{n}+1})-V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}}(s_{t_{n}+1})\right)^{2}}{N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sqrt{\frac{81}{N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}}

\displaystyle\leq 18\sqrt{SAT},

(214)

in which the first inequality follows from the update rule of
\sigma_{t}^{\mathrm{adv}}
in Line
2
of Algorithm
2
, and the second inequality uses the relation

|V_{t}(s_{t})-V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t}(s_{t})|\leq 9
(cf. (
16
)) and the property
\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}\eta^{N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}_{n}\leq 1
(cf. (
30
) in Lemma
6
). The last inequality holds due to the following series of inequalities:

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sqrt{\frac{1}{N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{(s,a)\in{\mathcal{S}}\times\mathcal{A}}\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t}(s,a)}\sqrt{\frac{1}{n}}

\displaystyle\leq 2\sum_{(s,a)\in{\mathcal{S}}\times\mathcal{A}}\sqrt{N_{t}(s,a)}

\displaystyle\leq 2\sqrt{\sum_{(s,a)\in{\mathcal{S}}\times\mathcal{A}}1}\cdot\sqrt{\sum_{(s,a)\in{\mathcal{S}}\times\mathcal{A}}N_{t}(s,a)}=2\sqrt{SAT},

(215)

where the last line is obtained by Cauchy-Schwarz and the fact that
\sum_{(s,a)}N_{t}(s,a)=T
.

Overall, we have

\mathcal{R}_{1,1}\leq 9c_{b}\sqrt{\frac{SAT}{1-\gamma}\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}}.

(216)

Step 2: bounding
\mathcal{R}_{1,2}
.

By the update rules of
\mu_{t}^{\mathrm{ref}}
and
\sigma_{t}^{\mathrm{ref}}
in (
112a
) and (
112b
), we can write the summation part of
\mathcal{R}_{1,2}
as

\displaystyle\quad\ \sum_{t=1}^{T}\sqrt{\frac{\sigma^{\mathrm{ref}}_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})-\left(\mu^{\mathrm{ref}}_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})\right)^{2}}{N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sqrt{\frac{1}{N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}}\sqrt{\frac{\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}\left(V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}}(s_{t_{n}+1})\right)^{2}}{N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}-\left(\frac{\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}}(s_{t_{n}+1})}{N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}\right)^{2}}.

(217)

To further bound the summation in (
217
), we focus on a part of each summand, which can be decomposed into two terms as follows:

\displaystyle\quad\ \frac{\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}\left(V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}}(s_{t_{n}+1})\right)^{2}}{N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}-\left(\frac{\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}}(s_{t_{n}+1})}{N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}\left(V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}}(s_{t_{n}+1})\right)^{2}-\left(V^{\star}(s_{t_{n}+1})\right)^{2}}{N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}+\frac{\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}\left(V^{\star}(s_{t_{n}+1})\right)^{2}}{N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}-\left(\frac{\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}V^{\star}(s_{t_{n}+1})}{N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}\left(V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}}(s_{t_{n}+1})+V^{\star}(s_{t_{n}+1})\right)\left(V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}}(s_{t_{n}+1})-V^{\star}(s_{t_{n}+1})\right)}{N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}+\frac{\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}\left(V^{\star}(s_{t_{n}+1})\right)^{2}}{N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}-\left(\frac{\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}V^{\star}(s_{t_{n}+1})}{N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\underbrace{\frac{\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}\frac{2}{1-\gamma}\left(V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}}(s_{t_{n}+1})-V^{\star}(s_{t_{n}+1})\right)}{N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}}_{=:\mathcal{R}_{1,2,1}^{(t)}}+\underbrace{\frac{\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}\left(V^{\star}(s_{t_{n}+1})\right)^{2}}{N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}-\left(\frac{\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}V^{\star}(s_{t_{n}+1})}{N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}\right)^{2}}_{=:\mathcal{R}_{1,2,2}^{(t)}}.

(218)

Above, the last line is obtained due to the fact
\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\geq\left\lVert V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}}\right\rVert_{\infty}\geq\left\lVert V^{\star}\right\rVert_{\infty}\geq 0
for all
t_{n}\leq T
.

In order to arrive at a bound on (
217
), we shall bound
\mathcal{R}_{1,2,1}^{(t)}
and
\mathcal{R}_{1,2,2}^{(t)}
separately.

Define
J_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})
as

J_{t}(s_{t},a_{t}):=\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}\left(V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}}(s_{t_{n}+1})-V^{\star}(s_{t_{n}+1})\right)\ind\left(V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}}(s_{t_{n}+1})-V^{\star}(s_{t_{n}+1})>12\right).

(219)

\mathcal{R}_{1,2,1}^{(t)}
can be simply decomposed as follows

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{1,2,1}^{(t)}

\displaystyle=\frac{2}{(1-\gamma)N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}\left(\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}\left(V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}}(s_{t_{n}+1})-V^{\star}(s_{t_{n}+1})\right)\ind\left(V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{n}}(s_{t_{n}+1})-V^{\star}(s_{t_{n}+1})\leq 9\right)+J_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{24}{1-\gamma}+\frac{2}{(1-\gamma)N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}J_{t}(s_{t},a_{t}).

(220)

On the other hand, we claim

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{1,2,2}^{(t)}\lesssim\Var_{s_{t},a_{t}}(V^{\star})+\frac{1}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}},

(221)

which will be justified in Appendix
I.1.1
.

Substituting (
220
) and (
221
) back into (
218
) and (
217
) allows us to have

\displaystyle\quad\ \sum_{t=1}^{T}\sqrt{\frac{\sigma^{\mathrm{ref}}_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})-\left(\mu^{\mathrm{ref}}_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})\right)^{2}}{N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sqrt{\frac{1}{N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}}\sqrt{\frac{1}{1-\gamma}+\frac{J_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}{(1-\gamma)N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}+\Var_{s_{t},a_{t}}(V^{\star})+\frac{1}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}}}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{t=1}^{T}\Bigg(\sqrt{\frac{1}{(1-\gamma)N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}}+\frac{\sqrt{J_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}}{N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})\sqrt{1-\gamma}}+\sqrt{\frac{\Var_{s_{t},a_{t}}(V^{\star})}{N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}}+\frac{\log^{1/4}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)\big(N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})\big)^{3/4}}\Bigg)

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{SAT}{1-\gamma}}+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\frac{\sqrt{J_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}}{N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})\sqrt{1-\gamma}}+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sqrt{\frac{\Var_{s_{t},a_{t}}(V^{\star})}{N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}}+\frac{(SA)^{3/4}}{1-\gamma}\left(T\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}\right)^{1/4},

(222)

in which the last line follows from (
215
) and (
I
).

Step 3: combining the preceding bounds.

Finally, substituting (
216
) and (
222
) back into (
213
) leads to

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{1}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{SAT}{1-\gamma}\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}}+\sqrt{\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sqrt{\frac{\sigma^{\mathrm{ref}}_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})-\left(\mu^{\mathrm{ref}}_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})\right)^{2}}{N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{SAT}{1-\gamma}\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}}+\frac{(SA)^{3/4}}{1-\gamma}T^{1/4}\log^{3/4}\frac{SAT}{\delta}+\sqrt{\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sqrt{\frac{\Var_{s_{t},a_{t}}(V^{\star})}{N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}}+\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{1-\gamma}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\frac{\sqrt{J_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}}{N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}

\displaystyle\overset{\mathrm{(i)}}{\lesssim}\sqrt{\frac{SAT}{1-\gamma}\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}}+\frac{(SA)^{3/4}}{1-\gamma}T^{1/4}\log^{3/4}\frac{SAT}{\delta}+\frac{SA}{(1-\gamma)^{15/4}}\log^{7/2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}

\displaystyle\overset{\mathrm{(ii)}}{\lesssim}\sqrt{\frac{SAT}{1-\gamma}\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}}+\frac{SA}{(1-\gamma)^{15/4}}\log^{7/2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}.

Here, (i) is true due to the following two inequalities

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sqrt{\frac{\Var_{s_{t},a_{t}}(V^{\star})}{N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{SAT}{1-\gamma}}+\frac{SA}{(1-\gamma)^{3}}\log\frac{SAT}{\delta},

(223)

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\frac{\sqrt{J_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}}{N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{SAT}+\frac{SA}{(1-\gamma)^{13/4}}\log^{3}\frac{SAT}{\delta},

(224)

whose proofs are deferred to Appendix
I.1.2
and Appendix
I.1.3
, respectively. In addition, (ii) uses
2ab\leq a^{2}+b^{2}
and is obtained via the following logic:

\displaystyle\frac{(SA)^{3/4}}{1-\gamma}T^{1/4}\log^{3/4}\frac{SAT}{\delta}

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{SA}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}\right)^{1/2}\left(SAT\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}\right)^{1/4}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{SA}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}+\sqrt{\frac{SAT}{1-\gamma}\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}}.

This concludes the proof of the advertised upper bound on
\mathcal{R}_{1}
that leads to (
212
).

I.1.1
Proof of Inequality (
221
)

We begin by constructing the necessary notation and verify the conditions for Lemma
7
. Consider any
N\in[T]
. Let

\displaystyle W_{i}:=({V}^{\star})^{2}\qquad\text{and}\qquad u_{i}(s,a,N):=\frac{1}{N}.

(225)

Clearly,
W_{i}
and
u_{i}(s,a,N)
are deterministic at the end of the
(i-1)
-th step. Moreover, define

\displaystyle C_{\mathrm{u}}:=\frac{1}{N}\qquad\text{and}\qquad\left\lVert W_{i}\right\rVert_{\infty}\leq\frac{1}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}=:C_{\mathrm{w}}.

(226)

Lastly, it is obvious that

\displaystyle 0\leq\sum_{n=1}^{N}u_{t_{n}(s,a)}(s,a,N)=1

(227)

holds for all
(N,s,a)\in[T]\times{\mathcal{S}}\times\mathcal{A}
.

By the definition (
28
)

\Var_{s_{t},a_{t}}(V^{\star})=P_{s_{t},a_{t}}(V^{\star})^{2}-\left(P_{s_{t},a_{t}}V^{\star}\right)^{2},

we have that with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle\quad\ \left|\frac{1}{{N_{t}}}\sum_{n=1}^{{N_{t}}}V^{\star}(s_{t_{n}+1})^{2}-\left(\frac{1}{{N_{t}}}\sum_{n=1}^{{N_{t}}}V^{\star}(s_{t_{n}+1})\right)^{2}-\Var_{s_{t},a_{t}}(V^{\star})\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left|\frac{1}{{N_{t}}}\sum_{n=1}^{{N_{t}}}V^{\star}(s_{t_{n}+1})^{2}-P_{s_{t},a_{t}}(V^{\star})^{2}\right|+\left|\left(\frac{1}{{N_{t}}}\sum_{n=1}^{{N_{t}}}V^{\star}(s_{t_{n}+1})\right)^{2}-(P_{s_{t},a_{t}}V^{\star})^{2}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{{N_{t}}}}+\left|\left(\frac{1}{{N_{t}}}\sum_{n=1}^{{N_{t}}}V^{\star}(s_{t_{n}+1})\right)^{2}-(P_{s_{t},a_{t}}V^{\star})^{2}\right|

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{{N_{t}}}}+\left|\frac{1}{{N_{t}}}\sum_{n=1}^{{N_{t}}}V^{\star}(s_{t_{n}+1})-P_{s_{t},a_{t}}V^{\star}\right|\cdot\left|\frac{1}{{N_{t}}}\sum_{n=1}^{{N_{t}}}V^{\star}(s_{t_{n}+1})+P_{s_{t},a_{t}}V^{\star}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{{N_{t}}}},

where the first inequality is by the definition (
28
):

\Var_{s_{t},a_{t}}(V^{\star})=P_{s_{t},a_{t}}(V^{\star})^{2}-\left(P_{s_{t},a_{t}}V^{\star}\right)^{2},

and the second inequality is obtained through the application of Lemma
7
with (
225
), (
226
) and
(N,s,a)=(N_{t},s_{t},a_{t})
, which allows us to conclude that with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{{N_{t}}}\sum_{n=1}^{{N_{t}}}\big(V^{\star}(s_{t_{n}+1})\big)^{2}-P_{s_{t},a_{t}}(V^{\star})^{2}\right|=\left|\frac{1}{{N_{t}}}\sum_{n=1}^{{N_{t}}}\left(P_{t_{n}}-P_{s_{t},a_{t}}\right)\left(V^{\star}\right)^{2}\right|\lesssim\frac{1}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{{N_{t}}}},

and similarly the last line is obtained with the fact
\left\lVert V^{\star}\right\rVert_{\infty}\leq\frac{1}{1-\gamma}
and an application of Lemma
7
, which allows us to conclude that with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{{N_{t}}}\sum_{n=1}^{{N_{t}}}V^{\star}(s_{t_{n}+1})-P_{s_{t},a_{t}}V^{\star}\right|=\left|\frac{1}{{N_{t}}}\sum_{n=1}^{{N_{t}}}\left(P_{t_{n}}-P_{s_{t},a_{t}}\right)V^{\star}\right|\lesssim\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{{N_{t}}}}.

I.1.2
Proof of Inequality (
223
)

Let us begin with the following manipulation

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sqrt{\frac{\Var_{s_{t},a_{t}}(V^{\star})}{N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{(s,a)\in{\mathcal{S}}\times\mathcal{A}}\sum_{n=1}^{N_{t}(s,a)}\sqrt{\frac{\Var_{s,a}(V^{\star})}{n}}

\displaystyle\overset{\mathrm{(i)}}{\leq}2\sum_{(s,a)\in{\mathcal{S}}\times\mathcal{A}}\sqrt{N_{t}(s,a)\Var_{s,a}(V^{\star})}

\displaystyle\overset{\mathrm{(ii)}}{\leq}2\sqrt{\sum_{(s,a)\in{\mathcal{S}}\times\mathcal{A}}1}\cdot\sqrt{\sum_{(s,a)\in{\mathcal{S}}\times\mathcal{A}}N_{t}(s,a)\Var_{s,a}(V^{\star})}

\displaystyle=2\sqrt{SA}\sqrt{\sum_{t=1}^{T}\Var_{s_{t},a_{t}}(V^{\star})},

(228)

in which (i) is due to
\sum_{n=1}^{N}1/{\sqrt{n}}\leq 2\sqrt{N}
; (ii) is by the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality.

It only remains to bound the variance term in (
228
). To this end, let us decompose it further into

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\Var_{s_{t},a_{t}}(V^{\star})

\displaystyle\leq\underbrace{\sum_{t=1}^{T}\Var_{s_{t},a_{t}}\big(V^{\pi_{t}}\big)}_{\alpha_{1}}+\underbrace{\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left|\Var_{s_{t},a_{t}}(V^{\star})-\Var_{s_{t},a_{t}}(V^{\pi_{t}})\right|}_{\alpha_{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{T}{1-\gamma}+\sqrt{\frac{SAT\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{7}}}+\frac{SA\log^{2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{9/2}}+\frac{1}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\sqrt{T\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}}+\frac{\log T}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\sqrt{SAT}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{T}{1-\gamma}+\sqrt{\frac{SAT\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{7}}}+\frac{SA\log^{2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{9/2}}+\frac{\log T}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\sqrt{SAT}.

(229)

The above inequality can be obtained with an upper bound on
\alpha_{1}
and
\alpha_{2}
respectively. Let us use
e_{s}
to denote the
S
-dimensional canonical basis vector whose entry is
1
in the
s
-th dimension. To bound
\alpha_{1}
in (
229
), we can first write
\alpha_{1}
as follows:

\displaystyle\Var_{s_{t},a_{t}}\big(V^{\pi_{t}}\big)

\displaystyle=P_{s_{t},a_{t}}\left(V^{\pi_{t}}\right)^{2}-\left(P_{s_{t},a_{t}}V^{\pi_{t}}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=P_{s_{t},a_{t}}\left(V^{\pi_{t}}\right)^{2}-\frac{1}{\gamma^{2}}\left(r(s_{t},a_{t})-V^{\pi_{t}}(s_{t})\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=P_{s_{t},a_{t}}\left(V^{\pi_{t}}\right)^{2}-\frac{1}{\gamma^{2}}r^{2}(s_{t},a_{t})-\frac{1}{\gamma^{2}}\left(V^{\pi_{t}}(s_{t})\right)^{2}+\frac{2}{\gamma^{2}}V^{\pi_{t}}(s_{t})\cdot r(s_{t},a_{t})

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\gamma^{2}}\left(\gamma^{2}P_{s_{t},a_{t}}-e_{s_{t}}^{\top}\right)\left(V^{\pi_{t}}\right)^{2}+\frac{2}{\gamma^{2}}V^{\pi_{t}}(s_{t})\cdot r(s_{t},a_{t}).

(230)

Here, the second line follows from the Bellman optimality equation

V^{\pi_{t}}(s_{t})=Q^{\pi_{t}}(s_{t},a_{t})=r(s_{t},a_{t})+\gamma P_{s_{t},a_{t}}V^{\pi_{t}}
. Now, we are ready to bound
\alpha_{1}
under two partition cases. If
\gamma<\frac{1}{2}
, then

\displaystyle\alpha_{1}

\displaystyle=\sum_{t=1}^{T}\Var_{s_{t},a_{t}}\big(V^{\pi_{t}}\big)

\displaystyle=\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(P_{s_{t},a_{t}}\left(V^{\pi_{t}}\right)^{2}-\left(P_{s_{t},a_{t}}V^{\pi_{t}}\right)^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{T}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2T}{1-\gamma}.

If
\gamma\geq\frac{1}{2}
, by (
230
), we can write

\displaystyle\alpha_{1}

\displaystyle=\sum_{t=1}^{T}\Var_{s_{t},a_{t}}\left(V^{\pi_{t}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\frac{1}{\gamma^{2}}\left(\gamma^{2}P_{s_{t},a_{t}}-e_{s_{t}}^{\top}\right)\left(V^{\pi_{t}}\right)^{2}+\frac{2}{\gamma^{2}}V^{\pi_{t}}(s_{t})\cdot r(s_{t},a_{t})\right)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\frac{1}{\gamma^{2}}\left|\left(\gamma^{2}P_{s_{t},a_{t}}-e_{s_{t}}^{\top}\right)\left(V^{\pi_{t}}\right)^{2}\right|+\frac{2}{\gamma^{2}}V^{\pi_{t}}(s_{t})\cdot r(s_{t},a_{t})\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\frac{1}{\gamma^{2}}\left|\left[(1-\gamma)e_{s_{t}}^{\top}+\gamma(e_{s_{t}}^{\top}-\gamma P_{s_{t},a_{t}})\right]\left(V^{\pi_{t}}\right)^{2}\right|+\frac{2}{\gamma^{2}}V^{\pi_{t}}(s_{t})\cdot r(s_{t},a_{t})\right)

\displaystyle\overset{(\mathrm{i})}{\leq}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\frac{1-\gamma}{\gamma^{2}}\left\lVert e_{s_{t}}^{\top}\right\rVert_{1}\left\lVert V^{\pi_{t}}\right\rVert^{2}_{\infty}+\frac{1}{\gamma}\left|(e_{s_{t}}^{\top}-\gamma P_{s_{t},a_{t}})V^{\pi_{t}}\right|\left\lVert V^{\pi_{t}}\right\rVert_{\infty}+\frac{2}{\gamma^{2}}V^{\pi_{t}}(s_{t})\right)

\displaystyle\overset{(\mathrm{ii})}{=}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\frac{1-\gamma}{\gamma^{2}}\left\lVert e_{s_{t}}^{\top}\right\rVert_{1}\left\lVert V^{\pi_{t}}\right\rVert^{2}_{\infty}+\frac{1}{\gamma}\left|r(s_{t},a_{t})\right|\left\lVert V^{\pi_{t}}\right\rVert_{\infty}+\frac{2}{\gamma^{2}}V^{\pi_{t}}(s_{t})\right)

\displaystyle\overset{(\mathrm{iii})}{\leq}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\frac{1}{2\gamma^{2}(1-\gamma)}+\frac{1}{\gamma(1-\gamma)}+\frac{2}{\gamma^{2}(1-\gamma)}\right)

\displaystyle\overset{(\mathrm{iv})}{\leq}\frac{12T}{1-\gamma},

where (i) is due to the Hölder inequality and
\left\lVert r\right\rVert_{\infty}\leq 1
; (ii) is due to the Bellman optimality equation
V^{\pi_{t}}(s_{t})=Q^{\pi_{t}}(s_{t},a_{t})=r(s_{t},a_{t})+\gamma P_{s_{t},a_{t}}V^{\pi_{t}}
and thus
(e_{s_{t}}^{\top}-\gamma P_{s_{t},a_{t}})V^{\pi_{t}}=r(s_{t},a_{t})
; (iii) is due to
\left\lVert V^{\pi_{t}}\right\rVert_{\infty}\leq\frac{1}{1-\gamma}
and
\left\lVert e_{s_{t}}\right\rVert_{1}=1
and
\left\lVert r\right\rVert_{\infty}\leq 1
; (iv) is due to
\gamma\geq\frac{1}{2}
.

On the other hand,
\alpha_{2}
in (
229
) can be bounded as follows

\displaystyle\alpha_{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left|\Var_{s_{t},a_{t}}(V^{\star})-\Var_{s_{t},a_{t}}(V^{\pi_{t}})\right|

\displaystyle=\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left|P_{s_{t},a_{t}}(V^{\star})^{2}-\left(P_{s_{t},a_{t}}V^{\star}\right)^{2}-P_{s_{t},a_{t}}(V^{\pi_{t}})^{2}+\left(P_{s_{t},a_{t}}V^{\pi_{t}}\right)^{2}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\left|P_{s_{t},a_{t}}\left(\left(V^{\star}-V^{\pi_{t}}\right)\circ\left(V^{\star}+V^{\pi_{t}}\right)\right)\right|+\left|\left(P_{s_{t},a_{t}}V^{\star}\right)^{2}-\left(P_{s_{t},a_{t}}V^{\pi_{t}}\right)^{2}\right|\right)

\displaystyle\overset{(\mathrm{i})}{\leq}\frac{4}{1-\gamma}\sum_{t=1}^{T}P_{s_{t},a_{t}}\left(V^{\star}-V^{\pi_{t}}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{4}{1-\gamma}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\{V^{\star}(s_{t+1})-V^{\pi_{t}}(s_{t+1})+\left(P_{s_{t},a_{t}}-P_{t}\right)\left(V^{\star}-V^{\pi_{t}}\right)\right\}

\displaystyle\overset{(\mathrm{ii})}{=}\frac{4}{1-\gamma}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\Delta_{t}+\phi_{t}\right)

\displaystyle\overset{(\mathrm{iii})}{\lesssim}\sqrt{\frac{SAT\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{7}}}+\frac{SA\log^{2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{9/2}}+\frac{1}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\sqrt{T\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}}+\frac{\log T}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\sqrt{SAT},

(231)

Above, (i) is obtained by observing that
V^{\star}-V^{\pi_{t}}\geq 0
and

\displaystyle\left|P_{s_{t},a_{t}}\left(\left(V^{\star}-V^{\pi_{t}}\right)^{\top}\left(V^{\star}+V^{\pi_{t}}\right)\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq P_{s_{t},a_{t}}\left(V^{\star}-V^{\pi_{t}}\right)\left(\left\lVert V^{\star}\right\rVert_{\infty}+\left\lVert V^{\pi_{t}}\right\rVert_{\infty}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{1-\gamma}P_{s_{t},a_{t}}\left(V^{\star}-V^{\pi_{t}}\right),

\displaystyle\left|\left(P_{s_{t},a_{t}}V^{\star}\right)^{2}-\left(P_{s_{t},a_{t}}V^{\pi_{t}}\right)^{2}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left|P_{s_{t},a_{t}}\left(V^{\star}-V^{\pi_{t}}\right)\right|\cdot\left|P_{s_{t},a_{t}}\left(V^{\star}+V^{\pi_{t}}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{1-\gamma}P_{s_{t},a_{t}}\left(V^{\star}-V^{\pi_{t}}\right).

In (ii) of (
231
), we define

\displaystyle\Delta_{t}:=V^{\star}(s_{t+1})-V^{\pi_{t}}(s_{t+1}),\qquad\phi_{t}:=\left(P_{s_{t},a_{t}}-P_{t}\right)\left(V^{\star}-V^{\pi_{t}}\right).

(232)

(iii) in (
231
) results from the following two bounds:

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\Delta_{t}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{SA\log^{2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{7/2}}+\sqrt{\frac{SAT\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{5}}}+\frac{\log T}{1-\gamma}\sqrt{SAT},

(233a)

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\phi_{t}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\sqrt{T\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}}.

(233b)

Now, we show how (
233a
) and (
233b
) are obtained. For (
233a
), we decompose it into two terms as follows:

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\Delta_{t}

\displaystyle=\sum_{t=1}^{T}\big(V^{\star}(s_{t+1})-V^{\pi_{t}}(s_{t+1})\big)=\sum_{t=1}^{T}\big(\underbrace{V^{\star}(s_{t+1})-V^{\pi_{t+1}}(s_{t+1})}_{=:\Delta_{t,1}}+\underbrace{V^{\pi_{t+1}}(s_{t+1})-V^{\pi_{t}}(s_{t+1})}_{=:\Delta_{t,2}}\big).

(234)

Notice
\sum_{t=1}^{T}\Delta_{t,1}
is the regret, so we can bound it with our crude regret bound in Lemma
10
, which gives

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\Delta_{t,1}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{SA\log^{2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{7/2}}+\sqrt{\frac{SAT\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{5}}}.

(235)

On the other hand, notice
\Delta_{t,2}
coincides with
\xi_{t,3}
(cf. (
123
)) in the proof of Lemma
5
. Thus, we can invoke (
154
) and have

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\Delta_{t,2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{t=1}^{T}\big(V^{\pi_{t+1}}(s_{t+1})-V^{\pi_{t}}(s_{t+1})\big)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{SA}{1-\gamma}\log T+\frac{(SA)^{3/4}T^{1/4}\log^{5/4}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{5/4}}+\sqrt{\frac{SA\log^{2}T}{1-\gamma}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\big(V_{t}(s_{t+1})-V^{\pi_{t+1}}(s_{t+1})\big)}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{SA}{1-\gamma}\log T+\frac{(SA)^{3/4}T^{1/4}\log^{5/4}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{5/4}}+\frac{\log T}{1-\gamma}\sqrt{SAT}

\displaystyle\lesssim SA\log^{2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}+\sqrt{\frac{SAT\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{5}}}+\frac{\log T}{1-\gamma}\sqrt{SAT},

(236)

in which the penultimate step is due to
\left\lVert V_{t}-V^{\pi_{t+1}}\right\rVert_{\infty}\leq\frac{1}{1-\gamma}
and the last step is due to
2ab\leq a^{2}+b^{2}
.

For (
233b
), we can directly invoke the Azuma-Hoeffding inequality (Theorem
2
) and obtain that with probability
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\phi_{t}=\sum_{t=1}^{T}\big(P_{s_{t},a_{t}}-P_{t}\big)\big(V^{\star}-V^{\pi_{t}}\big)\lesssim\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\sqrt{T\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}}.

Consequently, substituting (
229
) into (
228
) gives us

\displaystyle\quad\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sqrt{\frac{\Var_{s_{t},a_{t}}(V^{\star})}{N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{SA}\sqrt{\frac{T}{1-\gamma}+\sqrt{\frac{SAT\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{7}}}+\frac{SA\log^{2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{9/2}}+\frac{\log T}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\sqrt{SAT}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{SAT}{1-\gamma}}+\frac{(SA)^{3/4}}{(1-\gamma)^{7/4}}\left(T\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}\right)^{1/4}+\frac{SA}{(1-\gamma)^{9/4}}\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}+\frac{(SA)^{3/4}}{1-\gamma}T^{1/4}\sqrt{\log T}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\frac{SAT}{1-\gamma}}+\left(\frac{SAT}{1-\gamma}\right)^{1/4}\left(\frac{SA}{(1-\gamma)^{3}}\right)^{1/2}\log^{1/4}\frac{SAT}{\delta}+\frac{SA}{(1-\gamma)^{9/4}}\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}+\left(\frac{SAT}{1-\gamma}\right)^{1/4}\left(\frac{SA}{(1-\gamma)^{3/2}}\right)^{1/2}\sqrt{\log T}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{SAT}{1-\gamma}}+\frac{SA}{(1-\gamma)^{3}}\log\frac{SAT}{\delta},

where we have applied the basic inequality
2ab\leq a^{2}+b^{2}
for any
a,b\geq 0
.

I.1.3
Proof of Inequality (
224
)

Let us begin with the following manipulation

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\frac{\sqrt{J_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}}{N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}

\displaystyle=\sum_{(s,a)\in{\mathcal{S}}\times\mathcal{A}}\sum_{n=1}^{N_{T}(s,a)}\frac{\sqrt{J_{t_{n}(s,a)}(s,a)}}{n}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{(s,a)\in{\mathcal{S}}\times\mathcal{A}}\sqrt{J_{N_{T}(s,a)}(s,a)}\log T

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{SA\sum_{(s,a)\in{\mathcal{S}}\times\mathcal{A}}J_{N_{T}(s,a)}(s,a)}\log T.

(237)

Here, the first inequality holds using the harmonic number and the monotonicity of
J_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})
with respect to
t
(cf. (
219
)) due to the monotonicity of
V_{t}^{\mathrm{R}}
, while the second inequality comes from Cauchy-Schwarz.

We can continue by writing out
J_{N_{t}(s,a)}(s,a)
with its definition explicitly:

\displaystyle\sqrt{\sum_{(s,a)\in{\mathcal{S}}\times\mathcal{A}}J_{N_{T}(s,a)}(s,a)}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\sum_{t=1}^{T}\Big(V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t}(s_{t+1})-V^{\star}(s_{t+1})\Big)\ind\Big(V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t}(s_{t+1})-V^{\star}(s_{t+1})>9\Big)}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\sum_{t=1}^{T}\Big(V_{t}(s_{t+1})+6-V_{t}^{\mathrm{LCB}}(s_{t+1})\Big)\ind\Big(V_{t}(s_{t+1})+6-V_{t}^{\mathrm{LCB}}(s_{t+1})>9\Big)}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\sum_{t=1}^{T}\Big(V_{t}(s_{t+1})+6-V_{t}^{\mathrm{LCB}}(s_{t+1})\Big)\ind\Big(V_{t}(s_{t+1})-V_{t}^{\mathrm{LCB}}(s_{t+1})>3\Big)}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\sum_{t=1}^{T}3\Big(V_{t}(s_{t+1})-V_{t}^{\mathrm{LCB}}(s_{t+1})\Big)\ind\Big(V_{t}(s_{t+1})-V_{t}^{\mathrm{LCB}}(s_{t+1})>3\Big)},

(238)

where the first inequality follows from Lemma
3
(cf. (
16
)) and the pessimism of
V^{\mathrm{LCB}}
in Lemma
2
, which implies

V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t}(s_{t+1})-V^{\star}(s_{t+1})\leq V_{t}(s_{t+1})+6-V^{\star}(s_{t+1})\leq V_{t}(s_{t+1})+6-V_{t}^{\mathrm{LCB}}(s_{t+1}),

and the last inequality holds since
3<V_{t}(s_{t+1})-V_{t}^{\mathrm{LCB}}(s_{t+1})
when
\ind\Big(V_{t}(s_{t+1})-V_{t}^{\mathrm{LCB}}(s_{t+1})>3\Big)\neq 0
.

Furthermore, invoking (
3
) from Lemma
3
in (
238
) gives

\displaystyle\quad\ \sqrt{\sum_{(s,a)\in{\mathcal{S}}\times\mathcal{A}}J_{N_{T}(s,a)}(s,a)}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{\left(SAT\right)^{1/4}}{(1-\gamma)^{13/4}}\left(\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}\right)^{5/4}+\frac{SA}{(1-\gamma)^{6}}\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}+\sqrt{\frac{\log^{2}T}{(1-\gamma)^{5}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\big(V_{t-1}(s_{t})-V^{\pi_{t}}(s_{t})\big)}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(SAT\right)^{1/8}\left(\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}\right)^{5/8}\left(\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\right)^{13/8}+\frac{\sqrt{SA\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}}}{(1-\gamma)^{3}}+\frac{T^{1/4}\sqrt{\log T}}{(1-\gamma)^{3/2}}

\displaystyle=T^{1/8}\left(SA\right)^{1/8}\left(\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}\right)^{5/8}\left(\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\right)^{13/8}+\frac{\sqrt{SA\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}}}{(1-\gamma)^{3}}+\frac{T^{1/4}}{\sqrt{\log T}}\frac{\log T}{(1-\gamma)^{3/2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sqrt{T}}{\log T}+\log T+\left(SA\right)^{1/4}\left(\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}\right)^{5/4}\left(\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\right)^{13/4}+\frac{\sqrt{SA\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}}}{(1-\gamma)^{3}}+\frac{\sqrt{T}}{\log T}+\frac{\log^{2}T}{(1-\gamma)^{3}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sqrt{T}}{\log T}+\frac{\sqrt{SA}}{(1-\gamma)^{13/4}}\log^{2}\frac{SAT}{\delta},

where the second inequality is by
V_{t-1}(s_{t})-V^{\pi_{t}}(s_{t})\leq\frac{1}{1-\gamma}
, and the penultimate inequality is by
2ab\leq a^{2}+b^{2}
.

Finally, substitution into (
237
) gives

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\frac{\sqrt{J_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}}{N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(\sqrt{SA}\log T\right)\left(\frac{\sqrt{T}}{\log T}+\frac{\sqrt{SA}}{(1-\gamma)^{13/4}}\log^{2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{SAT}+\frac{SA}{(1-\gamma)^{13/4}}\log^{3}\frac{SAT}{\delta},

which concludes this proof.

I.2
Bounding
\mathcal{R}_{2}

In this section, let us again adopt the shorthand notation
t_{i}:=t_{i}(s_{t},a_{t})
. Also define

\lambda_{t}:=\sum_{N=N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}^{N_{T-1}(s_{t},a_{t})}\eta_{N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}^{N}.

(239)

We can decompose
\mathcal{R}_{2}
in (
211
) as follows:

\mathcal{R}_{2}:=\underbrace{\gamma\sum_{t=1}^{T}\lambda_{t}\left(P_{t}-P_{s_{t},a_{t}}\right)\left(V^{*}-V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t}\right)}_{\eqqcolon\mathcal{R}_{2,1}}+\underbrace{\gamma\sum_{t=1}^{T}\lambda_{t}\frac{\sum_{i=1}^{N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}\left(V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{i}}(s_{t_{i}+1})-P_{s_{t},a_{t}}V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t}\right)}{N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}}_{\eqqcolon\mathcal{R}_{2,2}}.

Note that
\lambda_{t}\leq 1+\frac{1-\gamma}{2}
because of the property
\sum_{N=n}^{\infty}\eta_{n}^{N}\leq 1+\frac{1-\gamma}{2}
in (
34
) of Lemma
6
. In the following, we shall control these two terms separately.

Step 1: bounding
\mathcal{R}_{2,1}
.

We begin by constructing the necessary notation and verify the conditions for Lemma
8
. Let

\displaystyle W_{i}\coloneqq V^{\mathrm{R}}_{i}-V^{\star}\qquad\text{and}\qquad u_{i}(s_{i},a_{i})\coloneqq\lambda_{i},

(240)

where
\lambda_{i}
is as defined in (
239
). Clearly,
W_{i}
and
u_{i}(s_{i},a_{i})
are deterministic at the end of the
(i-1)
-th step, given
N(s_{i},a_{i})
. Moreover, define

\displaystyle\left|u_{i}(s_{i},a_{i})\right|\leq 1+\frac{1-\gamma}{2}\eqqcolon C_{\mathrm{u}}\qquad\text{and}\qquad\left\lVert W_{i}\right\rVert_{\infty}\leq\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\eqqcolon C_{\mathrm{w}}.

(241)

Hence, we can apply Lemma
8
with (
240
) and (
241
) and conclude that with probability at least
1-\delta/2
,

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{2,1}

\displaystyle\leq\gamma\left|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\lambda_{t}\left(P_{t}-P_{s_{t},a_{t}}\right)\left(V^{*}-V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\gamma\sqrt{C_{\mathrm{u}}^{2}C_{\mathrm{w}}SA\sum_{i=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}_{i-1}\left[P_{i}W_{i}\right]\log\frac{T}{\delta}}+C_{\mathrm{u}}C_{\mathrm{w}}SA\log\frac{T}{\delta}

\displaystyle\lesssim\gamma\sqrt{\frac{SA}{1-\gamma}\sum_{i=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}_{i-1}\left[P_{i}\big(V^{\mathrm{R}}_{i}-V^{\star}\big)\right]\log\frac{T}{\delta}}+\frac{SA}{1-\gamma}\log\frac{T}{\delta}

\displaystyle=\gamma\sqrt{\frac{SA}{1-\gamma}\bigg\{\sum_{t=1}^{T}P_{s_{t},a_{t}}\big(V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t}-V^{\star}\big)\bigg\}\log\frac{T}{\delta}}+\frac{SA}{1-\gamma}\log\frac{T}{\delta}.

(242)

To finish the upper bound on
\mathcal{R}_{2,1}
, it remains to control

\sum_{t=1}^{T}P_{s_{t},a_{t}}\left(V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t}-V^{\star}\right)=\sum_{t=1}^{T}P_{t}\left(V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t}-V^{\star}\right)+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(P_{s_{t},a_{t}}-P_{t}\right)\left(V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t}-V^{\star}\right),

(243)

which can be further decomposed into two terms. Let us first examine the first term:

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}P_{t}\big(V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t}-V^{\star}\big)

\displaystyle\overset{\mathrm{(i)}}{\leq}\sum_{t=1}^{T}P_{t}\big(V_{t}+9-V^{\star}\big)

\displaystyle\leq 9T+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\big(V_{t}(s_{t+1})-V^{\star}(s_{t+1})\big)

\displaystyle\overset{\mathrm{(ii)}}{\lesssim}T+\frac{SA\log^{2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{7/2}}+\sqrt{\frac{SAT\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{5}}}

(244)

with probability at least
1-{\delta}/{4}
. Above, (i) holds according to (
16
), and (ii) is valid since

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\big(V_{t}(s_{t+1})-V^{\star}(s_{t+1})\big)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{t=1}^{T}\big(V_{t}(s_{t+1})-V^{\pi_{t+1}}(s_{t+1})\big)\lesssim\frac{SA\log^{2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{7/2}}+\sqrt{\frac{SAT\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{5}}},

where the first inequality follows since
V^{\star}\geq V^{\pi_{t+1}}
, and the second inequality comes from (
235
) in Lemma
10
.

Additionally, for the second term in (
243
), the Azuma-Hoeffding inequality (Theorem
2
) directly leads to

\displaystyle\left|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(P_{t}-P_{s_{t},a_{t}}\right)\left(V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t}-V^{\star}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{T}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\log\frac{1}{\delta}}

with probability at least
1-\delta/4
.

Together, we have that

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}P_{s_{t},a_{t}}\left(V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t}-V^{\star}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{t=1}^{T}P_{t}\left(V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t}-V^{\star}\right)+\left|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(P_{t}-P_{s_{t},a_{t}}\right)\left(V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t}-V^{\star}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim T+\frac{SA\log^{2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{7/2}}+\sqrt{\frac{SAT\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{5}}}

(245)

with probability at least
1-\delta/2
.

Finally, substituting this inequality into (
242
) allows us to conclude with

\displaystyle\quad\ \gamma\left|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\lambda_{t}\left(P_{t}-P_{s_{t},a_{t}}\right)\left(V^{*}-V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\gamma\sqrt{\frac{SA}{1-\gamma}\bigg\{\sum_{t=1}^{T}P_{s_{t},a_{t}}\big(V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t}-V^{\star}\big)\bigg\}\log\frac{T}{\delta}}+\frac{SA}{1-\gamma}\log\frac{T}{\delta}

\displaystyle\lesssim\gamma\sqrt{\frac{SA}{1-\gamma}\left(T+\frac{SA\log^{2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{7/2}}+\sqrt{\frac{SAT\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{5}}}\right)\log\frac{T}{\delta}}+\frac{SA}{1-\gamma}\log\frac{T}{\delta}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{SA}{1-\gamma}\left(T+\frac{SA\log^{2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{7/2}}+\frac{SA}{(1-\gamma)^{5}}\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}\right)\log\frac{T}{\delta}}+\frac{SA}{1-\gamma}\log\frac{T}{\delta}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{SAT}{1-\gamma}\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}}+\frac{SA}{(1-\gamma)^{3}}\log^{3/2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}

(246)

with probability at least
1-\delta
. Above, the penultimate line holds due to
2ab\leq a^{2}+b^{2}
.

Step 2: bounding
\mathcal{R}_{2,2}
.

We can start by decomposing
\mathcal{R}_{2,2}
into three terms as follows:

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{2,2}

\displaystyle\leq\gamma\sum_{t=1}^{T}\frac{\lambda_{t}}{N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}\sum_{i=1}^{N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}\left(V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t_{i}}(s_{t_{i}+1})-P_{s_{t},a_{t}}V^{\mathrm{R}}_{T}\right)

\displaystyle=\gamma\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{n=N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}^{N_{T}(s_{t},a_{t})}\frac{\lambda_{t}}{n}\left(V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t}(s_{t+1})-V^{\mathrm{R}}_{T}(s_{t+1})+\left(P_{t}-P_{s_{t},a_{t}}\right)V^{\mathrm{R}}_{T}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\left(1+\frac{1-\gamma}{2}\right)\log T\sum_{t=1}^{T}\big(V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t}(s_{t+1})-V^{\mathrm{R}}_{T}(s_{t+1})\big)+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{n=N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}^{N_{T}(s_{t},a_{t})}\frac{\lambda_{t}}{n}\big(P_{t}-P_{s_{t},a_{t}}\big)V^{\star}

\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{n=N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}^{N_{T}(s_{t},a_{t})}\frac{\lambda_{t}}{n}\big(P_{t}-P_{s_{t},a_{t}}\big)\big(V^{\mathrm{R}}_{T}-V^{\star}\big),

(247)

in which the first inequality is due to the monotonicity
V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t}\geq V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t+1}\geq\cdots\geq V^{\mathrm{R}}_{T}
, and the last inequality is due to the facts that
\sum_{n=N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}^{N_{T}(s_{t},a_{t})}\frac{1}{n}\leq\log T
(harmonic number) and
\lambda_{t}\leq 1+\frac{1-\gamma}{2}
(cf. (
239
)). To proceed, we shall control the three terms in (
247
) separately.

The first term in (
247
) can be controlled with Lemma
3
(cf. (
3
)) as follows:

\displaystyle\quad\ \sum_{t=1}^{T}\big(V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t}(s_{t+1})-V^{\mathrm{R}}_{T}(s_{t+1})\big)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\left(SA\right)^{3/4}T^{1/4}}{(1-\gamma)^{13/4}}\left(\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}\right)^{5/4}+\frac{SA}{(1-\gamma)^{6}}\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}+\sqrt{\frac{SA\log^{2}T}{(1-\gamma)^{5}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\big(V_{t-1}(s_{t})-V^{\pi_{t}}(s_{t})\big)}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{SAT}{1-\gamma}\log^{2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}+\frac{SA}{(1-\gamma)^{6}}\log^{3/2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}+\sqrt{\frac{SA\log^{2}T}{(1-\gamma)^{5}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\big(V_{t-1}(s_{t})-V^{\pi_{t}}(s_{t})\big)}

(248)

with probability at least
1-{\delta}/{3}
. Above, the last inequality is by
2ab\leq a^{2}+b^{2}
.

For the second term in (
247
), let us first construct the necessary notation and verify the conditions for Lemma
8
. We can set

\displaystyle W_{i}\coloneqq V^{\star},\qquad\text{and}\qquad u_{i}(s_{i},a_{i})\coloneqq\sum_{n=N_{i}(s_{i},a_{i})}^{N_{T}(s_{i},a_{i})}\frac{\lambda_{i}}{n}.

(249)

Clearly,
W_{i}
and
u_{i}(s_{i},a_{i})
are deterministic at the end of the
(i-1)
-th step, given
N_{T}(s_{i},a_{i})
. Furthermore, let

\displaystyle\left|u_{i}(s_{i},a_{i})\right|\leq\left(1+\frac{1-\gamma}{2}\right)\sum_{n=N_{i}(s_{i},a_{i})}^{N_{T}(s_{i},a_{i})}\frac{1}{n}\leq\left(1+\frac{1-\gamma}{2}\right)\log T\eqqcolon C_{\mathrm{u}}

(250)

due to the facts that
\sum_{n=N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}^{N_{T}(s_{t},a_{t})}\frac{1}{n}\leq\log T
(harmonic number) and
\lambda_{t}\leq 1+\frac{1-\gamma}{2}
(cf. (
239
)), and

\displaystyle\left\lVert W_{i}\right\rVert_{\infty}\leq\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\eqqcolon C_{\mathrm{w}}.

(251)

Hence, we can apply Lemma
8
with (
249
), (
250
) and (
251
) and conclude that with probability at least
1-{\delta}/{3}
,

\displaystyle\quad\ \left|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{n=N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}^{N_{T}(s_{t},a_{t})}\frac{\lambda_{t}}{n}\left(P_{t}-P_{s_{t},a_{t}}\right)V^{\star}\right|=\left|\sum_{t=1}^{T}X_{t}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{C_{\mathrm{u}}^{2}SA\sum_{i=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}_{i-1}\left[\left|(P_{i}-P_{s_{i},a_{i}})W_{i}\right|^{2}\right]\log\frac{T}{\delta}}+C_{\mathrm{u}}C_{\mathrm{w}}SA\log\frac{T}{\delta}

\displaystyle\overset{\mathrm{(i)}}{\asymp}\sqrt{\sum_{t=1}^{T}\Var_{s_{t},a_{t}}(V^{\star})\cdot SA\log^{3}\frac{T}{\delta}}+\frac{SA}{1-\gamma}\log^{2}\frac{T}{\delta}

\displaystyle\overset{\mathrm{(ii)}}{\lesssim}\sqrt{SA\left(\frac{T}{1-\gamma}+\sqrt{\frac{SAT\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{7}}}+\frac{SA\log^{2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{9/2}}\right)\log^{3}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}+\frac{SA}{1-\gamma}\log^{2}\frac{T}{\delta}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{SAT}{1-\gamma}\log^{3}\frac{SAT}{\delta}+\sqrt{\frac{SAT}{(1-\gamma)^{7}}}\log^{7/2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}+\frac{SA}{(1-\gamma)^{9/4}}\log^{5/2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}

\displaystyle\overset{\mathrm{(iii)}}{\lesssim}\sqrt{\frac{SAT}{1-\gamma}\log^{3}\frac{SAT}{\delta}+\frac{\log^{4}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{6}}}+\frac{SA}{(1-\gamma)^{9/4}}\log^{5/2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{SAT}{1-\gamma}\log^{3}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}+\frac{SA}{(1-\gamma)^{3}}\log^{2}\frac{SAT}{\delta},

(252)

in which (i) comes from the definition of variance in (
28
); (ii) holds due to (
229
); (iii) is valid since

\sqrt{\frac{SAT}{(1-\gamma)^{7}}}\log^{7/2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}=\sqrt{\frac{SAT}{1-\gamma}}\log^{3/2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}\cdot\frac{\log^{2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{3}}\lesssim\frac{SAT}{1-\gamma}\log^{3}\frac{SAT}{\delta}+\frac{\log^{4}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{6}}

due to
2ab\leq a^{2}+b^{2}
(by Cauchy-Schwarz).

Finally, for the third term in (
247
), in order to get a tight bound in the presence of the dependency between
P_{t}
and
V_{T}^{\mathrm{R}}
, we use the standard epsilon-net argument (see, e.g.,
Tao, 2012
), whose details are deferred to Appendix
I.2.1
. The final bound on this term is

\displaystyle\left|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{n=N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}^{N_{T}(s_{t},a_{t})}\frac{\lambda_{t}}{n}\big(P_{t}-P_{s_{t},a_{t}}\big)\big(V^{\mathrm{R}}_{T}-V^{\star}\big)\right|\lesssim\frac{SA}{(1-\gamma)^{3}}\log^{5/2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}+\sqrt{\frac{SAT}{1-\gamma}\log^{3}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}.

(253)

Bringing (
248
), (
252
) and (
253
) into (
247
), with a union bound argument, we can demonstrate

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{2,2}
