\displaystyle\leq C_{2,2}\Bigg\{\sqrt{\frac{SAT}{1-\gamma}\log^{3}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}+\frac{SA}{(1-\gamma)^{6}}\log^{5/2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}+\sqrt{\frac{\log^{2}T}{(1-\gamma)^{5}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\big(V_{t-1}(s_{t})-V^{\pi_{t}}(s_{t})\big)}\Bigg\}

(254)

with probability at least
1-\delta
, where
C_{3,2}>0
is some constant.

Step 3: combining the preceding bounds.

Taking (
246
) and (
254
) together, we immediately arrive at

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{2}\leq\left|\mathcal{R}_{2,1}\right|+\mathcal{R}_{2,2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{\mathrm{r},2}\left\{\sqrt{\frac{SAT}{1-\gamma}\log^{3}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}+\frac{SA}{(1-\gamma)^{7}}\log^{5/2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}+\sqrt{\frac{SA\log^{2}T}{(1-\gamma)^{5}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\big(V_{t-1}(s_{t})-V^{\pi_{t}}(s_{t})\big)}\right\}

(255)

with probability at least
1-2\delta
, where
C_{\mathrm{r},2}>0
is some constant.
This immediately concludes the proof.

I.2.1
Proof of (
253
): An Epsilon-Net Argument

Step 1: concentration on a fixed vector.

Let us establish a concentration bound over a fixed vector, the choice of which we will later specify when we introduce our epsilon net. Consider a fixed vector
V^{\mathrm{d}}\in\mathbb{R}^{S}
satisfying:

\displaystyle V^{\star}\leq V^{\mathrm{d}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{1-\gamma}.

(256)

We intend to derive a bound on the following quantity as in (
253
):

\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{n=N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}^{N_{T}(s_{t},a_{t})}\frac{\lambda_{t}}{n}\big(P_{t}-P_{s_{t},a_{t}}\big)\big(V^{\mathrm{d}}-V^{\star}\big).

Let us first construct the necessary notation and verify the conditions for Lemma
8
. We can set

\displaystyle W_{i}\coloneqq V^{\mathrm{d}}-V^{\star}\qquad\text{and}\qquad u_{i}(s_{i},a_{i})\coloneqq\sum_{n=N_{i}(s_{i},a_{i})}^{N_{T}(s_{i},a_{i})}\frac{\lambda^{i}_{h}}{n}.

(257)

Clearly,
W_{i}
and
u_{i}(s_{i},a_{i})
are deterministic at the end of the
(i-1)
-th step, given
N_{T}(s_{i},a_{i})
. Furthermore, let

\displaystyle\left|u_{i}(s_{i},a_{i})\right|\leq\left(1+\frac{1-\gamma}{2}\right)\sum_{n=N_{i}(s_{i},a_{i})}^{N_{T}(s_{i},a_{i})}\frac{1}{n}

\displaystyle\leq\left(1+\frac{1-\gamma}{2}\right)\log T\eqqcolon C_{\mathrm{u}}

(258)

due to the facts that
\sum_{n=N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}^{N_{T}(s_{t},a_{t})}\frac{1}{n}\leq\log T
(harmonic number) and
\lambda_{t}\leq 1+\frac{1-\gamma}{2}
(cf. (
239
)), and

\displaystyle\left\lVert W_{i}\right\rVert_{\infty}\leq\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\eqqcolon C_{\mathrm{w}}.

(259)

Hence, we can apply Lemma
8
with (
257
), (
258
) and (
259
) and conclude that with probability at least
1-\delta_{0}
,

\displaystyle\quad\ \left|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{n=N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}^{N_{T}(s_{t},a_{t})}\frac{\lambda_{t}}{n}\left(P_{t}-P_{s_{t},a_{t}}\right)\left(V^{\mathrm{d}}-V^{\star}\right)\right|=\left|\sum_{t=1}^{T}X_{t}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{C_{\mathrm{u}}^{2}C_{\mathrm{w}}\left\{\sum_{i=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}_{i-1}\left[P_{i}W_{i}\right]\right\}\log\frac{T^{SA}}{\delta_{0}}}+C_{\mathrm{u}}C_{\mathrm{w}}\log\frac{T^{SA}}{\delta_{0}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{\log^{2}T}{1-\gamma}\left\{\sum_{t=1}^{T}P_{s_{t},a_{t}}\left(V^{\mathrm{d}}-V^{\star}\right)\right\}\log\frac{T^{SA}}{\delta_{0}}}+\frac{\log T}{1-\gamma}\log\frac{T^{SA}}{\delta_{0}}.

(260)

The choice of
\delta_{0}
will be specified momentarily.

Step 2: constructing an epsilon net.

In this step, we construct a properly-sized epsilon net that can cover the space of
V^{\mathrm{R}}_{T}
. Specifically, we will construct an epsilon net
\mathcal{N}_{\alpha}
(the parameter
\alpha
will be specified shortly) such that:

•

For any
V\in[0,\frac{1}{1-\gamma}]^{S}
, one can find a point
V^{\mathrm{net}}\in\mathcal{N}_{\alpha}
such that

0\leq V(s)-V^{\mathrm{net}}(s)\leq\alpha\qquad\text{for all }s\in{\mathcal{S}};

•

The cardinality of
\mathcal{N}_{\alpha}
satisfies the following bound:

\left|\mathcal{N}_{\alpha}\right|\leq\left(\frac{1}{(1-\gamma)\alpha}\right)^{S}.

(261)

Clearly, such epsilon net exists by letting it be all intersection points in a grid on
[0,\frac{1}{1-\gamma}]^{S}
with interval length
\alpha
. Now, we extend the concentration bound (
260
) in Step 1 to all members of this epsilon net
\mathcal{N}_{\alpha}
. Set
\delta_{0}=\frac{\delta}{6}/\left(\frac{1}{(1-\gamma)\alpha}\right)^{S}
. Taking a union bound over all vectors in
\mathcal{N}_{\alpha}
, (
260
) gives that with probability at least
1-\delta_{0}\left(\frac{1}{(1-\gamma)\alpha}\right)^{S}=1-\delta/6
,

\displaystyle\quad\ \left|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{n=N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}^{N_{T}(s_{t},a_{t})}\frac{\lambda_{t}}{n}\left(P_{t}-P_{s_{t},a_{t}}\right)\left(V^{\mathrm{net}}-V^{\star}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{\log^{2}T}{1-\gamma}\sum_{t=1}^{T}P_{s_{t},a_{t}}\left(V^{\mathrm{net}}-V^{\star}\right)\log\frac{T^{SA}}{\delta_{0}}}+\frac{\log T}{1-\gamma}\log\frac{T^{SA}}{\delta_{0}}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\frac{SA\log^{2}T}{1-\gamma}\sum_{t=1}^{T}P_{s_{t},a_{t}}\left(V^{\mathrm{net}}-V^{\star}\right)\log\frac{T}{(1-\gamma)^{S}\alpha^{S}\delta}}+\frac{SA\log T}{1-\gamma}\log\frac{T}{(1-\gamma)^{S}\alpha^{S}\delta}

(262)

simultaneously for all elements
V^{\mathrm{net}}\in\mathcal{N}_{\alpha}
.

Step 3: obtaining a uniform bound.

Now, we are ready to establish a uniform bound over the entire space of
V^{\mathrm{R}}_{T}
, which is just all optimistic vectors in
[0,\frac{1}{1-\gamma}]^{S}
. Consider an arbitrary vector

V^{\mathrm{u}}\in\mathbb{R}^{S}
satisfying
V^{\star}\leq V^{\mathrm{u}}\leq\frac{1}{1-\gamma}
. By the construction of
\mathcal{N}_{\alpha}
, one can find a point
V^{\mathrm{net}}\in\mathcal{N}_{\alpha}

such that

\displaystyle 0\leq V^{\mathrm{u}}(s)-V^{\mathrm{net}}(s)\leq\alpha\qquad\text{for all }s\in{\mathcal{S}}.

(263)

Setting
\alpha=\frac{1}{(1-\gamma)T^{1/S}}
, we can deduce that

\displaystyle\quad\ \left|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{n=N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}^{N_{T}(s_{t},a_{t})}\frac{\lambda_{t}}{n}\left(P_{t}-P_{s_{t},a_{t}}\right)\left(V^{\mathrm{u}}-V^{\star}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{n=N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}^{N_{T}(s_{t},a_{t})}\frac{\lambda_{t}}{n}\left(P_{t}-P_{s_{t},a_{t}}\right)\left(V^{\mathrm{net}}-V^{\star}\right)\right|+\left|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{n=N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}^{N_{T}(s_{t},a_{t})}\frac{\lambda_{t}}{n}\left(P_{t}-P_{s_{t},a_{t}}\right)\left(V^{\mathrm{u}}-V^{\mathrm{net}}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\overset{(\mathrm{i})}{\lesssim}\left|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{n=N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}^{N_{T}(s_{t},a_{t})}\frac{\lambda_{t}}{n}\left(P_{t}-P_{s_{t},a_{t}}\right)\left(V^{\mathrm{net}}-V^{\star}\right)\right|+T\alpha\log T

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{SA\log^{2}T}{1-\gamma}\sum_{t=1}^{T}P_{s_{i},a_{i}}\left(V^{\mathrm{net}}-V^{\star}\right)\log\frac{T}{(1-\gamma)^{S}\alpha^{S}\delta}}+\frac{SA\log T}{1-\gamma}\log\frac{T}{(1-\gamma)^{S}\alpha^{S}\delta}+T\alpha\log T

\displaystyle\overset{(\mathrm{ii})}{\asymp}\sqrt{\frac{SA\log^{2}T}{1-\gamma}\sum_{t=1}^{T}P_{s_{i},a_{i}}\left(V^{\mathrm{net}}-V^{\star}\right)\log\frac{T}{\delta}}+\frac{SA\log T}{1-\gamma}\log\frac{T}{\delta},

(264)

in which (i) is due to

\displaystyle\quad\ \left|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{n=N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}^{N_{T}(s_{t},a_{t})}\frac{\lambda_{t}}{n}\left(P_{t}-P_{s_{t},a_{t}}\right)\left(V^{\mathrm{u}}-V^{\mathrm{net}}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{n=N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}^{N_{T}(s_{t},a_{t})}\frac{\lambda_{t}}{n}\left(\left\lVert P_{t}\right\rVert_{1}+\left\lVert P_{s_{t},a_{t}}\right\rVert_{1}\right)\left\lVert V^{\mathrm{u}}-V^{\mathrm{net}}\right\rVert_{\infty}\right|

\displaystyle\leq 2\left(1+\frac{1-\gamma}{2}\right)T\alpha\log T

(265)

because
\sum_{n=N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}^{N_{T}(s_{t},a_{t})}\frac{1}{n}\leq\log T
(harmonic number) and
\lambda_{t}\leq 1+\frac{1-\gamma}{2}
(cf. (
239
)).

(ii) holds due to the condition (
263
) and our choice of
\alpha
.

In summary with probability at least
1-\delta/6
, (
264
) holds simultaneously for all
V^{\mathrm{u}}\in\mathbb{R}^{S}
obeying
V^{\star}\leq V^{\mathrm{u}}\leq\frac{1}{1-\gamma}
.

Step 4: controlling the original quantity of interest.

With the uniform bound over the set of all optimistic vectors in
\mathbb{R}^{S}
from Step 3, we can finally control the original quantity of interest

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{n=N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}^{N_{T}(s_{t},a_{t})}\frac{\lambda_{t}}{n}\big(P_{t}-P_{s_{t},a_{t}}\big)\big(V^{\mathrm{R}}_{T}-V^{\star}\big).

(266)

Substitution into (
264
) with
V^{\mathrm{R}}_{T}
as
V^{\mathrm{u}}
yields

\displaystyle\quad\ \left|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{n=N_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})}^{N_{T}(s_{t},a_{t})}\frac{\lambda_{t}}{n}\big(P_{t}-P_{s_{t},a_{t}}\big)\big(V^{\mathrm{R}}_{T}-V^{\star}\big)\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{SA\log^{2}T}{1-\gamma}\sum_{t=1}^{T}P_{s_{i},a_{i}}\left(V^{\mathrm{R}}_{T}-V^{\star}\right)\log\frac{T}{\delta}}+\frac{SA}{1-\gamma}\log^{2}\frac{T}{\delta}

\displaystyle\overset{(\mathrm{i})}{\lesssim}\sqrt{\frac{SA\log^{2}T}{1-\gamma}\left\{T+\frac{SA\log^{2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{7/2}}+\sqrt{\frac{SAT\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{5}}}\right\}\log\frac{T}{\delta}}+\frac{SA}{1-\gamma}\log^{2}\frac{T}{\delta}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{SA\log^{2}T}{1-\gamma}\left\{\sqrt{\frac{SAT}{(1-\gamma)^{5}}\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}}+T\right\}\log\frac{T}{\delta}}+\frac{SA}{(1-\gamma)^{9/4}}\log^{5/2}\frac{T}{\delta}

\displaystyle\overset{(\mathrm{ii})}{\lesssim}\sqrt{\frac{SA}{1-\gamma}\left\{\frac{SA}{(1-\gamma)^{5}}\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}+T\right\}\log^{3}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}+\frac{SA}{(1-\gamma)^{9/4}}\log^{5/2}\frac{T}{\delta}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{SA}{(1-\gamma)^{3}}\log^{5/2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}+\sqrt{\frac{SAT}{1-\gamma}\log^{3}\frac{SAT}{\delta}},

(267)

in which (i) is obtained because with probability exceeding
1-\delta/6
,

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}P_{s_{t},a_{t}}\big(V^{\mathrm{R}}_{T}-V^{\star}\big)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{t=1}^{T}P_{s_{t},a_{t}}\big(V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t}-V^{\star}\big)\leq T+\frac{SA\log^{2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{7/2}}+\sqrt{\frac{SAT\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{5}}},

(268)

where the first inequality holds because
V_{t}
is non-increasing in
t
and
V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t}
can only be updated from
V_{t}
(thus
V^{\mathrm{R}}_{t}
is monotonically non-increasing), and the second inequality follows from (
245
), which we are allowed to use because Lemma
1
suggests

\displaystyle V^{\star}(s)\leq V^{\mathrm{R}}_{T}(s)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\quad\text{for any }s\in{\mathcal{S}}.

(269)

(ii) holds since

\sqrt{\frac{SAT}{(1-\gamma)^{5}}\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}}=\sqrt{\frac{SA}{(1-\gamma)^{5}}\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}}\sqrt{T}\lesssim\frac{SA}{(1-\gamma)^{5}}\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}+T.
